# Kaishi 1.5k Mnemonics — Words 1–100

---

## 1. 私 (わたし / watashi) — I (polite, general)

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: watashi)**
"WA-TA-SHI — I want a WAFFLE and TAHI (Thai tea)! That's what **I** want!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 私)**
Look at 私: the left side is 禾 (grain/rice plant), the right side is 厶 (private/self). Think of a rice farmer saying "This rice is for **ME** — I am私 (watashi)!" The禾 looks like a little rice stalk bending toward you, pointing at yourself: "**I** (watashi) am the rice farmer!"

---

## 2. あなた (あなた / anata) — you

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: anata)**
"ANATA sounds like "a NAUGHTY" — "YOU are a naughty person!" Point at someone: "ANATA! You did it!"

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (あなた)**
The hiragana あ looks like a person with arms wide open pointing at YOU. な has a cross shape like pointing a finger. た has a line like a finger jab. "ANATA — I'm pointing at YOU!"

---

## 3. さん (さん / san) — san (honorific)

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: san)**
"SAN sounds like SON. Everyone's SON deserves the respect of さん! Add it after names like a polite badge."

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (さん)**
The hiragana さ looks like a little person bowing respectfully. ん is a humble nod. Together: a bowing figure saying "I add SAN to your name out of respect!"

---

## 4. 彼 (かれ / kare) — he, him

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: kare)**
"CARE — I CARE about him. HE is someone I care about." Or: "KARE sounds like CAR-eh — he drives a car."

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 彼)**
彼 has 彳 (step/stay) on the left and 皮 (skin) on the right. Think: "That HE (kare) stays (彳) with his skin (皮) — he's the guy over there, the one standing still with his skin showing!"

---

## 5. 好き (すき / suki) — fond of, liked

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: suki)**
"SUKI sounds like SKI — I LOVE skiing! I'm fond of skiing!" Or: "SOUKI — a SOUKI (socky) person I LIKE."

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 好)**
好 is made of 女 (woman) + 子 (child). A mother loves her child — that's 好き (suki)! The woman (女) embraces the child (子) = LIKED, FOND OF.

---

## 6. 人 (ひと / hito) — person

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: hito)**
"HE-TOE — a PERSON with a weird HEEL-TOE walk." Or: "HI-TO — a tiny HI (greeting) from a person."

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 人)**
人 looks like two legs walking — that's a PERSON! Just two strokes: one leg going left, one going right. A PERSON walking. 人 = HITO = person.

---

## 7. いい (いい / ii) — good

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: ii)**
"EE-EE — like a monkey going EE-EE when something GOOD happens!" 

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (いい)**
The hiragana い is written twice: いい. Two identical strokes going down — double good! Like giving two thumbs up: "That's ii (good) AND ii (good) = very GOOD!"

---

## 8. 人 (じん / jin) — person from (nationality)

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: jin)**
"JEAN — a JEAN-wearing person from a country." Or: "GIN — a person from GINza."

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 人)**
Same kanji as 人 (person), but read as JIN when talking about nationality. Think: "Nihon-JIN (Japanese person)" — the same two-legged person shape, but now they carry a passport with JIN stamped on it.

---

## 9. 日本語 (にほんご / nihongo) — Japanese language

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: nihongo)**
"NI-HON-GO: NI (knee) + HON (book) + GO (go). I KNEE my BOOK and GO study Japanese!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 日本語)**
日 = sun/day (a rectangle with a line — the sun). 本 = book/origin (a tree 木 with a line at the bottom — the ROOT/ORIGIN). 語 = language (words 言 + five 五 + mouth 口). "The SUN (日) country's ORIGINAL (本) WORDS (語) = JAPANESE LANGUAGE!"

---

## 10. 勉強 (べんきょう / benkyou) — study

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: benkyou)**
"BEN-KYOU: BEN (like a guy named Ben) + KYOU (cute). Ben studies hard because he thinks studying is KYOU (cute)!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 勉強)**
勉 = exert/effort (has 力 power/strength inside). 強 = strong (has 弓 bow). "Study takes EFFORT (勉) and makes you STRONG (強)!" Think: Ben uses his STRENGTH to STUDY.

---

## 11. 本 (ほん / hon) — book

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: hon)**
"HON — like HONK! A book goes HONK when you slam it shut!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 本)**
本 is a tree (木) with a line at the base — that's the ROOT/ORIGIN. Books are the ROOT of knowledge! Or: the line marks the BASE of the tree = where books (本) are kept, at the base of the bookshelf.

---

## 12. これ (これ / kore) — this

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: kore)**
"KO-RE — like "CALL RE (Ray)!" This person right here, CALL RE, this is THIS!"

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (これ)**
これ in hiragana: こ looks like two horizontal lines (here), れ looks like a person with a hand reaching out. "This (kore) thing right HERE that I'm REaching for!"

---

## 13. 何 (なに・なん / nani/nan) — what

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: nani)**
"NANI?! That's Japanese for WHAT?! You already know this one from anime — NANI?!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 何)**
何 has 亻(person) on the left and 可 (possible) on the right. A PERSON (亻) asks "WHAT is POSSIBLE (可)?" = 何 (nani) = WHAT?

---

## 14. それ (それ / sore) — that

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: sore)**
"SORE — like a SORE thumb. THAT thing over there is a SORE spot!"

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (それ)**
それ: そ looks like someone gesturing FAR away, れ is reaching. "SORE — that thing OVER THERE, not here, THAT!"

---

## 15. あれ (あれ / are) — that over there

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: are)**
"ARE — "ARE you looking at THAT over there?!" Pointing very far away: "ARE (あれ)!"

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (あれ)**
あ looks like someone with arms wide, れ reaching far. ARE you seeing THAT very far away thing? Further than それ (sore)!

---

## 16. どれ (どれ / dore) — which, what

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: dore)**
"DORA — like DORA the Explorer! She always asks "WHICH one? WHICH way?" DORE!"

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (どれ)**
どれ starts with ど (do-question marker). When you ask WHICH, you need a question: DORE = WHICH? Think DORAemon asking "WHICH gadget should I use?"

---

## 17. 毎日 (まいにち / mainichi) — every day

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: mainichi)**
"MY NICHE — Every day I work in MY NICHE!" Or: "MAI (my) + NICHI (day) = MY DAY, every single day!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 毎日)**
毎 = every (looks like a mother 母 with extra strokes — a mother does things EVERY day). 日 = day (sun/box). "EVERY (毎) DAY (日) = EVERY DAY!"

---

## 18. 兄 (あに / ani) — older brother

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: ani)**
"ANI sounds like ONION — my older brother smells like ONION!" Or: "AH-NEE! That's my older bro — AH-NEE!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 兄)**
兄 has 口 (mouth) on top and 儿 (legs) on bottom. Think: a BIG MOUTH with LEGS = your older BROTHER who talks a lot and runs around! 口 (mouth) + 儿 (legs) = big bro (兄 = ani).

---

## 19. いる (いる / iru) — to have, to exist (animate)

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: iru)**
"EEL — an EEL is a living creature that EXISTS! Iru = to exist (for animals and people)."

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (いる)**
いる is hiragana: い (two strokes, like a person) + る (a loop, like something alive curling up). A living being (い) curling up (る) = EXISTS, is there.

---

## 20. ある (ある / aru) — to have, to exist (inanimate)

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: aru)**
"A ROOM — a ROOM exists! Things exist in a ROOM. ARU!"

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (ある)**
ある: あ (open mouth, like a room) + る (loop). An open space (あ) with something in it (る) = an OBJECT EXISTS there. Inanimate things ARU (exist).

---

## 21. あまり (あまり / amari) — (not) very, (not) much

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: amari)**
"AMARI sounds like AMARETTO — I don't drink AMARETTO very much. AMARI = not much!"

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (あまり)**
あまり: あ (ah!) + ま (waiting) + り (two/reason). "AH, I'm WAITING but not MUCH is happening." AMARI = not very much.

---

## 22. 今 (いま / ima) — now

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: ima)**
"IMA — like "I'M A" — "I'M A doing it RIGHT NOW!" IMA = NOW!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 今)**
今 has 人 (person) on top and a horizontal stroke below. A person standing on a line = RIGHT HERE, RIGHT NOW. The person (人) is grounded on the spot (一) = NOW (ima).

---

## 23. 時間 (じかん / jikan) — time, hour

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: jikan)**
"JI-KAN — like "GEE, CAN you tell me the TIME?" JI = clock time, KAN = interval. TIME!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 時間)**
時 = time (sun 日 + temple 寺 — the sun moves across the temple = TIME). 間 = interval (gate 門 + sun 日 — the sun passing through a gate = SPACE/TIME in between). "SUN (日) at the TEMPLE (寺) between GATES (門) = TIME/INTERVAL!"

---

## 24. 無い (ない / nai) — nonexistent, not being

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: nai)**
"NYE — like a lightsaber NYE sound — the thing is GONE, it's NOT THERE! NAI!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 無)**
無 is complex — it looks like a grid with fire 灬 at the bottom. Fire burns things until they're GONE = 無い (nai) = nonexistent! Or: think of the grid as empty shelves — nothing there = NAI.

---

## 25. この (この / kono) — this one

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: kono)**
"KO-NO — like "CO-NOisseur" — a connoisseur picks THIS ONE. KONO!"

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (この)**
この: こ (here, two lines close together) + の (of/possessive). "HERE (こ) OF (の) — THIS one right here!" Closer than その (sono).

---

## 26. その (その / sono) — that one

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: sono)**
"SO-NO — "SO, NO, not this one — THAT one!" SONO!"

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (その)**
その: そ (over there, curving away) + の (of). "OVER THERE (そ) OF (の) — THAT one!" Further than この (kono).

---

## 27. あの (あの / ano) — that one there

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: ano)**
"ANO — like "UH, NO" — pointing very far away: "UH NO (ano), THAT one way over there!"

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (あの)**
あの: あ (arms wide = far away) + の (of). "WAY OVER THERE (あ) OF (の) — THAT one!" The furthest demonstrative.

---

## 28. どの (哪个 / dono) — which one

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: dono)**
"DONO — like "DOUGHNUT" — "WHICH doughnut do you want?" DONO!"

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (どの)**
どの: ど (do-question) + の (of). "DO (question) OF (の) — WHICH one?" The question word among demonstratives.

---

## 29. 見る (みる / miru) — to see, to look at

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: miru)**
"MIRROR — you LOOK at yourself in a MIRROR! MIRU = to see!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 見)**
見 has 目 (eye) on top and 儿 (legs) on bottom. An EYE (目) with LEGS (儿) — eyes that walk around = LOOKING, SEEING! Your eyes (目) walk (儿) around = 見る (miru).

---

## 30. 全然 (ぜんぜん / zenzen) — (not) at all

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: zenzen)**
"ZEN-ZEN — like a ZEN master meditating: "NOT at all interested in worldly things!" ZEN ZEN = not at all!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 全然)**
全 = all/whole (人 person + 王 king — the king covers ALL). 然 = so/correct (has fire 灬). "ALL (全) BURNED (然) = NOT AT ALL left!" Everything is gone = not at all.

---

## 31. 面白い (おもしろい / omoshiroi) — interesting, amusing

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: omoshiroi)**
"O-MO-SHI-ROI: OH MO (oh my), SHI (she) ROI (royal)! "Oh my, she's royally INTERESTING!""

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 面白い)**
面 = face/surface (looks like a face with a line down the middle). 白 = white (a drop of white inside a box). "Your FACE (面) turns WHITE (白) from LAUGHTER — that's how INTERESTING and amusing it is!" When something is omoshiroi, your face (面) goes 白 (white) from laughing.

---

## 32. する (する / suru) — to do, to make

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: suru)**
"SUE-ROO — Sue ROO-ts around doing things! SUE-ROO = to DO!"

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (する)**
する is all hiragana: す (a loop — like a busy circle) + る (a spiral — motion). A busy person spinning in circles, DOing things = する (suru)!

---

## 33. なる (なる / naru) — to become

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: naru)**
"NARU — like NARUTO! Naruto wants to BECOME Hokage! NARU = to BECOME!"

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (なる)**
なる: な (has a cross — like transformation, things crossing/changing) + る (loop). Something crossing/changing (な) and looping into something new (る) = BECOMING. NARU!

---

## 34. 先生 (せんせい / sensei) — teacher

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: sensei)**
"SENSEI — you already know this! It means TEACHER in Japanese. SEN (ahead) + SEI (life) = one who lived life AHEAD of you = TEACHER!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 先生)**
先 = ahead/previous (has 儿 legs walking ahead + 一 one). 生 = life/birth (sprout growing from ground). "One who walks AHEAD (先) in LIFE (生) = TEACHER (先生)!" Your sensei is ahead of you in life's journey.

---

## 35. ください (ください / kudasai) — please give..., please do...

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: kudasai)**
"KUDASAI — sounds like "COOL-DASAI" — "Please (cool-dasai) give me something cool!""

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (ください)**
ください: く (open mouth, asking) + だ (bold question) + さ (pointing) + い (existence). An open mouth (く) asking boldly (だ) and pointing (さ) at what you want (い) = PLEASE give me that!

---

## 36. 名前 (なまえ / namae) — name

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: namae)**
"NA-MAE — like "NAMASTE" — "NAMASTE, my NAME is..." NAMAE!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 名前)**
名 = name (夕 evening + 口 mouth — your mouth says your name at evening introductions). 前 = before/front (has scissors 刂 and a boat 月). "Your mouth (口) in the EVENING (夕) says your name BEFORE (前) introducing yourself = NAME (名前)!"

---

## 37. 教える (おしえる / oshieru) — to teach, to tell

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: oshieru)**
"OH-SHIE-RU — "OH, SHIE (like she), RU (running)!" OH, SHE's RUNNING to TEACH you!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 教)**
教 has 耂 (old/elder) + 子 (child) on the left, and 攵 (hand/force) on the right. An ELDER (老) with a CHILD (子) and a HAND (攵) guiding them = TEACHING! The elder teaches the child with their hand = 教える (oshieru).

---

## 38. ここ (ここ / koko) — here

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: koko)**
"KO-KO — like "COCO" — "COCO is RIGHT HERE!" KOKO = HERE!"

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (ここ)**
ここ: two こ's! Double "here" = RIGHT HERE! こ = close/proximal. Two of them = emphatic HERE. KOKO!

---

## 39. そこ (そこ / soko) — there

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: soko)**
"SO-KO — "SO CLOSE, but not HERE — over THERE!" SOKO!"

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (そこ)**
そこ: そ (curving away) + こ (here). Curving AWAY from here (そ) + here (こ) = over THERE. SOKO!

---

## 40. あそこ (あそこ / asoko) — over there

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: asoko)**
"A-SO-KO — "A SOFA is way over THERE!" ASOKO = OVER THERE!"

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (あそこ)**
あそこ: あ (arms wide = far) + そ (away) + こ (here). "ARMS WIDE (あ) pointing AWAY (そ) from HERE (こ)" = very FAR away = OVER THERE!

---

## 41. どこ (どこ / doko) — where

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: doko)**
"DO-ko — "DO you KNOW where?" DOKO = WHERE?!"

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (どこ)**
どこ: ど (question marker — DO) + こ (here). "DO (question) HERE (こ)?" = WHERE IS HERE? DOKO = WHERE?

---

## 42. 置く (おく / oku) — to put, to place

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound:oku)**
"OH-KU — "OH, OK!" You PUT something down and say "OH, OK!""

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 置)**
置 has 罒 (net/net) on top and 直 (straight/fix) below. You use a NET (罒) to STRAIGHTEN (直) things out by PUTTING them in place. 置く (oku) = to PUT, to PLACE.

---

## 43. 家 (いえ / ie) — house, home

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: ie)**
"EE-EH — "EE-EH, that's my HOUSE!" (pointing at home)."

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 家)**
家 has 宀 (roof) on top and 豕 (pig) on the bottom. A ROOF (宀) with a PIG (豕) underneath = HOME! In old Japan, having a pig under your roof meant you had a HOUSE. 家 = ie = house.

---

## 44. 帰る (かえる / kaeru) — to return, to go back

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: kaeru)**
"KAERU — like a FROG (蛙 kaeru)! A FROG always RETURNS to its pond!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 帰)**
帰 has 帚 (broom) on the right and 巾 (cloth) on the left. Think: "I grab my BROOM (帚) and CLOTH (巾) and RETURN HOME to clean!" 帰る (kaeru) = to go back, to return.

---

## 45. 話 (はなし / hanashi) — talk, story

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: hanashi)**
"HA-NA-SHI — "HA! NA (then) SHI (she) told a STORY!" HANASHI!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 話)**
話 has 言 (word/speech) on the left and 舌 (tongue) on the right. Your TONGUE (舌) makes WORDS (言) = a TALK, a STORY! HANASHI!

---

## 46. 知る (しる / shiru) — to know

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: shiru)**
"SHI-RU — "SHE RULES because she KNOWS everything!" SHIRU!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 知)**
知 has 矢 (arrow) on the left and 口 (mouth) on the right. An ARROW (矢) of information enters your MOUTH (口) = you NOW KNOW! Knowledge pierces in like an arrow. 知る (shiru) = to know.

---

## 47. 彼女 (かのじょ / kanojo) — she, her, girlfriend

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: kanojo)**
"KA-NO-JO — "CA, NO, JOKE — she is NOT a joke, she's my GIRLFRIEND!""

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 彼女)**
彼 = he (彳 + 皮). 女 = woman. The "he" (彼) + "woman" (女) = HIS WOMAN = his GIRLFRIEND! She (彼女) is the woman connected to "him."

---

## 48. ちゃん (ちゃん / chan) — chan (affectionate suffix)

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: chan)**
"CHAN — like CHAN in "CHANEL"! Add CHAN to show affection — like a cute designer label for names!"

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (ちゃん)**
ちゃん: ち (chi = thousand, looks like a little swirl) + ん (nn, closing sound). A THOUSAND (ち) little kisses (ん) = CHAN! The cutest honorific.

---

## 49. はい (はい / hai) — yes (polite)

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: hai)**
"HAI — you've heard this! "HAI!" = YES! Quick and sharp, like a military "YES, SIR!""

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (はい)**
はい: は (ha = topic marker, mouth opening) + い (existence). Your mouth opens (は) and confirms existence (い) = YES! HAI!

---

## 50. いいえ (いいえ / iie) — no (polite)

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: iie)**
"I-I-E — "I... I... Ehh..." — hesitating before saying NO. IIE = NO!"

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (いいえ)**
いいえ: い (exists) + い (exists) + え (stretched/strained). "It exists, it exists... NO WAIT, EHH, it doesn't!" Double い then え = struggling to say NO. IIE!

---

## 51. 日本 (にほん / nihon) — Japan

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: nihon)**
"NI-HON — NI (two) + HON (books). Japan is known for TWO (NI) great BOOKS (HON) — or think: the land of the rising sun!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 日本)**
日 = sun (a rectangle with a line — the SUN). 本 = origin/root (tree 木 with a base line). "The SUN's (日) ORIGIN (本) = JAPAN!" Japan = land of the rising sun = where the sun originates.

---

## 52. 学校 (がっこう / gakkou) — school

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: gakkou)**
"GAK-KOU — "GOTTA GO TO SCHOOL!" GAK-KOU!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 学校)**
学 = learning (has 子 child under a roof 宀 with two X's = studying at a desk). 校 = school (tree 木 +交 = where trees grow and people interact). "A CHILD (学) under a ROOF studying with TREES (校) around = SCHOOL!"

---

## 53. 行く (いく / iku) — to go

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: iku)**
"IKU — "I GO!" IKU sounds like "I GO" — I GO (iku) there!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 行)**
行 has 彳 (step/going) and 亍 (stop). Two parts of a crossroad — you're WALKING, GOING somewhere! 行く (iku) = to GO.

---

## 54. 明日 (あした / ashita) — tomorrow

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: ashita)**
"A-SHI-TA — "A SHOE (ashi) will step into TOMORROW!" ASHITA!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 明日)**
明 = bright (日 sun + 月 moon — sun and moon together = BRIGHT). 日 = day. "BRIGHT (明) DAY (日) coming next = TOMORROW!" Tomorrow will be a bright new day.

---

## 55. 来る (くる / kuru) — to come

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: kuru)**
"KOOL-ROO — "A KOOL ROO (kangaroo) COMES hopping in!" KURU!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 来)**
来 looks like a tree 木 with two strokes on the sides. Think of arrows pointing INWARD toward a tree = something COMING TO the tree! 来る (kuru) = to come.

---

## 56. また (また / mata) — again, also

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: mata)**
"MATA — like "MY TA" (ta-da!) — "TA-DA! Here I am AGAIN!" MATA!"

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (また)**
また: ま (round, looping) + た (past time marker). Something that loops (ま) into the past (た) and comes back = AGAIN! MATA!

---

## 57. いつ (いつ / itsu) — when

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: itsu)**
"ITSU — like "IT'S YOU? When did YOU get here?" ITSU = WHEN?!"

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (いつ)**
いつ: い (go/exist) + つ (point/end). "GO (い) to the POINT (つ) — WHEN does it end?" ITSU = WHEN?

---

## 58. 思う (おもう / omou) — to think

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: omou)**
"OH-MO — "OH MO (oh my)! Let me THINK about that!" OMOU!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 思)**
思 has 田 (rice field) on top and 心 (heart) on the bottom. Your HEART (心) working in a FIELD (田) = THINKING! When your heart works in the field of your mind, you 思う (omou) = think.

---

## 59. こう (こう / kou) — like this, this kind

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: kou)**
"KOH — "KOH, do it LIKE THIS!" KOU = this way!"

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (こう)**
こう: こ (here, close) + う (direction/motion). HERE (こ) with MOTION (う) = LIKE THIS, this WAY! KOU!

---

## 60. そう (そう / sou) — in such manner, that way

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: sou)**
"SO — "SO, that's how it is!" SOU = THAT WAY, like that!"

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (そう)**
そう: そ (that/over there) + う (direction). THAT direction (そ) + motion (う) = IN THAT WAY, SO (sou)!

---

## 61. どう (どう / dou) — how, what

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: dou)**
"DO — "DO it? HOW?" DOU = HOW? WHAT?"

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (どう)**
どう: ど (question marker, DO) + う (motion). "DO (question) + MOTION = HOW do you DO it?" DOU!

---

## 62. 会う (あう / au) — to meet

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: au)**
"AU — "A-U! A and U MEET to make the vowel chart!" Or: "I'll SEE YOU (au) — we MEET!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 会)**
会 has 人 (person) on top and 云 (cloud/say) below. Two PEOPLE (人) meeting above the CLOUDS (云) = a MEETING! 会う (au) = to meet.

---

## 63. 次 (つぎ / tsugi) — next, subsequent

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: tsugi)**
"TSU-GI — "TSUGI (tsugi) is NEXT!" Or: "TOOGIE — who's NEXT on TOOGIE (dance show)?"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 次)**
次 has 冫 (ice/cold) on the left and 欠 (lack/yawn) on the right. "ICE (冫) is LACKING (欠) — we need the NEXT batch!" 次 = NEXT.

---

## 64. 方 (ほう / hou) — direction, side

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: hou)**
"HO-HO — like Santa's HO HO — Santa goes in that DIRECTION!" Or: "HO! That SIDE over there!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 方)**
方 has a top stroke like an arrow and 方 (square/direction). The character itself looks like an arrow pointing in a DIRECTION! 方 (hou) = direction, side.

---

## 65. 方 (かた / kata) — person (polite form)

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: kata)**
"KA-TA — "KARATE person!" A MARTIAL ARTS PERSON (kata) is very respectful!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 方)**
Same kanji 方, but read KATA when meaning "person" (polite). Think: 方 (direction) → a person has a DIRECTION in life = a RESPECTED PERSON. KATA = polite "person."

---

## 66. 聞く (きく / kiku) — to hear

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: kiku)**
"KEY-COO — "I KEY in and COO (listen) — I HEAR something!" KIKU!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 聞)**
聞 has 門 (gate) on the outside and 耳 (ear) on the inside. An EAR (耳) at a GATE (門) = HEARING! You press your ear to the gate to HEAR what's happening inside. 聞く (kiku) = to hear.

---

## 67. 聞く (きく / kiku) — to ask

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: kiku)**
"KEY-COO — "I KEY (listen) then ASK questions!" KIKU = to ASK!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 聞)**
Same kanji as "to hear" — 聞 (gate 門 + ear 耳). To ASK is the flip side of HEARING: you HEAR (ear at gate) then ASK (ear at gate). KIKU covers both meanings!

---

## 68. お願いします (おねがいします / onegaishimasu) — please

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: onegaishimasu)**
"ONEGAI-SHIMASU: ONE-GUY-SHE-MASU — "ONE GUY (she) MASUKES a PLEASE!" Or break it: ONE-GAI (one wish) + SHIMASU (do) = "MAKE ONE WISH, PLEASE!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of お願い)**
願 = wish/request (has 頁 head/page + 原 plain/origin — your HEAD asking for an original wish). お願い = a WISH, a PLEASE. "I PUT MY HEAD (願) down and ASK: PLEASE!"

---

## 69. 分かる (わかる / wakaru) — to understand

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: wakaru)**
"WA-KARU — "WOW, I GET IT! I UNDERSTAND!" Or: "WACK-A-RULE — I WACK the rule until I UNDERSTAND it!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 分)**
分 has 八 (divide) on top and 刀 (knife) below. A KNIFE (刀) DIVIDING (八) things = you can now SEPARATE and UNDERSTAND! When you 分 (divide) knowledge, you UNDERSTAND (wakaru).

---

## 70. よく (よく / yoku) — well

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: yoku)**
"YO-KU — "YO! KU (cool)! Things are going WELL!" YOKU!"

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (よく)**
よく: よ (good/night) + く (open). "GOOD (よ) + OPEN (く) = things OPEN up WELL!" YOKU!

---

## 71. よく (よく / yoku) — often

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: yoku)**
"YO-KU — "I OFTEN say YO-KU (well)!" Same word, different meaning!"

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (よく)**
よく: よ (good) + く (open/frequency). "GOOD (よ) things happen OFTEN (く as in repeated opening)!" YOKU = OFTEN.

---

## 72. 考える (かんがえる / kangaeru) — to think, to consider

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: kangaeru)**
"KAN-GA-ERU — "CAN (kan) a GA (gas) ERU (frog) THINK?" A frog THINKING with a gas brain — how absurd!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 考)**
考 has 老 (old) on top and a bent stroke below. An OLD person (老) bending over = THINKING deeply! OLD people are known for THINKING (考) a lot. 考える (kangaeru) = to think, to consider.

---

## 73. ありがとうございます (ありがとうございます / arigatou gozaimasu) — thank you (formal)

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: arigatou gozaimasu)**
"ARI-GA-TOU GOZAI-MASU: "A RIG (ari) a TOY (tou) GOES I (gozai) MASU (my house)! THANK YOU for the rig/toy going to my house!""

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 有り難い)**
有り難い: 有 (exist) + 難 (difficult). "It EXISTS (有) but it's DIFFICULT (難) to repay such kindness = THANK YOU!" Gratitude for something rare and precious. ARIGATOU!

---

## 74. 話す (はなす / hanasu) — to talk, to speak

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: hanasu)**
"HA-NA-SU — "HA! NA (then) SU (sue) TALKS!" HANASU = to TALK!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 話)**
話 has 言 (speech) on the left and 舌 (tongue) on the right. Your TONGUE (舌) makes SPEECH (言) = TALKING! 話す (hanasu) = to speak, to talk.

---

## 75. もっと (もっと / motto) — more, a bit more

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: motto)**
"MOTTO — like "MOTTO" in Italian (phrase)! "I want MORE! MOTTO!""

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (もっと)**
もっと: も (also/more) + っ (emphasis/geminate) + と (and/with). "MORE (も) with EMPHASIS (っ) AND (と) = EVEN MORE!" MOTTO!

---

## 76. ゆっくり (ゆっくり / yukkuri) — slowly

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: yukkuri)**
"YU-KKU-RI — "YOU COOL-ly go SLOWLY." YUKKURI = slowly, take your time!"

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (ゆっくり)**
ゆっくり: ゆ (leisurely curve) + っ (pause) + く (open) + り (gentle). A LEISURELY (ゆ) PAUSE (っ), then OPENLY (く) GENTLY (り) = SLOWLY! YUKKURI!

---

## 77. 言う (いう / iu) — to say

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: iu)**
"EE-YOU — "I (EE) YOU (YOU) — I SAY to you!" IU = to SAY!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 言)**
言 has 亠 (lid) on top and 口 (mouth) below, with horizontal strokes like words coming out. A MOUTH (口) with WORDS coming out = SAYING! 言う (iu) = to say.

---

## 78. 友達 (ともだち / tomodachi) — friend, companion

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: tomodachi)**
"TO-MO-DA-CHI: TO MO (my) DA (darling) CHI (tea)! "TO MY DARLING, have TEA!" — that's what FRIENDS do!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 友達)**
友 = friend (looks like two hands clasping). 達 = companion (has 辶 walk + 羊 sheep). "Two hands CLASPING (友) while WALKING (達) together = FRIENDS!" 友達 (tomodachi) = friend.

---

## 79. 待つ (まつ / matsu) — to wait

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: matsu)**
"MA-TSU — "MASTER, please WAIT!" MATSU!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 待)**
待 has 彳 (step) on the left and 寺 (temple) on the right. You STOP STEPPING (彳) at the TEMPLE (寺) = WAITING! You wait at the temple. 待つ (matsu) = to wait.

---

## 80. 昨日 (きのう / kinou) — yesterday

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: kinou)**
"KI-NO-U — "KEY NO U (you) — the KEY to YESTERDAY is knowing where YOU were!" KINOU!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 昨日)**
昨 = previous (日 sun + 乍 just now — the sun that JUST PASSED). 日 = day. "The sun that JUST PASSED (昨) + DAY (日) = YESTERDAY!" 昨日 (kinou) = yesterday.

---

## 81. 町 (まち / machi) — town, city

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: machi)**
"MA-CHI — "MY CHI (energy)! I feel the CITY's energy!" MACHI = town!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 町)**
町 has 田 (rice field) on the left and 丁 (block/section) on the right. A RICE FIELD (田) organized into CITY BLOCKS (丁) = a TOWN! 町 (machi) = town, city.

---

## 82. 高い (たかい / takai) — expensive

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: takai)**
"TA-KA-I — "This TACO is EYE-wateringly expensive!" A $100 taco = TAKAI!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 高)**
高 has 亠 (lid/roof) + 口 (mouth) + 冂 (enclosure) + 口 (mouth). A tall building with stories — prices stacked SKY-HIGH! TAKAI = expensive!

---

## 83. 高い (たかい / takai) — high, tall

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: takai)**
"TACO-EYE — "Look UP with your TACO-EYE at that TALL building!" TAKAI = TALL!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 高)**
Same kanji 高 — it literally looks like a tall tower with multiple levels (口 boxes stacked)! The structure goes UP and UP = TALL, HIGH. 高い (takai) = tall.

---

## 84. 一番 (いちばん / ichiban) — (the) most, number one

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: ichiban)**
"ICHI-BAN — "ITCHY BAN (bananas)! The MOST itchy bananas!" ICHIBAN = #1!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 一番)**
一 = one (a single horizontal stroke — THE ONE). 番 = number/order. "THE ONE (一番) NUMBER = NUMBER ONE, THE MOST!" 一番 (ichiban) = the best, the most.

---

## 85. ちょっと (ちょっと / chotto) — a little

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: chotto)**
"CHO-TTO — "CHO (choose) TTO (a tiny bit)" — just a LITTLE bit! CHOTTO!"

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (ちょっと)**
ちょっと: ち (chi = thousand, many) + っ (small pause) + と (and) + お (small). Something BIG (ち) then a TINY PAUSE (っ) gets SMALLER (とお) = just a LITTLE! CHOTTO!

---

## 86. もう (もう / mou) — already, yet

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: mou)**
"MO — "MO? ALREADY?!" Like a cow MOOing in surprise — MOU, it's ALREADY done!"

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (もう)**
もう: も (already/more) + う (past). "MORE (も) in the PAST (う) = ALREADY happened!" MOU!

---

## 87. もう (もう / mou) — another, again

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: mou)**
"MO — "MO? AGAIN?!" Like a cow wanting MORE MOO-ilk! MOU = AGAIN, ANOTHER!"

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (もう)**
もう: も (more) + う (motion toward). "MORE (も) coming TOWARD (う) = ANOTHER one, AGAIN!" MOU!

---

## 88. 父 (ちち / chichi) — (speaker's) father

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: chichi)**
"CHI-CHI — "CHI-CHI, my DADDY!" (like a kid calling their dad). Or: "CHEESE! Dad always says CHEESE in photos!""

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 父)**
父 looks like a person with a wide stance and arms out. Think: DAD standing with LEGS APART, arms wide, like he's ready to HUG you! 父 = chichi = father.

---

## 89. 大好き (だいすき / daisuki) — love (like a lot)

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: daisuki)**
"DAI-SU-KI — "DAI (big) SUKI (like)! I BIG-LIKE you = I LOVE you!""

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 大好き)**
大 = big (a person with arms stretched wide). 好 = like (woman 女 + child 子). BIG (大) + LIKE (好) = LOVE! 大好き (daisuki) = I love/like you a lot!

---

## 90. 母 (はは / haha) — (speaker's) mother

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: haha)**
"HA-HA — "HA HA!" What MOM always does — laugh! HAHA = MOM!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 母)**
母 looks like a woman with two dots inside — think of them as BREASTS, representing MOTHER. The frame (几) is her body, the dots are nurturing. 母 (haha) = mother.

---

## 91. あげる (あげる / ageru) — to give (receiver not in speaker's group)

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: ageru)**
"A-GE-RU — "I GIVE a GERU (gorilla) an A (apple)!" AGERU = to GIVE!"

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (あげる)**
あげる: あ (ah, open) + げ (get) + る (loop). OPENING UP (あ) to GET (げ) something and LOOPING it over (る) to someone else = GIVING! AGERU!

---

## 92. くれる (くれる / kureru) — to give (receiver in speaker's group)

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: kureru)**
"KU-RE-RU — "COOL, RE (ray) ROO (kangaroo) GIVES me a gift!" KURERU = to give me!"

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (くれる)**
くれる: く (open, receiving) + れ (reach) + る (loop). Something REACHES (れ) to you through an OPEN hand (く) and comes TO YOU (る) = someone GIVES to you/the speaker's group. KURERU!

---

## 93. もらう (もらう / morau) — to receive

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: morau)**
"MO-RA-U — "MORE OWL (morau sounds like "more owl")! I RECEIVED more owls!""

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (もらう)**
もらう: も (more) + ら (ら = inside, receiving) + う (motion toward). "MORE (も) things coming INSIDE (ら) toward me (う) = RECEIVING!" MORAU!

---

## 94. 物 (もの / mono) — thing, object

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: mono)**
"MO-NO — "MO' NO? I want MO' THINGS!" MONO = thing!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 物)**
物 has 牛 (cow) on the left and 勿 (do not) on the right. "A COW (牛) is NOT (勿) nothing — it's a THING!" 物 (mono) = thing, object.

---

## 95. 君 (きみ / kimi) — you (to someone of equal or lower status)

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: kimi)**
"KEY-ME — "KEY to ME? YOU (kimi) have the key!" KIMI = you!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 君)**
君 has 尹 (govern/rule) on top and 口 (mouth) below. A RULER (尹) with a MOUTH (口) = someone who speaks with authority, addressing YOU directly. KIMI = you!

---

## 96. 中 (なか / naka) — inside, middle

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: naka)**
"NA-KA — "NA (then) KARATE (ka)! Karate is practiced INSIDE a dojo!" NAKA!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 中)**
中 is a vertical line through a box — something going RIGHT THROUGH THE MIDDLE! The line pierces the center of the rectangle = INSIDE, MIDDLE. 中 (naka) = inside.

---

## 97. 出来る (できる / dekiru) — to be able to do, to be good at

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: dekiru)**
"DEKI-RU — "I CAN DO IT! DEKI (day-key) RU (rule)! Today's the day I CAN DO it!""

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 出)**
出 looks like a foot leaving a cave — COMING OUT! When you COME OUT of your shell, you CAN DO things! 出 (exit/come out) + 来 (come) = something COMING OUT = ABLE TO DO! 出来る (dekiru) = can do.

---

## 98. 本当 (ほんとう / hontou) — truth, reality

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: hontou)**
"HON-TOU — "HON (book) TOU (tower)! The truth is written in the BOOK TOWER!" HONTOU!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 本当)**
本 = book/origin (tree 木 + base). 当 = hit/correct (looks like something hitting the mark). "The ORIGIN (本) that HITS the MARK (当) = the TRUTH!" 本当 (hontou) = truth.

---

## 99. 立つ (たつ / tatsu) — to stand, to rise

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: tatsu)**
"TA-TSU — "TATTOO (tatsu)! I STAND proud with my tattoo!" TATSU = to STAND!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 立)**
立 looks like a person standing with legs apart and arms out. The top stroke is the head, the two strokes below are arms/legs = a PERSON STANDING! 立つ (tatsu) = to stand.

---

## 100. 前 (まえ / mae) — front, before

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: mae)**
"MA-E — "MY EYE looks to the FRONT!" MAE = before, front!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 前)**
前 has 一 (one) + 月 (moon) + 刂 (knife). Think: "The moon (月) with a knife (刂) in FRONT (前) of it — the knife cuts BEFORE the moon rises!" 前 (mae) = front, before.
# Kaishi 1.5k Mnemonics — Words 101–200

---

## 101. 入る (はいる / hairu) — to enter

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: hairu)**
"HAI-RU — "HIGH ROO (kangaroo) jumps and ENTERS the room!" HAIRU!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 入)**
入 looks like a person leaning forward, walking THROUGH a door. The two strokes spread apart like someone stepping inside. 入 (hairu) = to ENTER.

---

## 102. 家族 (かぞく / kazoku) — family

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: kazoku)**
"KA-ZO-KU — "CAR (ka) ZOO (zo) KU (cool)!" My FAMILY goes to the car zoo — it's cool!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 家族)**
家 = house/roof + pig (you already know this). 族 = tribe/group (方 direction + 矢 arrow + 人 person). "Under one ROOF (家) with your TRIBE (族) = FAMILY!" 家族 (kazoku) = family.

---

## 103. 住む (すむ / sumu) — to live, to reside

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: sumu)**
"SUE-MOO — "SUE MOOs — she LIVES on a farm!" SUMU = to live!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 住)**
住 has 亻(person) on the left and 主 (main/master) on the right. A PERSON (亻) who is the MASTER (主) of a place = they LIVE there! 住む (sumu) = to reside.

---

## 104. 大きな (おおきな / ookina) — big, large

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: ookina)**
"OO-KI-NA — "OO (wow!) KI (key) NA (nah)!" "Wow, that's a BIG key? Nah, it's even BIGGER!""

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 大)**
大 is a person with arms stretched WIDE — as wide as possible = BIG! Add きな to make it an adjective. 大きな (ookina) = big, large.

---

## 105. 大きい (おおきい / ookii) — big, large

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: ookii)**
"OO-KII — "OO-WEE, that's KII-inda big!" OOKII!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 大)**
Same kanji 大 — a person with arms spread WIDE = BIG! 大きい (ookii) = big. The -い ending makes it an i-adjective.

---

## 106. 声 (こえ / koe) — voice, sound

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: koe)**
"CO-A (like CO-Acola) — "I HEAR your VOICE say CO-A!" KOE = voice!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 声)**
声 has 士 (warrior) on top and a curved bottom. Think of a WARRIOR (士) using his VOICE to shout commands! The top part is a head, the bottom is sound waves. 声 (koe) = voice.

---

## 107. 目 (め / me) — eye

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: me)**
"ME — "Look at ME! Use your EYE!" ME = EYE!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 目)**
目 is literally a sideways EYE — a rectangle with lines inside like an iris and pupil! Turn it upright and it's an eye shape. 目 (me) = eye. One of the easiest kanji!

---

## 108. 手 (て / te) — hand

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: te)**
"TEA — "I hold my TEA in my HAND!" TE = HAND!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 手)**
手 looks like a hand with FIVE fingers — the vertical stroke is the palm and the horizontal strokes are fingers! 手 (te) = hand.

---

## 109. 上げる (あげる / ageru) — to raise, to increase

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: ageru)**
"A-GE-RU — "I GIVE a gorilla a boost and RAISE it up!" AGERU!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 上)**
上 has a horizontal base line with a stroke going UP. Something sitting on a line going UPWARD = RAISING! 上げる (ageru) = to raise, to lift up.

---

## 110. 嫌い (きらい / kirai) — dislike

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: kirai)**
"KI-RAI — "KEY RYE (bread)! I DISLIKE rye bread!" KIRAI!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 嫌)**
嫌 has 女 (woman) on the left and 兼 (simultaneously) on the right. A WOMAN (女) who simultaneously (兼) does something you DON'T like = DISLIKE! 嫌い (kirai) = to hate, dislike.

---

## 111. おじいさん (おじいさん / ojiisan) — grandfather

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: ojiisan)**
"OH-JII-SAN — "OH, JEAN (jii) SAN!" Your GRANDFATHER is named OH JEAN SAN!"

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (おじいさん)**
お (o = honorific prefix) + じい (jii = old man, from 爺) + さん (san = honorific). An HONORABLE (お) OLD MAN (じい) with SAN = GRANDFATHER! The じい looks like an old man with a cane.

---

## 112. おばあさん (おばあさん / obaasan) — grandmother

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: obaasan)**
"OH-BAA-SAN — "OH, BAA (like a sheep) SAN!" Your GRANDMA goes BAA like a sheep!"

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (おばあさん)**
お (honorific) + ばあ (baa = old woman, from 婆) + さん (honorific). An HONORABLE (お) OLD WOMAN (ばあ) with SAN = GRANDMOTHER!

---

## 113. 元気 (げんき / genki) — energetic, healthy

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: genki)**
"GEN-KI — "GEN (dragon ball) KI (energy)!" Dragon Ball gives you GENKI — ENERGY and HEALTH!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 元気)**
元 = origin/beginning (two lines under a roof = foundation). 気 = spirit/energy (rice grain with steam). "Your ORIGINAL (元) SPIRIT (気) = ENERGY!" Being 元気 means your original energy is flowing!

---

## 114. いつも (いつも / itsumo) — always, usually

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: itsumo)**
"I-TSU-MO — "I (i) TSU (two) MO (more)! I ALWAYS want TWO MORE!""

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (いつも)**
いつも: い (go) + つ (point) + も (more). "GO (い) to the POINT (つ) EVERY TIME (も) = ALWAYS!" Itsumo = always.

---

## 115. 持つ (もつ / motsu) — to have, to hold

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: motsu)**
"MOT-SU — "MOTSU (offal) — you HOLD it in your hand!" Or: "I'VE GOT (motsu) it!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 持)**
持 has 扌(hand radical) on the left and 寺 (temple) on the right. Your HAND (扌) at the TEMPLE (寺) HOLDS the prayer beads! 持つ (motsu) = to hold, to have.

---

## 116. みんな (みんな / minna) — all, everybody

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: minna)**
"MIN-NA — "MINI and NAomi — EVERYBODY is here!" MINNA!"

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (みんな)**
みんな: み (see/look) + ん (everyone) + な (name). "SEE (み) EVERYONE's NAME (な) = ALL OF YOU!" MINNA = everybody!

---

## 117. 使う (つかう / tsukau) — to use

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: tsukau)**
"TSU-KA-U — "TSU (two) CAR (ka) U (you) — TWO CARS, USE them!""

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 使)**
使 has 亻(person) on the left and 吏 (officer) on the right. A PERSON (亻) acting as an OFFICER (吏) USES tools and authority! 使う (tsukau) = to use.

---

## 118. 大丈夫 (だいじょうぶ / daijoubu) — alright, OK

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: daijoubu)**
"DAI-JOU-BU — "DIE (dai) JOU (joe) BU (boo)! Joe doesn't DIE — he's ALRIGHT!" DAIJOUBU!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 大丈夫)**
大 = big. 丈夫 = husband/man (strong man radical). A BIG (大) STRONG MAN (丈夫) = everything is ALRIGHT! A big strong man protects you = DAIJOUBU, no worries!

---

## 119. 始める (はじめる / hajimeru) — to start

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: hajimeru)**
"HA-JI-ME-RU — "HAZE (ha-ji) me, ROO (ru)!" The kangaroo says "HAZE me to START things!""

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 始)**
始 has 女 (woman) on the left and 台 (pedestal/stage) on the right. A WOMAN (女) steps onto a STAGE (台) = the BEGINNING of a show! 始める (hajimeru) = to start.

---

## 120. 今日 (きょう / kyou) — today

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: kyou)**
"KEY-OH — "KEY-OH! What am I doing TODAY?" KYOU = TODAY!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 今日)**
日 = sun/day. The top part of 今 (now) combined with 日 (day) = TODAY, the CURRENT DAY. 今日 (kyou) = today.

---

## 121. 部屋 (へや / heya) — room

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: heya)**
"HEY-A — "HEY-A! Come into my ROOM!" HEYA!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 部屋)**
部 = section/part (has 阝village radical + 立 stand + 口 mouth). 屋 = room/house (尸 body + 至 arrive). A SECTION (部) where you ARRIVE (屋) = a ROOM! 部屋 (heya) = room.

---

## 122. 悪い (わるい / warui) — bad

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: warui)**
"WAR-U-I — "WAR is BAD!" WARUI = bad!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 悪)**
悪 has 亜 (sub-) on top and 心 (heart) on bottom. A SUB-STANDARD (亸) HEART (心) = BAD! When your heart is below par, things are BAD. 悪い (warui) = bad.

---

## 123. まあ (まあ / maa) — well...

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: maa)**
"MAA — like "MYAH" — a cat says MYAH when it's being NONCOMMITTAL. MAA = well..."

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (まあ)**
まあ: ま (wait/round) + あ (ah). "WAIT (ま)... AHH (あ)... well..." A hesitant sound. MAA = well, I guess...

---

## 124. 食べる (たべる / taberu) — to eat

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: taberu)**
"TABLE-ROO — "TABLE-ROO eats at the TABLE!" TABERU = to EAT!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 食)**
食 has a "roof" on top and 飠 (eat radical) below. It looks like a MOUTH opening over food! The strokes suggest food being lifted to a mouth. 食べる (taberu) = to eat.

---

## 125. 沢山 (たくさん / takusan) — a lot, plenty

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: takusan)**
"TAKU-SAN — "TACO SAN (Mr. Taco) has A LOT of tacos!" TAKUSAN!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 沢山)**
沢 = swamp (氵water + 尺 measuring). 山 = mountain. A SWAMP (沢) as big as a MOUNTAIN (山) = A LOT! 沢山 (takusan) = plenty, a lot.

---

## 126. 場所 (ばしょ / basho) — place, location

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: basho)**
"BA-SHO — "BARREL (ba) SHOW (sho) — the SHOW is at this PLACE!""

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 場所)**
場 = place (土 earth + 昜 bright/sun). 所 = place (戶 door + 斤 axe). "A bright (場) DOOR (所) to a LOCATION!" 場所 (basho) = place.

---

## 127. 呼ぶ (よぶ / yobu) — to call, to name

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: yobu)**
"YO-BU — "YO! BUDDY! I CALL you!" YOBU = to call!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 呼)**
呼 has 口 (mouth) on the left and 乎 (question marker) on the right. A MOUTH (口) asking a QUESTION (乎) = CALLING out! 呼ぶ (yobu) = to call, to name.

---

## 128. 動く (うごく / ugoku) — to move

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: ugoku)**
"U-GO-KU — "YOU GO, COOL! MOVE it!" UGOKU = to MOVE!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 動)**
動 has 重 (heavy) on the left and 力 (power) on the right. Even when something is HEAVY (重), with POWER (力) it can MOVE! 動く (ugoku) = to move.

---

## 129. 気持ち (きもち / kimochi) — feeling, sensation

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: kimochi)**
"KI-MO-CHI — "KI (energy) MO (more) CHI (tea)!" Your FEELING needs MORE TEA to feel good!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 気持ち)**
気 = spirit/energy. 持 = hold (hand radical + temple). "HOLDING (持) your SPIRIT (気) = your FEELING!" 気持ち (kimochi) = feeling, sensation.

---

## 130. 誰 (だれ / dare) — who

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: dare)**
"DARE — "I DARE you to guess WHO it is!" DARE = WHO?"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 誰)**
誰 has 言 (speech) on the left and 隹 (bird) on the right. "WORDS (言) from a BIRD (隹) — WHO is singing?" 誰 (dare) = who.

---

## 131. 子 (こ / ko) — child, kid

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: ko)**
"CO (like Coca-Cola) — "A KID drinks CO-la!" KO = child!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 子)**
子 looks like a BABY wrapped in a blanket with arms out! A simple pictograph of a CHILD. 子 (ko) = child.

---

## 132. どんな (どんな / donna) — what kind of...

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: donna)**
"DONNA — "DONNA (like Donna from that show), WHAT KIND of person is she?""

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (どんな)**
どんな: ど (question DO) + ん (what) + な (what). "DO + WHAT + WHAT = WHAT KIND of...?" DONNA = what kind.

---

## 133. 意味 (いみ / imi) — meaning, significance

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: imi)**
"EE-MEE — "EE-MEE? What's the MEANING of that?" IMI!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 意味)**
意 = idea/mind (音 sound + 心 heart — the sound of your heart = your IDEA). 味 = taste (口 mouth + 未 not yet — tasting something NOT YET known). "The IDEA (意) you TASTE (味) = the MEANING!" 意味 (imi) = meaning.

---

## 134. 言葉 (ことば / kotoba) — word, language

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: kotoba)**
"KO-TO-BA — "CALL (ko) TOM (to) BA (bar)!" He called Tom to a bar — WORDS were exchanged!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 言葉)**
言 = speech/word (mouth with lines coming out). 葉 = leaf (草 grass + 世 world + 木 tree). "WORDS (言) that fall like LEAVES (葉) = WORDS, LANGUAGE!" 言葉 (kotoba) = word.

---

## 135. 心配 (しんぱい / shinpai) — worry

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: shinpai)**
"SHIN-PAI — "SHIN (shin) PAIN! I'm WORRIED about my shin pain!" SHINPAI!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 心配)**
心 = heart. 配 = distribute/blend (酉 jar + 己 self). Your HEART (心) is all MIXED UP (配) = WORRY! When your heart is scrambled, you 心配 (shinpai).

---

## 136. 様子 (ようす / yousu) — appearance, state

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: yousu)**
"YO-U-SU — "YO, you SU (see) my APPEARANCE?" YOUSU = how things look!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 様子)**
様 = manner/appearance (木 tree + 羊 sheep + 水 water — nature's manner). 子 = child. "The MANNER (様) of a CHILD (子) = their APPEARANCE and STATE!" 様子 (yousu) = appearance.

---

## 137. 信じる (しんじる / shinjiru) — to believe, to trust

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: shinjiru)**
"SHIN-JI-RU — "SHIN (shin) JI (ji) ROO (kangaroo)! I TRUST my shin and my ji-roo!""

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 信)**
信 has 亻(person) on the left and 言 (word/speech) on the right. A PERSON (亻) whose WORDS (言) are true = BELIEVABLE! You TRUST someone whose words match their actions. 信じる (shinjiru) = to believe.

---

## 138. 自分 (じぶん / jibun) — self, oneself

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: jibun)**
"JI-BUN — "JI (self) BUN (bread)!" I eat MY OWN BUN — it's MYSELF!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 自分)**
自 = self (looks like a nose — you point at YOURSELF by pointing at your nose!). 分 = divide. "Your NOSE (自) DIVIDED (分) from others = YOURSELF!" 自分 (jibun) = self.

---

## 139. 調べる (しらべる / shiraberu) — to look up, to search

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: shiraberu)**
"SHIRA-BE-RU — "SHIRA (white) BEAR (ru) SEARCHES for food!""

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 調)**
調 has 言 (speech) on the left and 周 (circumference/around) on the right. "WORDS (言) going AROUND (周) = SEARCHING, investigating every angle!" 調べる (shiraberu) = to look up.

---

## 140. 事 (こと / koto) — thing, incident

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: koto)**
"KO-TO — "CALL (ko) TOM (to) — that's a big INCIDENT!" KOTO!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 事)**
事 is a complex kanji but think of it as a HAND holding something — a THING, an INCIDENT. The bottom part 亅 looks like something being held. 事 (koto) = thing, matter.

---

## 141. 同じ (おなじ / onaji) — same, identical

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: onaji)**
"O-NA-JI — "OH, NA (nah), JEANS (ji) — those are the SAME jeans!" ONAJI!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 同)**
同 has a box (冂) with a line and 口 (mouth) inside. Think of two boxes inside each other — IDENTICAL, the SAME! 同じ (onaji) = same.

---

## 142. 申し訳ない (もうしわけない / moushiwakenai) — sorry, inexcusable

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: moushiwakenai)**
"MOU-SHI-WAKE-NAI: "MOU (already) SHI (she) WAKE (awake) NAI (not)!" "She's ALREADY not AWAKE — I'm SO SORRY!""

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 申し訳)**
申 = say/humble. 訳 = reason (言 speech + 刀 knife — cutting through the reason). "HUMBLY (申) explaining the REASON (訳) that is NOT (ない) acceptable = SORRY, INEXCUSABLE!" 申し訳ない = I'm deeply sorry.

---

## 143. もちろん (もちろん / mochiron) — of course, no doubt

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: mochiron)**
"MO-CHI-RON — "MOCHI (rice cake) IRON — of COURSE mochi is made with an IRON press!""

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (もちろん)**
もちろん: も (more) + ち (thousand) + ろん (theory). "MORE (も) than a THOUSAND (ち) THEORIES (ろん) prove it = OF COURSE!" もちろん = of course, undoubtedly.

---

## 144. とても (とても / totemo) — very

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: totemo)**
"TO-TE-MO — "TOTEM pole is VERY tall!" TOTEMO = VERY!"

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (とても)**
とても: と (and) + て (hand) + も (even). "AND (と) even your HAND (て も) can't reach — it's VERY far!" TOTEMO = very.

---

## 145. 楽しい (たのしい / tanoshii) — fun, enjoyable

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: tanoshii)**
"TA-NO-SHII — "TAN-O-SHI (tango shoe)! Dancing in TANGO SHOES is FUN!""

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 楽)**
楽 has 白 (white) on top and 木 (tree) below with dots. Think of a white tree with musical notes falling from it — pure JOY! 楽しい (tanoshii) = fun, enjoyable.

---

## 146. 失礼します (しつれいします / shitsurei shimasu) — pardon me, excuse me

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: shitsurei shimasu)**
"SHITSU-REI-SHI-MASU: "SHITSU (room) REI (grace) SHI-MASU (do it)!" "I gracefully leave the ROOM — EXCUSE ME!""

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 失礼)**
失 = lose (looks like a person with something slipping away). 礼 = courtesy (礻altar + 乙 second). "LOSING (失) your COURTESY (礼) = how rude! PARDON ME for leaving!" 失礼 = discourtesy → "Excuse me for my rudeness."

---

## 147. 手伝う (てつだう / tetsudau) — to help

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: tetsudau)**
"TE-TSU-DA-U — "TEA (te) TSU (two) DAUGHTER (da) U (you)! Two daughters help you drink tea!""

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 手伝)**
手 = hand. 伝 = transmit/pass down (亻person + 专 exclusive). "Using your HAND (手) to PASS ON (伝) assistance = HELPING!" 手伝う (tetsudau) = to help.

---

## 148. 忙しい (いそがしい / isogashii) — busy, occupied

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: isogashii)**
"I-SO-GA-SHII — "I SO GOTTA GO (ga-shii) — I'm BUSY!""

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 忙)**
忙 has 忄(heart) on the left and 亡 (death/lost) on the right. When your HEART (忄) feels LOST (亡) in tasks = BUSY! Being so busy you feel like your heart is lost. 忙しい (isogashii) = busy.

---

## 149. 仕事 (しごと / shigoto) — work, job

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: shigoto)**
"SHI-GO-TO — "SHE (shi) GOES (go) TO (to) WORK!" SHIGOTO = work!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 仕事)**
仕 = serve (亻person + 十 ten — a person serving ten people). 事 = thing/matter. "A PERSON (仕) doing a THING (事) = WORK, JOB!" 仕事 (shigoto) = work.

---

## 150. 続ける (つづける / tsuzukeru) — to continue, to keep up

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: tsuzukeru)**
"TSU-ZU-KE-RU — "TSU (two) ZOO (zu) KE (key) ROO (kangaroo)! The kangaroo KEEPS GOING to the zoo!""

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 続)**
続 has 糸 (thread) on the left and 売 (sell) on the right. A THREAD (糸) that keeps UNROLLING = CONTINUING! Like a thread that never ends. 続ける (tsuzukeru) = to continue.

---

## 151. 見せる (みせる / miseru) — to show

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: miseru)**
"MI-SE-RU — "MISS (mi) SIR (se) ROO — the kangaroo SHOWS itself!" MISERU = to SHOW!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 見)**
見 = see (目 eye + 儿 legs). "An EYE (目) with LEGS (儿) walking toward you = SHOWING itself!" 見せる (miseru) = to show, to display.

---

## 152. うち (うち / uchi) — one's household, home

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: uchi)**
"U-CHI — "U HIT (chi) home! UCHI = my HOUSEHOLD!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 家)**
In kanji it's written 家 — roof 宀 + pig 豕. "Under the ROOF with the pig = YOUR HOUSEHOLD!" うち (uchi) = my home, my family.

---

## 153. 約束 (やくそく / yakusoku) — promise, appointment

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: yakusoku)**
"YA-KU-SOKU — "YA (ya!) COOL (ku) SOCK (soku)!" "Ya, cool sock! I PROMISE to wear them!""

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 約束)**
約 = promise/reduce (糸 thread + 勺 ladle — tying a KNOT as a PROMISE). 束 = bundle (木 tree + 口 mouth — a tied bundle). "TIE a KNOT (約) in a BUNDLE (束) = a PROMISE!" 約束 = a bound agreement.

---

## 154. 忘れる (わすれる / wasureru) — to forget

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: wasureru)**
"WA-SU-RE-RU — "WAS (wa) SU (sue) RE (ray) ROO — Sue the kangaroo FORGOT where she was!""

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 忘)**
忘 has 亡 (lost/death) on top and 心 (heart) on the bottom. When something is LOST (亡) from your HEART (心) = FORGOTTEN! 忘れる (wasureru) = to forget.

---

## 155. 守る (まもる / mamoru) — to protect, to observe

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: mamoru)**
"MA-MO-RU — "MAMA (ma) MORE (mo) ROO (kangaroo) — mama protects MORE kangaroos!""

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 守)**
守 has 宀 (roof) on top and 寸 (inch/small measure) below. "Under the ROOF (宀) even a SMALL INCH (寸) of ground you PROTECT!" 守る (mamoru) = to protect.

---

## 156. 変える (かえる / kaeru) — to change, to alter

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: kaeru)**
"KA-E-RU — "CAR (ka) EEL (e) ROO (ru) — the car changes into an eel-kangaroo!" CHAOS = CHANGE!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 変)**
変 has a roof-like top and 夂 (go) at the bottom with X's in between. Think of something being CROSSed out and REBORN = CHANGED! 変える (kaeru) = to change.

---

## 157. さっき (さっき / sakki) — (only) a moment ago

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: sakki)**
"SA-KKI — "SAKE (ki)! I JUST drank sake A MOMENT AGO!""

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (さっき)**
さっき: さ (pointing back) + っ (sharp stop) + き (past time). "POINTING (さ) to a SHARP (っ) PAST (き) moment = JUST NOW, a MOMENT AGO!" SAKKI!

---

## 158. 今度 (こんど / kondo) — next time

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: kondo)**
"KON-DO — "CON (kon) DO — "I'll CON (trick) you NEXT TIME!" KONDO!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 今度)**
今 = now. 度 = degree/time. "THIS NOW (今) TIME (度) = THIS TIME, but also NEXT TIME!" Kondo can mean "this time" or "next time" depending on context.

---

## 159. これから (これから / korekara) — from now on

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: korekara)**
"KO-RE-KA-RA — "CALL (ko) RE (ray) CAR (ka) RA (ra)!" "From NOW, call Ray's car!""

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (これから)**
これ (this) + から (from). "FROM (から) THIS (これ) POINT ON = FROM NOW ON!" これから (korekara) = from here on out.

---

## 160. 止める (とめる / tomeru) — to stop (at some place)

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: tomeru)**
"TO-ME-RU — "TOM (to) ME (me) ROO (kangaroo) — TOM stops the kangaroo!""

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 止)**
止 looks like a FOOT STOPPED on the ground — the toes planted, no longer moving! The vertical line is the leg, the horizontal is the ground. 止める (tomeru) = to stop.

---

## 161. 止める (やめる / yameru) — to stop (doing something)

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: yameru)**
"YA-ME-RU — "YA (yeah!) ME (me) ROO (kangaroo) — YEAH, I'll STOP now!" YAMERU!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 止)**
Same kanji 止 (stop/cease). But read YAMERU when meaning "to quit/cease doing something." Think: "I plant my foot (止) and SAY (や) ENOUGH = I'll STOP doing that!" YAMERU!

---

## 162. 頼む (たのむ / tanomu) — to ask, to request

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: tanomu)**
"TA-NO-MU — "TAN (tan) NO (no) MOO (cow)! TAN says NO MOO — I REQUEST silence!""

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 頼)**
頼 has 束 (bundle) on the left and 頁 (head/page) on the right. "A BUNDLE (束) for your HEAD (頁) = you ASK for help carrying it!" 頼む (tanomu) = to request.

---

## 163. 許す (ゆるす / yurusu) — to forgive, to permit

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: yurusu)**
"YU-RU-SU — "YOU (yu) RULE (ru) SUE (su)! You rule that Sue is FORGIVEN!""

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 許)**
許 has 言 (speech) on the left and 午 (noon/horse) on the right. "WORDS (言) spoken at NOON (午) = PARDON declared publicly!" 許す (yurusu) = to forgive, permit.

---

## 164. 嬉しい (うれしい / ureshii) — happy, glad

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: ureshii)**
"U-RE-SHII — "YOU (u) RACE (re) SHE (shii)! You're HAPPY she won the race!""

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 嬉)**
嬉 has 喜 (joy) and 女 (woman). A WOMAN (女) filled with JOY (喜) = HAPPY! 嬉しい (ureshii) = happy, glad.

---

## 165. 頑張る (がんばる / ganbaru) — to persevere, to do one's best

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: ganbaru)**
"GAN-BA-RU — "GAN (gun) BA (bar) ROO (kangaroo)! The kangaroo at the gun bar PERSEVERES!""

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 頑張)**
頑 = stubborn (元 origin + 頁 head — an ORIGINAL head = stubborn). 張 = stretch/pull (弓 bow + 長 long). "STUBBORN (頑) and STRETCHED (張) to the limit = PERSEVERING!" 頑張る (ganbaru) = do your best!

---

## 166. なるほど (なるほど / naruhodo) — I see, really

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: naruhodo)**
"NA-RU-HO-DO — "NA (then) RU (run) HO (who) DO (what)! Then I RUN to see WHO and WHAT — I SEE, I get it!""

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (なるほど)**
なる (become) + ほど (degree/to that extent). "It BECOMES (なる) to that DEGREE (ほど) = I SEE, NOW I get it!" なるほど = aha, I see!

---

## 167. つまり (つまり / tsumari) — in short, basically

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: tsumari)**
"TSU-MA-RI — "TSU (two) MA (mother) RI (read)!" In short, TWO MOTHERS READ!"

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (つまり)**
つまり: つ (point/end) + ま (round/full circle) + り (reason). "Getting to the POINT (つ) going AROUND (ま) to the REASON (り) = IN SHORT!" つまり = in other words.

---

## 168. 国 (くに / kuni) — country

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: kuni)**
"KU-NI — "COOL (ku) KNEE (ni)! My knee belongs to my COUNTRY!""

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 国)**
国 has 囗 (enclosure/border) around 玉 (jade). "BORDERS (囗) protecting precious JADE (玉) = a COUNTRY!" 国 (kuni) = country, nation.

---

## 169. 優しい (やさしい / yasashii) — gentle, kind

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: yasashii)**
"YA-SA-SHII — "YA (yeah!) SAUCE (sa) SHE (shii) — she's KIND with her sauce!""

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 優)**
優 has 亻(person) on the left and a complex right side meaning "surplus/excellent." A PERSON (亻) who is EXCELLENT and GENEROUS = GENTLE, KIND! 優しい (yasashii) = kind.

---

## 170. 嫌 (いや / iya) — disliking, unwilling

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: iya)**
"IYA — "EE-YAH! I DON'T WANT TO!" IYA = NO, I dislike it!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 嫌)**
嫌 has 女 (woman) and 兼 (simultaneously). "A WOMAN who SIMULTANEOUSLY says NO = UNWILLING, DISLIKING!" IYA = I don't want to!

---

## 171. そんなに (そんなに / sonnani) — that much

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: sonnani)**
"SON-NA-NI — "SON (sun) NA (nah) NI (knee)!" Not THAT MUCH sun on my knee!"

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (そんなに)**
そんな (that kind of) + に (to/at). "TO (に) THAT (そんな) degree = THAT MUCH!" そんなに = not that much / that much.

---

## 172. 今回 (こんかい / konkai) — this time

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: konkai)**
"KON-KAI — "CON (kon) KAISER — Kaiser rolls THIS TIME!" KONKAI!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 今回)**
今 = now. 回 = rotation/time. "THIS (今) ROTATION (回) = THIS TIME!" 今回 (konkai) = this time around.

---

## 173. 夜 (よる / yoru) — evening, night

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: yoru)**
"YO-RU — "YO, ROO (kangaroo)! It's NIGHT time!" YORU = night!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 夜)**
夜 has 亠 (lid) on top and 夕 (evening) in the middle with a person 亻below. "A LID (亠) covering the EVENING (夕) while a PERSON (亻) sleeps = NIGHT!" 夜 (yoru) = evening.

---

## 174. 買う (かう / kau) — to buy

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: kau)**
"KAU — "COW! I BUY a cow!" KAU = to BUY!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 買)**
買 has 罒 (net) on top and 貝 (shell/money) below. "A NET (罒) to catch SHELLS (貝, old money) = BUYING things!" 買う (kau) = to buy.

---

## 175. 今まで (いままで / ima made) — until now

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: ima made)**
"IMA (now) MADE (until) — "UNTIL NOW, right UP TO this moment!""

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 今まで)**
今 = now. まで = until. "UNTIL (まで) NOW (今) = UNTIL NOW!" 今まで (ima made) = up to this point.

---

## 176. 奴 (やつ / yatsu) — that guy, (s)he (derogatory)

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: yatsu)**
"YA-TSU — "YA (yeah) TSU (two)! That GUY — yeah, the second one!""

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 奴)**
奴 has 女 (woman) on the left and 又 (again) on the right. "A WOMAN (女) who comes AGAIN (又) — that PERSON, that GUY!" 奴 (yatsu) = that dude.

---

## 177. あいつ (あいつ / aitsu) — that guy

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: aitsu)**
"A-I-TSU — "EYE (ai) TSU (two) — I SEE (ai) that GUY (tsu)!" AITSU = that dude!"

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (あいつ)**
あ (ah, pointing) + い (existence) + つ (guy/one). "AH (あ), THAT one (いつ) EXISTS (い) = THAT GUY over there!" AITSU = that guy (informal).

---

## 178. もし (もし / moshi) — if, in the event of...

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: moshi)**
"MOSHI — "MOUSE-y! If there's a MOUSE, I'm scared!" MOSHI = IF!"

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (もし)**
もし: も (if/more) + し (death/do). "IF (も) something DIES (し) = in the EVENT of!" MOSHI = if, suppose...

---

## 179. 理解 (りかい / rikai) — understanding, comprehension

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: rikai)**
"RI-KAI — "REE (ri) COW (kai = like "ky" sound)! I UNDERSTAND why the cow jumped!""

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 理解)**
理 = reason/logic (王 king + 里 village — the king's logic). 解 = untie/resolve (角 horn + 刀 knife + 牛 cow — cutting the horn off a cow to resolve things). "Using REASON (理) to UNTIE (解) a problem = UNDERSTANDING!" 理解 (rikai) = comprehension.

---

## 180. 夢 (ゆめ / yume) — dream

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: yume)**
"YOU-MAY — "YOU MAY be DREAMING!" YUME = DREAM!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 夢)**
夢 has 艒 (evening/night) elements on top, 罒 (net/eyes), and 夕 (evening) at the bottom. "CLOSED EYES (罒) in the EVENING (夕) = DREAMING!" 夢 (yume) = dream.

---

## 181. 俺 (おれ / ore) — I (male, casual)

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: ore)**
"OR-E — "OREO! I (ore) love OREOS!""

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 俺)**
俺 has 亻(person) on the left and 奄 (covering/surrounding) on the right. "A PERSON (亻) who COVERS (奄) everything — "I (ore) am in charge!" A masculine, confident way to say I. 俺 = ore.

---

## 182. 難しい (むずかしい / muzukashii) — difficult

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: muzukashii)**
"MU-ZU-KA-SHII — "MOOSE (mu) ZOO (zu) CA (ka) SHE (shii)! The moose at the zoo is HARD to understand!""

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 難)**
難 has 隹 (bird) on the right and a complex left side. Think: "Trying to catch a BIRD (隹) = DIFFICULT!" 難しい (muzukashii) = difficult, hard.

---

## 183. 今度 (こんど / kondo) — this time

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: kondo)**
"KON-DO — "CON (trick) DO — THIS TIME I'll trick you!""

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 今度)**
今 = now. 度 = degree/extent. "THIS (今) TIME (度) = THIS TIME!" Kondo used again — this time meaning "this time."

---

## 184. おかしい (おかしい / okashii) — strange, suspicious

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: okashii)**
"O-KA-SHII — "OH CA (car) SHE (shii)! OH, that car is SUSPICIOUS!" OKASHII = strange!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 怪)**
In kanji: 怪 (忄heart + 匕 dagger + 厶 private + 土 ground). A HEART (忄) that feels a DAGGER (匕) hidden in the GROUND (土) = SUSPICIOUS! おかしい (okashii) = strange, odd.

---

## 185. 男の子 (おとこのこ / otoko no ko) — boy

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: otoko no ko)**
"O-TO-KO-NO-KO: "OH! TOE (to) CALL (ko) NO (no) KID (ko)!" "Oh, the toe calls no kid — it's a BOY!""

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 男の子)**
男 = man (田 rice field + 力 strength — STRONG in the FIELD = MAN). の = of. 子 = child. "A MAN's (男) CHILD (子) = BOY!" 男の子 = boy.

---

## 186. 女の子 (おんなのこ / onna no ko) — girl

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: onna no ko)**
"ON-NA-NO-KO: "ON (on) NA (nah) NO KID!" "ON, nah — she's not a kid, she's a GIRL!""

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 女の子)**
女 = woman (looks like a person kneeling). の = of. 子 = child. "A WOMAN's (女) CHILD (子) = GIRL!" 女の子 = girl.

---

## 187. 寝る (ねる / neru) — to sleep

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: neru)**
"NE-RU — "NEAR (ne) ROO (kangaroo) — I SLEEP near the kangaroo!" NERU!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 寝)**
寝 has 宀 (roof) and various strokes suggesting a person lying down under covers. "Under a ROOF (宀) lying on a BED = SLEEPING!" 寝る (neru) = to sleep.

---

## 188. 伝える (つたえる / tsutaeru) — to convey, to transmit

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: tsutaeru)**
"TSU-TA-E-RU — "TSU (two) TAIL (ta) EEL (e) ROO — the eel-kangaroo TRANSMITS a message!""

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 伝)**
伝 has 亻(person) on the left and 专 (exclusive/sole) on the right. "A PERSON (亻) with an EXCLUSIVE (专) message = CONVEYING, TRANSMITTING!" 伝える (tsutaeru) = to pass on.

---

## 189. 小さい (ちいさい / chiisai) — small

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: chiisai)**
"CHII-SAI — "CHEESE (chii) SAKE (sai) — a SMALL cup of cheese sake!""

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 小)**
小 looks like THREE SMALL drops — tiny, little things! Two dots on the sides of a small line = SMALL. 小さい (chiisai) = small, little.

---

## 190. 選ぶ (えらぶ / erabu) — to choose, to elect

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: erabu)**
"E-RA-BU — "E (see) RA (ra) BU (boo)! I CHOOSE to see ra-boo!""

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 選)**
選 has 辶 (walk/movement) and 巽 (select). You WALK (辶) through options to SELECT (巽) the best one = CHOOSING! 選ぶ (erabu) = to choose.

---

## 191. 座る (すわる / suwaru) — to sit

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: suwaru)**
"SU-WA-RU — "SUE (su) WA (wa) RULES (ru) — Sue RULES at SITTING!""

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 座)**
座 has 广 (cliff/building) on top and 坐 (sit) below with two 人 people. "Under a BUILDING (广), TWO PEOPLE (人人) sit on GROUND (土) = SITTING!" 座る (suwaru) = to sit.

---

## 192. 簡単 (かんたん / kantan) — simple, easy

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: kantan)**
"KAN-TAN — "CAN (kan) TAN (tan)! I CAN TAN — it's EASY!" KANTAN = simple!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 簡単)**
簡 = simple (竹 bamboo + 門 gate + 日 sun). 単 = single (has 田 field shape). "BAMBOO (竹) through a GATE (門) in ONE (単) piece = SIMPLE!" 簡単 = easy.

---

## 193. お願い (おねがい / onegai) — favor

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: onegai)**
"ONE-GUY — "ONE GUY asks a FAVOR!" ONEGAI!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of お願い)**
お願い: お (honorific) + ね (sleep/ask) + が (gas/question) + い (existence). "HUMBLY (お) ASKING (ね) a QUESTION (が) about existence (い) = a FAVOR!" お願い = please, a favor.

---

## 194. 進む (すすむ / susumu) — to move forward

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: susumu)**
"SU-SU-MU — "SUE'S (su) SUMO (su-mu)! Sue's sumo wrestler MOVES FORWARD!""

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 進)**
進 has 辶 (walk) and 隹 (bird). A BIRD (隹) that WALKS (辶) FORWARD = MOVING AHEAD! 進む (susumu) = to advance.

---

## 195. 全く (まったく / mattaku) — entirely, truly

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: mattaku)**
"MA-TTA-KU — "MY (ma) TATTA (tta) COOL (ku)! I'm ENTIRELY cool!""

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 全く)**
全 = whole/all. く = emphasis. "THE WHOLE (全) THING (く) = ENTIRELY, TRULY!" 全く (mattaku) = completely, honestly.

---

## 196. なぜ (なぜ / naze) — why

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: naze)**
"NA-ZE — "NA (then) ZEUS (ze)! THEN WHY did Zeus do that?" NAZE = WHY?"

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (なぜ)**
なぜ: な (question/what) + ぜ (emphatic question). "WHAT (な) + EMPHATIC QUESTION (ぜ) = WHY?!" NAZE = why?!

---

## 197. 僕 (ぼく / boku) — I (male, polite)

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: boku)**
"BOCK (like a ROOSTER)! "I (boku) am a ROOSTER! BOKU!""

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 僕)**
僕 has 亻(person) on the left and 菐 (servant) on the right. "A PERSON (亻) who SERVES (菐) = a humble way to say I." 僕 (boku) = I (male, humble).

---

## 198. 水 (みず / mizu) — water

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: mizu)**
"MI-ZU — "MY (mi) ZOO (zu)! My zoo needs WATER!" MIZU = water!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 水)**
水 looks like flowing WATER — the central stroke is a stream, the side strokes are splashes! A pictograph of WATER flowing. 水 (mizu) = water.

---

## 199. 飲む (のむ / nomu) — to drink

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: nomu)**
"NO-MU — "NO MOO! I want to DRINK, not hear cows!" NOMU = to drink!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 飲)**
飲 has 飠 (eat radical) on the left and 欠 (lack/yawn/open mouth) on the right. "FOOD (食) with an OPEN MOUTH (欠) = DRINKING!" 飲む (nomu) = to drink.

---

## 200. 暇 (ひま / hima) — free time, not busy

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: hima)**
"HE-MA — "HEY-MA! HEY MA, I'm FREE!" HIMA = free time!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 暇)**
暇 has 日 (sun) on the left and 叚 (temporary/false) on the right. "SUN (日) time that is TEMPORARY (仮) = FREE TIME before work starts!" 暇 (hima) = free, leisure.
# Kaishi 1.5k Mnemonics — Words 201–300

---

## 201. 時 (とき / toki) — time, occasion

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: toki)**
"TOE-KI — "TOE-KICK! Every TIME I stub my toe, I kick!" TOKI = time!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 時)**
時 has 日 (sun) on the left and 寺 (temple) on the right. The SUN (日) moves past the TEMPLE (寺) marking TIME! 時 (toki) = time, occasion.

---

## 202. 時 (じ / ji) — hour, o'clock

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: ji)**
"JEEP (ji) — "At what HOUR does the JEEP arrive?" JI = o'clock!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 時)**
Same kanji 時 (sun 日 + temple 寺). Read JI when telling the HOUR specifically — like a clock striking at the temple. 三時 (sanji) = 3 o'clock.

---

## 203. 一 (いち / ichi) — one

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: ichi)**
"ITCHY — "ONE itchy spot!" ICHI = ONE!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 一)**
一 is a single horizontal stroke — the simplest kanji! ONE line = ONE. 一 (ichi) = 1.

---

## 204. 二 (に / ni) — two

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: ni)**
"KNEE — "I have TWO knees!" NI = TWO!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 二)**
二 is TWO horizontal strokes — two lines stacked = TWO! 二 (ni) = 2.

---

## 205. 三 (さん / san) — three

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: san)**
"SUN — "THREE suns in the sky!" SAN = THREE!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 三)**
三 is THREE horizontal strokes — three lines stacked = THREE! 三 (san) = 3.

---

## 206. 四 (よん・し / yon・shi) — four

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: yon)**
"YAWN — "I YAWN four times!" YON = FOUR!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 四)**
四 is a box (囗) with two strokes inside — think of FOUR sides of a box with legs inside! 四 (yon/shi) = 4.

---

## 207. 五 (ご / go) — five

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: go)**
"GO — "GO! High FIVE!" GO = FIVE!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 五)**
五 has strokes that look like the number 5 tilted — a horizontal top, a diagonal, and a horizontal bottom. 五 (go) = 5.

---

## 208. 六 (ろく / roku) — six

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: roku)**
"ROCK — "SIX rocks in a pile!" ROKU = SIX!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 六)**
六 looks like a person with arms out (亠 top + two legs below) — SIX limbs if you count a tail! 六 (roku) = 6.

---

## 209. 七 (なな・しち / nana・shichi) — seven

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: nana)**
"NANA — "NANA means SEVEN!" Easy — it even sounds like SEVEN's cousin!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 七)**
七 looks like a cross/plus sign tilted — think of it as a lucky SEVEN! 七 (nana/shichi) = 7.

---

## 210. 八 (はち / hachi) — eight

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: hachi)**
"HATCHI (like Hachiko!) — "HACHIKO waited EIGHT years!" HACHI = EIGHT!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 八)**
八 looks like two lines spreading apart — like a wide EIGHT or two hands opening up! 八 (hachi) = 8.

---

## 211. 九 (きゅう / kyuu) — nine

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: kyuu)**
"CUTE (kyuu) — "NINE cute kittens!" KYUU = NINE!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 九)**
九 looks like a curve and a hook — like the number 9 curving around! 九 (kyuu) = 9.

---

## 212. 十 (じゅう / juu) — ten

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: juu)**
"JUICE — "TEN cartons of JUICE!" JUU = TEN!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 十)**
十 is a cross/plus sign — like tally marks reaching TEN, a completed cross! 十 (juu) = 10.

---

## 213. くん (くん / kun) — kun (honorific for males)

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: kun)**
"COON (raccoon!) — "RACCOON-KUN is my friend!" KUN = honorific for boys!"

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (くん)**
くん: く (open) + ん (closure). "OPENLY (く) addressing (ん) a BOY or YOUNG MAN!" Add KUN after a male name to show friendly respect.

---

## 214. すみません (すみません / sumimasen) — sorry, excuse me

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: sumimasen)**
"SUMI-MASEN: "SUE ME (sumi) MACE-EN (amazing)!" "Sue, me mace is amazing — SORRY I broke it!""

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 済む)**
Root verb is 済む (sumu = to finish/resolve). 浈 has 氵water + 青 blue. "The WATER (氵) turns BLUE (青) = the matter is RESOLVED. I'm SORRY!" すみません = excuse me.

---

## 215. やる (やる / yaru) — to do

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: yaru)**
"YA-ROO — "YA, ROO (kangaroo)! DO it!" YARU = to DO!"

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (やる)**
やる: や (do/question) + る (loop). A simple action verb — "DO it!" YARU is casual for "to do" (more casual than する).

---

## 216. 顔 (かお / kao) — face

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: kao)**
"COW — "A cow's FACE!" KAO = FACE!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 顔)**
顔 has 立 (stand) + 彡 (hair/design) + 頁 (head). "A STANDING (立) HEAD (頁) with HAIR (彡) = your FACE!" 顔 (kao) = face.

---

## 217. 今朝 (けさ / kesa) — this morning

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: kesa)**
"KEY-SA — "KEY to SAfe — I found it THIS MORNING!" KESA!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 今朝)**
今 = now. 朝 = morning (十 + 日 + 十 + 月 = sun rising between two horizons). "NOW (今) + MORNING (朝) = THIS MORNING!" 今朝 (kesa) = this morning.

---

## 218. 出る (でる / deru) — to go out, to leave

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: deru)**
"DAY-ROO — "The kangaroo goes OUT at DAY!" DERU = to go out!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 出)**
出 looks like a foot stepping OUT of a cave (凵). The stroke breaks through the container = GOING OUT! 出る (deru) = to leave.

---

## 219. そして (そして / soshite) — and, and then

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: soshite)**
"SO-SHI-TE — "SO SHE (shite) — AND THEN she left!" SOSHITE = and then!"

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (そして)**
そう (so) + し (and) + て (and then). "THAT WAY (そう) AND (し) THEN (て) = AND THEN!" そして connects events.

---

## 220. まだ (まだ / mada) — yet, more

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: mada)**
"MA-DA — "MY (ma) DAD (da) — he's STILL/YET here!" MADA = yet!"

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (まだ)**
まだ: ま (wait/still) + だ (bold). "STILL (ま) WAITING, BOLDLY (だ) = YET, MORE!" まだ = not yet, still.

---

## 221. じゃ (じゃ / ja) — then, well

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: ja)**
"JA — "JA-JA! THEN let's go!" JA = then, well..."

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (じゃ)**
じゃ: じ (ji) + ゃ (small ya). "JI + small YA = THEN!" A casual connector. JA = well, then...

---

## 222. ため (ため / tame) — sake, purpose

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: tame)**
"TA-ME — "TA-MALE! I study for the SAKE of my goal!" TAME = purpose!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of ため)**
ため can be written as 為 (do/for the sake of). 為 has a complex shape — think of a hand doing something for a PURPOSE. ため = for the sake of.

---

## 223. ご飯 (ごはん / gohan) — cooked rice, meal

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: gohan)**
"GO-HAN — "GO get some HAN (rice)! TIME TO EAT!" GOHAN = meal!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of ご飯)**
ご (honorific) + 飯 (meal/food). 飭 has 飠 (eat radical) + 反 (opposite/again). "HONORABLE (ご) FOOD (飯) = a MEAL of rice!" ご飯 = rice/meal.

---

## 224. ところ (ところ / tokoro) — place

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: tokoro)**
"TO-KO-RO — "TOE CALLS ROO — at what PLACE?" TOKORO = place!"

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (ところ)**
ところ can be written as 所 (place). 所 has 戸 (door) + 斤 (axe). "A DOOR (戸) where you CHOP (斤) wood = a PLACE!" ところ = place.

---

## 225. 少し (すこし / sukoshi) — a bit, a little while

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: sukoshi)**
"SU-KO-SHI — "SUE CALLS (ko) SHE (shi) — just a BIT!" SUKOSHI = a little!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 少)**
少 has 小 (small) with an extra stroke — SMALLER than small = just a LITTLE! 少し (sukoshi) = a little bit.

---

## 226. 見える (みえる / mieru) — be visible

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: mieru)**
"MI-E-RU — "MY (mi) EEL (e) ROO (ru) is VISIBLE!" MIERU!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 見)**
見 = eye (目) + legs (儿). "An EYE (目) with LEGS (儿) = something your eyes WALK TO — it's VISIBLE!" 見える = can be seen.

---

## 227. 違う (ちがう / chigau) — to differ, to be wrong

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: chigau)**
"CHI-GAU — "CHEESE (chi) GOWN (gau)! That's WRONG — I ordered a cheese GOWN!""

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 違)**
違 has 辶 (walk) + 韋 (tanned leather). "WALKING (辶) in a DIFFERENT DIRECTION with leather shoes = DIFFERENT, WRONG!" 違う = different.

---

## 228. 男 (おとこ / otoko) — man, male

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: otoko)**
"OH-TOE-CALL — "OH, TOE-CALL! That's what a MAN does!""

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 男)**
男 has 田 (rice field) on top and 力 (strength) below. A person with STRENGTH (力) working in the FIELD (田) = a MAN! 男 (otoko) = man.

---

## 229. ただ (ただ / tada) — free (of charge)

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: tada)**
"TA-DA — "TA-DA! It's FREE!" Magic trick reveal = FREE!"

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (ただ)**
ただ: た (past) + だ (bold). "In the PAST (た), it was BOLDLY (だ) given = FREE!" ただ = free, no charge.

---

## 230. ただ (ただ / tada) — simply, just

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: tada)**
"TA-DA — "It's SIMPLY ta-da! Nothing more!" JUST a magic trick!"

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (ただ)**
ただ: た (that) + だ (copula). "It is THAT (た) (だ) = SIMPLY that, JUST that." ただ = just, merely.

---

## 231. 人間 (にんげん / ningen) — human being, people

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: ningen)**
"NIN-GEN — "NINJA (nin) + GEN (like a generator)! A NINJA generator makes HUMANS!""

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 人間)**
人 = person. 間 = between (gate 門 + sun 日). "A PERSON (人) BETWEEN (間) gates = a HUMAN BEING walking through life!" 人間 = human.

---

## 232. 作る (つくる / tsukuru) — to make, to create

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: tsukuru)**
"TSU-KU-RU — "TSU (two) COOL (ku) ROO (kangaroo) — two cool kangaroos CREATE!""

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 作)**
作 has 亻(person) on the right and 乍 (suddenly) on the left. "A PERSON (亻) who SUDDENLY (乍) makes something = CREATING!" 作る (tsukuru) = to make.

---

## 233. 感じる (かんじる / kanjiru) — to feel, to sense

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: kanjiru)**
"KAN-JI-RU — "CAN (kan) GI (ji) ROO (ru) FEEL it!" KANJIRU!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 感)**
感 has 咸 (all) on top and 心 (heart) on the bottom. "ALL (咸) of your HEART (心) = FEELING everything!" 感じる = to feel.

---

## 234. 上 (うえ / ue) — up, above

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: ue)**
"OO-AY — "OO-AY! Look UP!" UE = above!"
