**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 上)**
上 has a horizontal base with a stroke going UP. Something sitting on a line going UPWARD = ABOVE! 上 (ue) = up.

---

## 235. 強い (つよい / tsuyoi) — strong

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: tsuyoi)**
"TSU-YO-I — "TSU (two) YO-YO (yo)! A two-yoyo person is STRONG!""

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 強)**
強 has 弓 (bow) on the left and 虫 (insect) on the right. "A BOW (弓) strong enough to shoot an INSECT (虫) = STRONG!" 強い (tsuyoi) = strong.

---

## 236. 出す (だす / dasu) — to take out

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: dasu)**
"DA-SU — "DA (the) SU ( Sue)! TAKE IT OUT, Sue!" DASU = to take out!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 出)**
出 = exit (foot leaving a cave). "A FOOT leaving a CAVE = TAKING OUT!" 出す (dasu) = to put out, take out.

---

## 237. 笑う (わらう / warau) — to laugh, to smile

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: warau)**
"WA-RA-U — "WAH (wa)! RAH (ra)! U (you)! WAH RAH — I'm LAUGHING!""

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 笑)**
笑 has 竹 (bamboo) on top and 夭 (young/early) below. "Under BAMBOO (竹), a YOUNG (夭) person LAUGHS as bamboo tickles them!" 笑う (warau) = to laugh.

---

## 238. 毎月 (まいつき / maitsuki) — every month

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: maitsuki)**
"MAI-TSU-KI — "MY (mai) TSU (two) KEY (ki)! Every month I get two keys!""

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 毎月)**
毎 = every (see #17). 月 = month (moon). "EVERY (毎) MOON (月) = EVERY MONTH!" 毎月 = every month.

---

## 239. 毎年 (まいとし / maitoshi) — every year

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: maitoshi)**
"MAI-TO-SHI — "MY (mai) TOE (to) SHE (shi) — every year my toe sees her!""

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 毎年)**
毎 = every. 年 = year (looks like a person carrying harvest). "EVERY (毎) HARVEST (年) = EVERY YEAR!" 毎年 = annually.

---

## 240. 多く (おおく / ooku) — many, much

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: ooku)**
"OO-KU — "OOH, COOL (ku)! There are MANY!" OOKU = many!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 多)**
多 has 夕 (evening) stacked twice. "EVENING (夕) after EVENING (夕) = MANY days!" 多く = many, a lot.

---

## 241. くらい (くらい / kurai) — approximately, about

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: kurai)**
"KU-RAI — "COOL (ku) RYE (rai) bread — ABOUT that much!" KURAI!"

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (くらい)**
くらい can be written as 位 (degree/approximate). 位 has 亻(person) + 立 (stand). "A PERSON (亻) STANDING (立) at a certain POINT = ABOUT that level." くらい = approximately.

---

## 242. 姉 (あね / ane) — older sister

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: ane)**
"A-NE — "AH (a) NE (knee)! My OLDER SISTER hurt her knee!" ANE = big sis!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 姉)**
姉 has 女 (woman) on the left and 市 (city/market) on the right. "A WOMAN (女) who runs the CITY MARKET (市) = the OLDER SISTER in charge!" 姉 (ane) = older sister.

---

## 243. 弟 (おとうと / otouto) — younger brother

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: otouto)**
"OH-TOE-TOE-TOE — "OH, TOE TOE TOE! My little brother keeps tripping over his toes!""

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 弟)**
弟 has 弓 (bow) with strokes suggesting someone smaller below. Think of a YOUNG boy playing with a BOW — the little bro! 弟 (otouto) = younger brother.

---

## 244. 妹 (いもうと / imouto) — younger sister

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: imouto)**
"EE-MO-U-TO — "EE MOO (cow) TO (too)! My little sister goes EE MOO too!""

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 妹)**
妹 has 女 (woman) on the left and 未 (not yet) on the right. "A WOMAN (女) who is NOT YET (未) grown up = YOUNGER SISTER!" 妹 (imouto) = little sis.

---

## 245. 力 (ちから / chikara) — strength, power

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: chikara)**
"CHI-KA-RA — "CHEESE (chi) CAR (ka) RA (ra)! My car has the POWER of CHEESE!""

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 力)**
力 looks like a MUSCULAR ARM flexing! The bent stroke is the bicep, the hook is the fist. 力 (chikara) = strength!

---

## 246. 世界 (せかい / sekai) — world

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: sekai)**
"SE-KAI — "SAY (se) COW (kai) — the WORLD says MOO!" SEKAI = world!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 世界)**
界 = boundary (田 field + 介 between). 世 = generation/world. "BOUNDARIES (界) between GENERATIONS (世) = the WORLD!" 世界 = the whole world.

---

## 247. 相手 (あいて / aite) — partner, opponent

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: aite)**
"A-I-TE — "EYE (ai) TEA (te)! My PARTNER drinks tea with me!" AITE!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 相手)**
相 = mutual (木 tree + 目 eye — looking at each other). 手 = hand. "MUTUALLY (相) shaking HANDS (手) = a PARTNER or OPPONENT!" 相手 = the other party.

---

## 248. 姿 (すがた / sugata) — figure, appearance

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: sugata)**
"SU-GA-TA — "SUE (su) GOT (ga) A (ta) FIGURE!" SUGATA = figure!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 姿)**
姿 has 次 (next) on top and 女 (woman) below. "NEXT (次) to a WOMAN (女) you notice her APPEARANCE!" 姿 = figure, form.

---

## 249. 死ぬ (しぬ / shinu) — to die

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: shinu)**
"SHI-NU — "SHE (shi) KNEW (nu) she would DIE!" SHINU!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 死)**
死 has 歹 (death/bones) on the left and 匕 (spoon/dagger) on the right. "BONES (歹) and a DAGGER (匕) = DEATH!" 死ぬ (shinu) = to die.

---

## 250. 早い (はやい / hayai) — early

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: hayai)**
"HAYAI — "HIGH (ha) YAI (like ya-i)! I got UP HIGH early!" HAYAI = early!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 早)**
早 has 日 (sun) on top and 十 (ten) below. "The SUN (日) at TEN (十) o'clock = EARLY morning!" 早い (hayai) = early.

---

## 251. 早い (はやい / hayai) — fast (in terms of time)

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: hayai)**
"HAYAI — "That was FAST! HAYAI — the time flew by!""

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 早)**
Same kanji 早. EARLY and FAST are related — if you're EARLY, you're moving FAST! 早い = fast, quick.

---

## 252. 頭 (あたま / atama) — head

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: atama)**
"A-TA-MA — "A (a) TOMATO (ta-ma)! A tomato on my HEAD!" ATAMA = head!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 頭)**
頭 has 豆 (bean) on the left and 頁 (head) on the right. "A BEAN (豆) for a HEAD (頁) = your HEAD!" 頭 (atama) = head.

---

## 253. 戻る (もどる / modoru) — to return, to go back

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: modoru)**
"MO-DO-RU — "MO (more) DOOR (do) ROO (kangaroo)! The kangaroo RETURNS through the door!""

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 戻)**
戻 has 戸 (door) on the left and 大 (big) on the right. "A BIG (大) DOOR (戸) you GO BACK through!" 戻る (modoru) = to return.

---

## 254. ずっと (ずっと / zutto) — continuously, the whole time

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: zutto)**
"ZU-TTO — "ZOOM (zu) TTO (tight)! ZOOMING continuously the WHOLE TIME!""

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (ずっと)**
ずっと: ず (continuously) + っ (emphasis) + と (along). "CONTINUOUSLY (ず) with EMPHASIS (っ) ALONG (と) = the WHOLE TIME!" ずっと = always, continuously.

---

## 255. どうして (どうして / doushite) — why

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: doushite)**
"DO-U-SHI-TE — "DO (question) YOU (u) SHE (shi) TEA (te)? WHY does she drink tea?""

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (どうして)**
どう (how/why) + して (doing). "HOW (どう) ARE YOU DOING (して) it? = WHY?" どうして = why, for what reason.

---

## 256. やっぱり (やっぱり / yappari) — just as I thought, after all

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: yappari)**
"YA-P-PA-RI — "YA! PPA (pizza)! RI (read)! "YA, pizza! I THOUGHT so — after ALL!""

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (やっぱり)**
やはり (same meaning) with emphasis っ. や (do) + ぱ (bold/happening) + り (reason). "DOING (や) BOLDLY (ぱ) for a REASON (り) = as EXPECTED, AFTER ALL!" やっぱり = as I thought.

---

## 257. つもり (つもり / tsumori) — intention, purpose

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: tsumori)**
"TSU-MO-RI — "TSU (two) MO (more) READ (ri)! My INTENTION is to READ more!""

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (つもり)**
つもり can be written as 積もる (to pile up). "THOUGHTS PILING UP (つもり) = your INTENTION!" つもり = intention, plan.

---

## 258. 必要 (ひつよう / hitsuyou) — necessary, needed

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: hitsuyou)**
"HI-TSU-YO-U — "HI! TSU (two) YO-YO (you)! TWO yo-yos are NECESSARY!""

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 必要)**
必 = certain/must (heart 心 with a slash through it — your HEART says it's a MUST). 要 = important (西 west + 女 woman). "It's a MUST (必) and IMPORTANT (要) = NECESSARY!" 必要 = needed.

---

## 259. 終わる (おわる / owaru) — to finish, to end

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: owaru)**
"O-WA-RU — "OH (o) WAR (wa) RULES (ru)! The war ENDS!" OWARU!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 終)**
終 has 糸 (thread) on the left and 冬 (winter) on the right. "The THREAD (糸) reaches WINTER (冬) = the end of the cycle!" 終わる = to end.

---

## 260. 者 (もの / mono) — person (suffix)

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: mono)**
"MO-NO — "MO' NO THING? It's a PERSON!" MONO = person who..."

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 者)**
者 has 耂 (old) on top and 日 (sun) below. "An OLD (老) person in the SUN (日) = an ELDER PERSON!" 者 = person who does something (suffix).

---

## 261. 後 (あと / ato) — after

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: ato)**
"A-TOE — "AFTER I stub my TOE!" ATO = after!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 後)**
後 has 彳 (step) and 攵 (hand) elements. "STEPS (彳) taken AFTER (後) someone — you follow BEHIND!" 後 (ato) = after, behind.

---

## 262. こちら (こちら / kochira) — here, this way

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: kochira)**
"KO-CHI-RA — "CALL (ko) CHEESE (chi) RA (ra)! Say cheese — look THIS WAY!" KOCHIRA!"

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (こちら)**
こ (this) + ち (chi) + ら (plural). "THIS (こ) direction, over HERE (ちら)!" More polite than ここ. こちら = this way.

---

## 263. 向かう (むかう / mukau) — to face, to head toward

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: mukau)**
"MU-KA-U — "MOOSE (mu) CAR (ka) YOU (u)! The moose HEADS TOWARD your car!""

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 向)**
向 has a box with an opening — like a WINDOW you FACE toward. "A WINDOW (向) you look through = FACING, HEADING TOWARD!" 向かう (mukau) = to face.

---

## 264. きっと (きっと / kitto) — surely, certainly

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: kitto)**
"KI-TTO — "KEY (ki) TTO (tight)! I CERTAINLY have the KEY!" KITTO!"

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (きっと)**
きっと: き (certain) + っ (emphasis) + と (with). "CERTAIN (き) with EMPHASIS (っ) = SURELY, CERTAINLY!" きっと = surely.

---

## 265. 変わる (かわる / kawaru) — to change

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: kawaru)**
"KA-WA-RU — "CAR (ka) WAR (wa) RULES (ru)! Cars CHANGE during war!""

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 変)**
変 has X marks and 夂 (go). Things getting CROSSED OUT and MOVING = CHANGING! 変わる (kawaru) = to change (intransitive).

---

## 266. 凄い (すごい / sugoi) — wonderful, great, a lot

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: sugoi)**
"SU-GO-I — "SUE (su) GO (go) I! Sue says GO — it's AMAZING!""

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 凄)**
凄 has 冫(ice) on the left and 妻 (wife) on the right. "ICE (冫) cold WIFE (妻) = so WONDERFUL she gives you chills!" 凄い (sugoi) = amazing!

---

## 267. 女 (おんな / onna) — woman, girl

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: onna)**
"ON-NA — "ON a NA-pkin? That's what a GIRL does!" ONNA = woman!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 女)**
女 looks like a woman KNEELING with arms crossed. A pictograph of a seated, graceful WOMAN. 女 (onna) = woman.

---

## 268. ちゃんと (ちゃんと / chanto) — properly

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: chanto)**
"CHAN-TO — "CHAN (chan) TO (to)! Do it PROPERLY for CHAN!" CHANTO = properly!"

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (ちゃんと)**
ちゃん (proper/correct) + と (manner). "CORRECTLY (ちゃん) in that MANNER (と) = PROPERLY!" ちゃんと = properly, correctly.

---

## 269. 音 (おと / oto) — sound

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: oto)**
"OH-TO — "OH! TOE! What a SOUND!" OTO = sound!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 音)**
音 has 立 (stand) on top and 日 (sun) below. "Something STANDING (立) in the SUN (日) makes a SOUND!" 音 (oto) = sound.

---

## 270. 体 (からだ / karada) — body

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: karada)**
"KA-RA-DA — "CAR (ka) RA (ra) DANCE (da)! My BODY dances!" KARADA = body!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 体)**
体 has 亻(person) on the left and 本 (origin/root) on the right. "A PERSON's (亻) ROOT/ORIGIN (本) = their BODY!" 体 (karada) = body.

---

## 271. 掛ける (かける / kakeru) — to hang, to put on

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: kakeru)**
"KA-KE-RU — "CAR (ka) KEY (ke) ROO (ru)! HANG the keys on the car!" KAKERU!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 掛)**
掛 has 扌(hand) on the left and 卦 (divination/hexagram) on the right. "Using your HAND (扌) to HANG something like a fortune hexagram!" 掛ける = to hang, put on.

---

## 272. 歩く (あるく / aruku) — to walk, to go on foot

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: aruku)**
"A-RU-KU — "A (a) RULE (ru) COOL (ku)! WALKING is a cool rule!""

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 歩)**
歩 has 止 (stop) on top and 少 (few) below. "STOP (止) a FEW (少) steps = WALKING!" 歩く (aruku) = to walk.

---

## 273. 存在 (そんざい / sonzai) — existence, being

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: sonzai)**
"SON-ZAI — "SON (sun) ZAI (die)! The sun's EXISTENCE never DIES!" SONZAI!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 存在)**
存 = exist/consider (才 talent + 子 child — a talented child EXISTS). 在 = exist (才 + 土 earth). "TALENTED (存) on EARTH (在) = EXISTING!" 存在 = existence.

---

## 274. 過ぎる (すぎる / sugiru) — to exceed, to pass by

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: sugiru)**
"SU-GI-RU — "SUE (su) GI (ji) ROO (ru) — the kangaroo EXCEEDS the limit!""

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 過)**
過 has 辶 (walk) and 咼 (wrong/hole). "WALKING (辶) past a HOLE (咼) = EXCEEDING, going TOO FAR!" 過ぎる = to exceed.

---

## 275. 聞こえる (きこえる / kikoeru) — to be able to hear

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: kikoeru)**
"KI-KO-E-RU — "KEY (ki) CALL (ko) EEL (e) ROO — I CAN HEAR the eel-kangaroo!""

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 聞)**
聞 = gate (門) + ear (耳). "An EAR (耳) at a GATE (門) = you CAN HEAR what's inside!" 聞こえる = to be audible.

---

## 276. 受ける (うける / ukeru) — to receive, to accept

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: ukeru)**
"U-KE-RU — "YOU (u) KEY (ke) ROO (ru) — the kangaroo ACCEPTS your key!""

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 受)**
受 has 爪 (claw) on top and 又 (again) below. "A CLAW (爪) grabbing AGAIN (又) = RECEIVING!" 受ける (ukeru) = to accept, receive.

---

## 277. 今年 (ことし / kotoshi) — this year

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: kotoshi)**
"KO-TO-SHI — "CALL (ko) TOM'S (to) SHE (shi) — she's here THIS YEAR!""

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 今年)**
今 = now. 年 = year. "NOW (今) + YEAR (年) = THIS YEAR!" 今年 (kotoshi) = this year.

---

## 278. 去年 (きょねん / kyonen) — last year

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: kyonen)**
"KYO-NE — "KEY-O (kyo) NO! LAST YEAR, no!" KYONEN = last year!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 去年)**
去 = go/leave (土 earth + 厶 private — leaving private ground). 年 = year. "LEFT (去) + YEAR (年) = the year that LEFT = LAST YEAR!" 去年 = last year.

---

## 279. 来年 (らいねん / rainen) — next year

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: rainen)**
"RAI-NE — "RYE (rai) KNEE (ne)! NEXT YEAR I'll plant rye on my knee!""

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 来年)**
来 = come (arrows pointing at a tree). 年 = year. "COMING (来) + YEAR (年) = the year COMING = NEXT YEAR!" 来年 = next year.

---

## 280. 殺す (ころす / korosu) — to kill

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: korosu)**
"KO-RO-SU — "CALL (ko) ROSE (ro) SUE (su)! Call Rose to kill Sue!" Dark but memorable!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 殺)**
殺 has 乂 (cutting) and 殳 (weapon/lance). "CUTTING (乂) with a WEAPON (殳) = KILLING!" 殺す (korosu) = to kill.

---

## 281. まさか (まさか / masaka) — by no means, never!

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: masaka)**
"MA-SA-KA — "MY (ma) SOCKS (sa-ka)?! BY NO MEANS did I wear those!""

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (まさか)**
まさか: ま (wait) + さ (past) + か (question). "WAIT (ま), in the PAST (さ), QUESTION (か)?! = NEVER! NO WAY!" まさか = you don't mean...!

---

## 282. 絶対 (ぜったい / zettai) — absolutely

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: zettai)**
"ZET-TAI — "ZOMBIE (ze) TIE (tai)! I ABSOLUTELY need a zombie tie!""

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 絶対)**
絶 = cut off (糸 thread + 刀 knife — cutting the thread completely). 対 = opposite/pair. "CUT OFF (絶) all OPPOSITES (対) = ABSOLUTELY!" No compromise! 絶対 = absolutely.

---

## 283. 理由 (りゆう / riyuu) — reason, cause

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: riyuu)**
"RI-YU-U — "READ (ri) YOU (yu) U (you)! READ the REASON, you!""

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 理由)**
理 = reason (王 king + 里 village — the king's logic). 由 = from/by reason of (田 field with a line). "LOGIC (理) from (由) the field = REASON!" 理由 = cause, reason.

---

## 284. 口 (くち / kuchi) — mouth

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: kuchi)**
"COO-CHI — "COO-CHI like a baby! A baby's MOUTH!" KUCHI = mouth!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 口)**
口 is a simple BOX shape — an OPEN MOUTH! The simplest kanji for a body part. 口 (kuchi) = mouth.

---

## 285. 外 (そと / soto) — outside

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: soto)**
"SO-TO — "SO TO-tally OUTSIDE!" SOTO = outside!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 外)**
外 has 夕 (evening) on the left and 卜 (divination) on the right. "EVENING (夕) DIVINATION (卜) happens OUTSIDE under the stars!" 外 (soto) = outside.

---

## 286. 逃げる (にげる / nigeru) — to escape, to run away

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: nigeru)**
"NI-GE-RU — "NEAR (ni) GATE (ge) ROO (ru)! Escape NEAR the gate!""

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 逃)**
逃 has 辶 (walk/escape) and 兆 (omen/sign). "FOLLOWING (辶) an OMEN (兆) to ESCAPE!" 逃げる (nigeru) = to flee.

---

## 287. 誰か (だれか / dareka) — someone

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: dareka)**
"DA-RE-KA — "DARE (da) RE (ray) CAR (ka)! SOMEONE dared Ray's car!""

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (誰か)**
誰 = who (see #130) + か (question/someone). "WHO (誰) + SOME (か) = SOMEONE!" 誰か = someone, somebody.

---

## 288. まるで (まるで / marude) — just like, completely

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: marude)**
"MA-RU-DE — "MY (ma) RULE (ru) DAY (de)! My rule day is JUST LIKE a holiday!""

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (まるで)**
まるで: ま (round/full) + る (loop) + で (at/in). "FULLY (まる) LOOPING (で) = COMPLETELY, JUST LIKE!" まるで = as if, just like.

---

## 289. 他 (ほか / hoka) — another, somewhere else

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: hoka)**
"HO-KA — "HO-BO (hoka) — the hobo goes SOMEWHERE ELSE!" HOKA = another place!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 他)**
他 has 亻(person) on the left and 也 (also) on the right. "ANOTHER (也) PERSON (亻) = someone ELSE!" 他 (hoka) = elsewhere, another.

---

## 290. 他 (た / ta) — other (people, abstract)

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: ta)**
"TA — "TA-DA! OTHER people appeared!" TA = others!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 他)**
Same kanji 他, different reading. When meaning "OTHER people/things," read TA. "Other PEOPLE (他 = 亻+ 也)" 他 (ta) = others.

---

## 291. 問題 (もんだい / mondai) — problem, question

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: mondai)**
"MON-DAI — "MONSTER (mon) DAI (die)! The monster problem must DIE!""

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 問題)**
問 = question (門 gate + 口 mouth — asking at a gate). 題 = topic/subject (是 correct + 頁 page). "ASKING (問) at a PAGE (題) = a PROBLEM to solve!" 問題 = problem.

---

## 292. 今後 (こんご / kongo) — after this, in the future

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: kongo)**
"KON-GO — "CON (trick) GO! AFTER THIS, I'll CON you into going!" KONGO!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 今後)**
今 = now. 後 = after. "NOW (今) + AFTER (後) = FROM NOW ON!" 今後 = in the future, henceforth.

---

## 293. 普通 (ふつう / futsuu) — normal, regular, average

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: futsuu)**
"FU-TSU-U — "FOOT (fu) TSU (two) YOU (u)! Two foots is NORMAL for you!" (Think: you have two feet!)"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 普通)**
普 = ordinary (並 common + 日 sun — COMMON as DAY). 通 = through/pass (辶 walk + 甬 passage). "COMMONLY (普) PASSING THROUGH (通) = NORMAL!" 普通 = ordinary.

---

## 294. 心 (こころ / kokoro) — heart, mind

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: kokoro)**
"KO-KO-RO — "CALL (ko) CAL (ko) RO (ro)! My HEART calls!" KOKORO = heart!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 心)**
心 looks like a HEART with three drops — the three dots are chambers/blood drops! A simple pictograph of a HEART. 心 (kokoro) = heart, mind.

---

## 295. 春 (はる / haru) — spring

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: haru)**
"HA-RU — "HA! ROO (kangaroo)! SPRING is here!" HARU = spring!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 春)**
春 has 三 (three) + 人 (person) + 日 (sun). "THREE PEOPLE (三人) in the SUN (日) = SPRING!" Three people warming in the spring sun.

---

## 296. 夏 (なつ / natsu) — summer

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: natsu)**
"NA-TSU — "NAUGHTY (na-tsu)! Be NAUGHTY in SUMMER!" NATSU!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 夏)**
夏 has a head on top and 夊 (slow walk) below. "A HEAD on top with SLOW STEPS (夏) = walking slowly in the SUMMER HEAT!" 夏 = summer.

---

## 297. 秋 (あき / aki) — fall/autumn

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: aki)**
"A-KI — "A KEY (ki) falls in AUTUMN!" AKI = fall!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 秋)**
秋 has 禾 (grain) on the left and 火 (fire) on the right. "GRAIN (禾) burning like FIRE (火) = AUTUMN leaves!" 秋 = autumn.

---

## 298. 冬 (ふゆ / fuyu) — winter

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: fuyu)**
"FU-YU — "FUR (fu) YOU (yu)! Wear FUR in WINTER!" FUYU!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 冬)**
冬 has 夂 (go/stop) on top and 冫(ice) below. "STOPPING (冬) in ICE (冫) = WINTER!" 冬 = winter.

---

## 299. 以上 (いじょう / ijou) — ...or more, beyond

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: ijou)**
"I-JOU — "I (i) JOU (joe) — I go BEYOND joe!" IJOU = more than!"

**Kanji Mnemonic (Visual Shape of 以上)**
以 = by means of. 上 = above/up. "BY MEANS OF going UP (上) = BEYOND, OR MORE!" 以上 = that or more.

---

## 300. それで (それで / sorede) — then, so

**Pronunciation Mnemonic (Sound: sorede)**
"SO-RE-DE — "SO (so) RAY (re) DAY (de)! SO then, Ray's DAY came — AND SO it happened!""

**Kanji/Shape Mnemonic (それで)**
それ (that) + で (by means of/at). "THAT (それ) + BY MEANS OF (で) = THEN, SO (consequence)!" それで = and so, then.
# Kaishi 1.5k Mnemonics — Words 301–400

---

## 301. 生きる (いきる / ikiru) — to live, to exist
**Pronunciation:** "EE-KI-RU — I (ee) KEY (ki) ROO (kangaroo)! I LIVE for the kangaroo's key!"
**Kanji:** 生 looks like a PLANT SPROUTING from the ground — new LIFE! 生きる = to live.

## 302. 最初 (さいしょ / saisho) — first, beginning
**Pronunciation:** "SAI-SHO — SIDE SHOW! The FIRST act is a side show!"
**Kanji:** 最 = most/utmost. 初 = first (衤clothing + 刀 knife — cutting cloth for the FIRST time). "The MOST FIRST = THE BEGINNING!"

## 303. 最後 (さいご / saigo) — last, final
**Pronunciation:** "SAI-GO — SAI (sigh) GO! The LAST sigh before I go!"
**Kanji:** 最 = most. 後 = after/behind. "MOST AFTER = the FINAL, LAST one!"

## 304. 日 (ひ / hi) — day, sun
**Pronunciation:** "HE — HE shines every DAY!"
**Kanji:** 日 is a RECTANGLE with a line in the middle — the SUN seen through a window!

## 305. 間 (あいだ / aida) — interval, between
**Pronunciation:** "A-I-DA — EYE (ai) DA! The EYE sees BETWEEN them!"
**Kanji:** 門 (gate) + 日 (sun) inside = "The SUN between GATES = an INTERVAL!"

## 306. 間 (ま / ma) — interval, pause
**Pronunciation:** "MA — MA! A PAUSE! A moment of MA!"
**Kanji:** Same kanji 間. Read MA for a pause/timing.

## 307. について (について / ni tsuite) — concerning, regarding
**Pronunciation:** "NI-TSU-ITE — NEAR two EATERS! CONCERNING two eaters!"
**Kanji:** に (to/at) + ついて (following). "FOLLOWING a topic AT = CONCERNING!"

## 308. 左 (ひだり / hidari) — left
**Pronunciation:** "HI-DA-RI — HE DA READS on the LEFT side!"
**Kanji:** 左 has 𠂇 (hand) + 工 (work). "Your LEFT HAND does the WORK!"

## 309. 右 (みぎ / migi) — right
**Pronunciation:** "MI-GI — MY GEE! MY RIGHT hand, GEE whiz!"
**Kanji:** 右 has 𠂇 (hand) + 口 (mouth). "Your RIGHT hand feeds your MOUTH!"

## 310. 感じ (かんじ / kanji) — feeling, impression
**Pronunciation:** "KANJI — It's a KANJI! I have a FEELING about this!"
**Kanji:** 咸 (all) + 心 (heart). "ALL the HEART = a FEELING!"

## 311. 胸 (むね / mune) — chest
**Pronunciation:** "MU-NE — MOOSE KNEE hit my CHEST!"
**Kanji:** 月 (body) + 匈 (chest/box). "BODY PART shaped like a BOX = your CHEST!"

## 312. 表情 (ひょうじょう / hyoujou) — expression, look
**Pronunciation:** "HYOU-JOU — HEY YOU JOKE! That EXPRESSION is a joke!"
**Kanji:** 表 = surface. 情 = feeling. "SURFACE FEELING = your FACIAL EXPRESSION!"

## 313. 多い (おおい / ooi) — a lot, many
**Pronunciation:** "OO-I — OO-WEE! That's a LOT!"
**Kanji:** 夕 (evening) stacked twice = EVENING after EVENING = MANY days!

## 314. 初めて (はじめて / hajimete) — for the first time
**Pronunciation:** "HA-JI-ME-TE — HAZE ME TEA for the FIRST TIME!"
**Kanji:** 初 = first (衤clothing + 刀 knife — cutting cloth FIRST TIME).

## 315. 無理 (むり / muri) — unreasonable, impossible
**Pronunciation:** "MU-RI — MOO READ! A cow reading? IMPOSSIBLE!"
**Kanji:** 無 = nothing/not. 理 = reason. "NO REASON = IMPOSSIBLE!"

## 316. 欲しい (ほしい / hoshii) — wanting, wishing for
**Pronunciation:** "HO-SHII — HO! SHE! I WANT her!"
**Kanji:** 谷 (valley) + 欠 (lack). "A VALLEY of LACK = you WANT to fill it!"

## 317. ごめん (ごめん / gomen) — sorry
**Pronunciation:** "GO-MEN — GO MEN! SORRY, go find the men!"
**Kanji:** ご (honorific) + めん (face). "HUMBLY bowing my FACE = SORRY!"

## 318. 覚える (おぼえる / oboeru) — to memorize
**Pronunciation:** "O-BO-E-RU — OH BALL EEL ROO! I MEMORIZE it!"
**Kanji:** 育 (grow) + 見 (see). "GROWING INSIGHT = MEMORIZING!"

## 319. 別に (べつに / betsu ni) — (not) particularly
**Pronunciation:** "BE-TSU-NI — BET two knees — NOT PARTICULARLY!"
**Kanji:** 另 (separate) + 刂(knife). "SEPARATED = NOT PARTICULARLY related!"

## 320. 思い出す (おもいだす / omoidasu) — to recollect, to recall
**Pronunciation:** "O-MOI-DA-SU — OH MY DAUGHTER SUE! I RECALL her!"
**Kanji:** 思 (think) + 出 (come out). "THOUGHTS COMING OUT = RECALLING!"

## 321. 入れる (いれる / ireru) — to put in
**Pronunciation:** "I-RE-RU — I PUT IN a ray of kangaroo!"
**Kanji:** 入 = enter (two strokes going inward). "PUTTING IN = entering!"

## 322. 開く (あく / aku) — to open (intransitive)
**Pronunciation:** "AH-KU — AH, COOL! The door OPENS!"
**Kanji:** 門 (gate) + 开 (open). "A GATE that OPENS!"

## 323. 開く (ひらく / hiraku) — to open (transitive)
**Pronunciation:** "HI-RA-KU — HE RA COOL! HE opens the cool door!"
**Kanji:** Same kanji 開. Read HIRAKU when YOU open something (event, ceremony).

## 324. 残る (のこる / nokoru) — to remain, to be left over
**Pronunciation:** "NO-KO-RU — NO one calls — REMAINS!"
**Kanji:** 歹 (death) + 戋 (spear). "After DEATH, what REMAINS is your legacy!"

## 325. 答える (こたえる / kotaeru) — to answer
**Pronunciation:** "KO-TA-E-RU — CALL TAIL EEL ROO! The eel-kangaroo ANSWERS!"
**Kanji:** 竹 (bamboo) + 合 (join/fit). "FITTING together = ANSWERING!"

## 326. 怖い (こわい / kowai) — fearful, scary
**Pronunciation:** "KO-WAI — CALL WHY — because it's SCARY!"
**Kanji:** 忄(heart) + 布 (cloth). "Your HEART COVERED in FEAR = SCARY!"

## 327. 年 (ねん / nen) — counter for years
**Pronunciation:** "NE — HEY! How many YEARS?"
**Kanji:** 年 looks like a person carrying harvest — marking PASSING YEARS!

## 328. 年 (とし / toshi) — year, age
**Pronunciation:** "TOE-SHE — My TOE is ONE YEAR old!"
**Kanji:** Same kanji 年. Read TOSHI for age.

## 329. 取る (とる / toru) — to take, to get
**Pronunciation:** "TO-ROO — I TOOK the kangaroo!"
**Kanji:** 耳 (ear) + 又 (hand). "HAND grabbing by the EAR = TAKING!"

## 330. 全て (すべて / subete) — all, entirely
**Pronunciation:** "SU-BE-TE — SUE bets ALL the tea!"
**Kanji:** 全 (whole) + て (hand). "THE WHOLE thing in your HAND = ENTIRELY!"

## 331. 助ける (たすける / tasukeru) — to help, to save
**Pronunciation:** "TA-SU-KE-RU — TIE SUKE ROO! SUKE the kangaroo HELPS!"
**Kanji:** 且 (moreover) + 力 (power). "MOREOVER with POWER = STRENGTH to HELP!"

## 332. 状況 (じょうきょう / joukyou) — state of affairs
**Pronunciation:** "JOU-KYOU — JOE CUTE! The STATE of affairs is cute!"
**Kanji:** 状 = form/condition. 況 = situation. "FORM of the SITUATION = STATE OF AFFAIRS!"

## 333. 確認 (かくにん / kakunin) — confirmation
**Pronunciation:** "KA-KU-NIN — CAR COOL NINJA! CONFIRM the ninja!"
**Kanji:** 確 = certain. 認 = recognize. "CERTAINLY RECOGNIZING = CONFIRMATION!"

## 334. 消える (きえる / kieru) — to go out, to disappear
**Pronunciation:** "KI-E-RU — KEY EEL ROO DISAPPEARED!"
**Kanji:** 氵(water) + 肖 (small/decline). "WATER making things SMALLER = DISAPPEARING!"

## 335. 長い (ながい / nagai) — long
**Pronunciation:** "NA-GA-I — NOW GO I — SO LONG I could go!"
**Kanji:** Think of a STRETCHED OUT shape = LONG!

## 336. 驚く (おどろく / odoroku) — to be surprised
**Pronunciation:** "O-DO-RO-KU — OH DOOR ROO! The door SURPRISES me!"
**Kanji:** 敬 (respect) + 馬 (horse). "A RESPECTFUL HORSE bowing = SURPRISING!"

## 337. 腕 (うで / ude) — arm
**Pronunciation:** "OO-DE — OO, DAY! I stretch my ARM in the DAYlight!"
**Kanji:** 月 (body) + 宛 (reach). "BODY PART that REACHES = ARM!"

## 338. 以外 (いがい / igai) — except for
**Pronunciation:** "I-GAI — I except THAT guy!"
**Kanji:** 以 = by means of. 外 = outside. "OUTSIDE of what's INSIDE = EXCEPT FOR!"

## 339. 困る (こまる / komaru) — to be at a loss, troubled
**Pronunciation:** "KO-MA-RU — I lost my marble — I'm TROUBLED!"
**Kanji:** 囗 (enclosure) + 木 (tree). "TREE TRAPPED in a BOX = IN TROUBLE!"

## 340. 離れる (はなれる / hanareru) — to be separated
**Pronunciation:** "HA-NA-RE-RU — HAZELNUT SEPARATES from Ray's kangaroo!"
**Kanji:** 隹 (bird) + 离 (separation). "BIRD taking flight = SEPARATING!"

## 341. 気 (き / ki) — spirit, temperament
**Pronunciation:** "KEY — I have the KEY! That's my SPIRIT!"
**Kanji:** 气 (steam/air) with energy inside = SPIRIT!

## 342. 書く (かく / kaku) — to write
**Pronunciation:** "KA-KU — CAR COOL! I WRITE about a cool car!"
**Kanji:** 聿 (brush) + 日 (day). "BRUSH on a DAY = WRITING!"

## 343. 光 (ひかり / hikari) — light, flash
**Pronunciation:** "HI-KA-RI — HE CAR READ by LIGHT!"
**Kanji:** Small rays on top + legs below = LIGHT rays running!

## 344. 走る (はしる / hashiru) — to run
**Pronunciation:** "HA-SHI-RU — HASH SHE RUNS!"
**Kanji:** 土 (earth) + legs below. "EARTH with LEGS = RUNNING!"

## 345. 戦う (たたかう / tatakau) — to wage war, to fight
**Pronunciation:** "TA-TA-KA-U — BYE BYE to your car — we FIGHT!"
**Kanji:** 単 (single) + 戈 (halberd weapon). "SINGLE WEAPON = FIGHTING!"

## 346. 余り (あまり / amari) — remainder, leftover
**Pronunciation:** "AMARI — AMARETTO leftover! REMAINDER of my drink!"
**Kanji:** 余 = surplus. "SURPLUS shown = LEFTOVER!"

## 347. 全部 (ぜんぶ / zenbu) — all, everything
**Pronunciation:** "ZEN-BU — ZEN BUDDHA has EVERYTHING!"
**Kanji:** 全 (whole) + 部 (section). "WHOLE SECTION = EVERYTHING!"

## 348. 瞬間 (しゅんかん / shunkan) — instant, moment
**Pronunciation:** "SHUN-KAN — SHUN CAN do it in an INSTANT!"
**Kanji:** 瞬 = blink (eye dancing). 間 = interval. "BLINK of an INTERVAL = INSTANT!"

## 349. 説明 (せつめい / setsumei) — explanation
**Pronunciation:** "SETSU-MEI — SETS MAY need EXPLANATION!"
**Kanji:** 説 = explain (words). 明 = bright (sun + moon). "WORDS making things BRIGHT = EXPLANATION!"

## 350. 決める (きめる / kimeru) — to decide
**Pronunciation:** "KI-ME-RU — KEY ME ROO! The kangaroo and I DECIDE!"
**Kanji:** 氵(water) + 夬 (decisive). "WATER making a DECISIVE cut = DECIDING!"

## 351. 女性 (じょせい / josei) — woman, female
**Pronunciation:** "JO-SEI — JOE'S (jou) SAY (sei) — Joe says WOMAN!"
**Kanji:** 女 = woman. 性 = gender/nature. "WOMAN's GENDER = FEMALE!"

## 352. 頂く (いただく / itadaku) — to receive (humble)
**Pronunciation:** "I-TA-DA-KU — I TIED (ta) DA COOL (daku) to RECEIVE!"
**Kanji:** 頭 (head) element — bowing your HEAD to RECEIVE humbly!

## 353. 敵 (てき / teki) — enemy
**Pronunciation:** "TE-KI — TEA KEY! The ENEMY stole my tea key!"
**Kanji:** 舌 (tongue) + 攵 (hand). "A lying TONGUE with a HAND raised = ENEMY!"

## 354. 起きる (おきる / okiru) — to occur
**Pronunciation:** "O-KI-RU — OH KEY ROO! Something OCCURS with the key!"
**Kanji:** 起 = rise (走 run + 己 self). "SELF RISING = something OCCURS!"

## 355. 起きる (おきる / okiru) — to get up
**Pronunciation:** "O-KI-RU — OH! KEY ROO GETS UP!"
**Kanji:** Same kanji 起. "Rising from bed = GETTING UP!"

## 356. 落ちる (おちる / ochiru) — to fall
**Pronunciation:** "O-CHI-RU — OH CHEESE ROO! The kangaroo FALLS with cheese!"
**Kanji:** 艹 (grass) + 水 (water) + 口 (mouth). "Water falling from grass = FALLING!"

## 357. 足 (あし / ashi) — leg, foot
**Pronunciation:** "A-SHI — A SHOE for my FOOT!"
**Kanji:** 足 looks like a LEG with a foot at the bottom!

## 358. それに (それに / sore ni) — in addition
**Pronunciation:** "SO-RE-NI — SO RAY NEAR — IN ADDITION, Ray is near!"
**Kanji:** それ (that) + に (to). "IN ADDITION TO that!"

## 359. 先に (さきに / saki ni) — before, ahead
**Pronunciation:** "SA-KI-NE — SAKE KEY BEFORE dinner!"
**Kanji:** 先 = ahead/previous. に = at. "AHEAD AT = BEFORE!"

## 360. 魔法 (まほう / mahou) — magic
**Pronunciation:** "MA-HOU — MY HOUSE has MAGIC!"
**Kanji:** 魔 = demon/magic (麻 hemp + 鬼 demon). 法 = law/method. "DEMON'S METHOD = MAGIC!"

## 361. 探す (さがす / sagasu) — to look for, to search
**Pronunciation:** "SA-GA-SU — SUE GASPS while SEARCHING!"
**Kanji:** 扌(hand) + 穴 (cave). "HAND in a CAVE = SEARCHING!"

## 362. 子供 (こども / kodomo) — child, kid
**Pronunciation:** "KO-DO-MO — CALL DOOR MORE KIDS!"
**Kanji:** 子 = child. 供 = companion. "CHILD companions = KIDS!"

## 363. 怒る (おこる / okoru) — to get angry
**Pronunciation:** "O-KO-RU — OH CALL ROO! The kangaroo gets ANGRY!"
**Kanji:** 奴 (slave) + 心 (heart). "Your heart treats you like a slave = ANGRY!"

## 364. 笑顔 (えがお / egao) — smile
**Pronunciation:** "E-GA-O — EAGLE (e-ga) OH! Eagle SMILES!"
**Kanji:** 笑 = laugh. 顔 = face. "LAUGHING FACE = SMILE!"

## 365. 先輩 (せんぱい / senpai) — senior
**Pronunciation:** "SEN-PAI — SENATOR PAI is my SENIOR!"
**Kanji:** 先 = ahead. 輩 = companion. "Someone AHEAD of you = SENIOR!"

## 366. 続く (つづく / tsuzuku) — to continue
**Pronunciation:** "TSU-ZU-KU — TWO ZOO COOL! The show CONTINUES!"
**Kanji:** 糸 (thread) + 売. "THREAD unrolling = CONTINUING!"

## 367. こんなに (こんなに / konna ni) — this much, such
**Pronunciation:** "KON-NA-NI — CON NA NEAR! This MUCH con is near!"
**Kanji:** こんな (this kind) + に (to). "THIS MUCH, THIS MUCH!"

## 368. 変 (へん / hen) — strange, funny
**Pronunciation:** "HEN — A HEN is STRANGE!"
**Kanji:** 変 has X marks = CROSSed OUT = STRANGE, unusual!

## 369. 読む (よむ / yomu) — to read
**Pronunciation:** "YO-MU — YO MOO! I READ about a moo!"
**Kanji:** 言 (speech) + 売 (sell). "SPEECH you SELL = READING words!"

## 370. 当然 (とうぜん / touzen) — natural, as a matter of course
**Pronunciation:** "TOU-ZEN — TOE ZEN! It's NATURAL for my toe!"
**Kanji:** 当 = hit/correct. 然 = so/right. "HITTING it RIGHT = NATURALLY so!"

## 371. 情報 (じょうほう / jouhou) — information
**Pronunciation:** "JOU-HOU — JOE HOO! Joe shares INFORMATION!"
**Kanji:** 情 = feeling. 報 = report. "FEELING reported = INFORMATION!"

## 372. 見つける (みつける / mitsukeru) — to find
**Pronunciation:** "MI-TSU-KE-RU — MY TSU KEY ROO! I FOUND the key!"
**Kanji:** 見 (see) + 付 (attach). "SEEING and ATTACHING to = FINDING!"

## 373. 少女 (しょうじょ / shoujo) — girl
**Pronunciation:** "SHOU-JO — SHOW JO is a GIRL!"
**Kanji:** 少 = few/small. 女 = woman. "SMALL WOMAN = GIRL!"

## 374. 大変 (たいへん / taihen) — terrible, serious
**Pronunciation:** "TAI-HEN — MY (tai) HEN! That's a SERIOUS hen!"
**Kanji:** 大 = big. 変 = change. "BIG CHANGE = TERRIBLE, SERIOUS!"

## 375. かなり (かなり / kanari) — fairly, rather
**Pronunciation:** "KA-NA-RI — CANARY (ka-na-ri)! It's FAIRLY a canary!"
**Kanji:** かなり: FAIRLY, RATHER much!

## 376. 下 (した / shita) — under, below
**Pronunciation:** "SHI-TA — SHE TA (below) the table!"
**Kanji:** 下 has a line with a stroke going DOWN = BELOW!

## 377. 下 (もと / moto) — under (supervision of)
**Pronunciation:** "MO-TO — MOAT! Under the MOAT's supervision!"
**Kanji:** Same kanji 下. Read MOTO for "under (supervision)."

## 378. かかる (かかる / kakaru) — to take (time/money)
**Pronunciation:** "KA-KA-RU — CAR CAR ROO! It TAKES two cars!"
**Kanji:** 掛ける related. "HANGING on = TAKING time/money!"

## 379. 掛かる (かかる / kakaru) — to hang, to be hung
**Pronunciation:** "KA-KA-RU — CAR CAR ROO! The picture HANGS on the car!"
**Kanji:** 扌(hand) + 卦 (hexagram). "HAND HANGING something!"

## 380. 一度 (いちど / ichido) — once
**Pronunciation:** "I-CHI-DO — ITCHY DOG! ONCE the itchy dog scratched!"
**Kanji:** 一 = one. 度 = time/degree. "ONE TIME = ONCE!"

## 381. 完全 (かんぜん / kanzen) — perfect, complete
**Pronunciation:** "KAN-ZEN — CAN ZEN! I can zen = PERFECTLY complete!"
**Kanji:** 完 = complete. 全 = whole. "COMPLETELY WHOLE = PERFECT!"

## 382. 後ろ (うしろ / ushiro) — back, behind
**Pronunciation:** "U-SHI-RO — OH SHE RO! She's BEHIND me!"
**Kanji:** 後 = after/behind + ろ. "BEHIND, at the BACK!"

## 383. 泣く (なく / naku) — to cry
**Pronunciation:** "NA-KU — NAUGHTY KID! The naughty kid CRIES!"
**Kanji:** 氵(water) + 口 (mouth). "WATER from your MOUTH = CRYING!"

## 384. 状態 (じょうたい / joutai) — state, condition
**Pronunciation:** "JOU-TAI — JOE'S THIGH! The CONDITION of Joe's thigh!"
**Kanji:** 状 = form. 態 = attitude/condition. "FORM + ATTITUDE = STATE!"

## 385. 関係 (かんけい / kankei) — relation(ship)
**Pronunciation:** "KAN-KEI — CAN KEY! The RELATIONSHIP CAN have a key!"
**Kanji:** 門 (gate) + 弓 (bow) + 糸 (thread). "TIED through gates = RELATIONSHIP!"

## 386. 向ける (むける / mukeru) — to turn toward
**Pronunciation:** "MU-KE-RU — MOOSE KEY ROO! Turn TOWARD the moose key!"
**Kanji:** 向 (face/direction) + る. "FACING + action = TURNING TOWARD!"

## 387. 奥 (おく / oku) — inner part, back
**Pronunciation:** "O-KU — OH COOL! The INNER part is cool!"
**Kanji:** 奥 has 大 (big) + 宀 (roof) + 米 (rice). "BIG ROOF with RICE inside = INNER part!"

## 388. 軽い (かるい / karui) — light (weight)
**Pronunciation:** "KA-RU-I — CAR LOO (light) I! My car is LIGHT!"
**Kanji:** 車 (car) + 工 (work). "A WORKING CAR that's LIGHT!"

## 389. 返す (かえす / kaesu) — to return, to repay
**Pronunciation:** "KA-E-SU — CAR EEL SUE! RETURN the car, eel Sue!"
**Kanji:** 辶 (walk) + 反 (reverse). "REVERSING direction = RETURNING!"

## 390. 現れる (あらわれる / arawareru) — to appear
**Pronunciation:** "A-RA-WA-RE-RU — A RAH-WAH RE ROO! Something APPEARS!"
**Kanji:** 现 = appear (王 king + 見 see). "KING SEEN = APPEARING!"

## 391. 痛い (いたい / itai) — hurt, ouch
**Pronunciation:** "I-TA-I — I TIED MY EYE! OUCH, it HURTS!"
**Kanji:** 疒 (sickness) + 甬 (passage). "SICKNESS PASSING through = PAIN!"

## 392. どうやら (どうやら / douyara) — seems likely
**Pronunciation:** "DOU-YA-RA — DO YARD? It SEEMS LIKELY you do!"
**Kanji:** どう (how) + やら (it seems). "HOW it seems = SEEMS LIKELY!"

## 393. 点ける (つける / tsukeru) — to switch on
**Pronunciation:** "TSU-KE-RU — TSU KEY ROO! SWITCH ON the key!"
**Kanji:** 点 = dot/light. "MAKING a DOT of LIGHT = SWITCHING ON!"

## 394. 付ける (つける / tsukeru) — to attach, to add
**Pronunciation:** "TSU-KE-RU — TSU KEY ROO! ATTACH the key!"
**Kanji:** 付 = attach (亻person + 寸 inch). "PERSON ATTACHING at a POINT!"

## 395. 一瞬 (いっしゅん / isshun) — instant, for a moment
**Pronunciation:** "I-SSHU-N — ISSHUN! For just an INSTANT!"
**Kanji:** 一 = one. 瞬 = blink. "ONE BLINK = INSTANT!"

## 396. 隠す (かくす / kakusu) — to hide
**P�onunciation:** "KA-KU-SU — CAR COOL SUE HIDES!"
**Kanji:** 阝(hill) + 思 (think). "Behind a HILL, THINKING to HIDE!"

## 397. 準備 (じゅんび / junbi) — preparation
**Pronunciation:** "JUN-BI — JUNE BEE! PREPARING for the June bee!"
**Kanji:** 準 = prepare (water + bird). 備 = equip. "PREPARING and EQUIPPING!"

## 398. そろそろ (そろそろ / sorosoro) — soon, about time
**Pronunciation:** "SO-RO-SO-RO — SO SORE RO! SOON it'll be sore!"
**Kanji:** そろ (gradually). "GRADUALLY coming = ABOUT TIME!"

## 399. 落ち着く (おちつく / ochitsuku) — to calm down
**Pronunciation:** "O-CHI-TSU-KU — OH CHEESE TSU COOL! CALM DOWN with cheese!"
**Kanji:** 落 (fall) + 着 (arrive). "FALLING and ARRIVING = SETTLING DOWN!"

## 400. 恥ずかしい (はずかしい / hazukashii) — embarrassing, shy
**Pronunciation:** "HA-ZU-KA-SHII — HASU (lotus) COOL SHE! Having a lotus on your face is EMBARRASSING!"
**Kanji:** 耳 (ear) + 心 (heart). "EARS and HEART blushing = EMBARRASING!"

# Kaishi 1.5k Mnemonics — Words 401–500

---

## 401. 場合 (ばあい / baai) — situation, case
**Pronunciation:** "BA-AI — BUY EYE! In this SITUATION, buy an eye!"
**Kanji:** 場 = place. 合 = fit. "PLACE that FITS = the SITUATION!"

## 402. しっかり (しっかり / shikkari) — tightly, firmly
**Pronunciation:** "SHI-KKA-RI — SHE CAR RIDE TIGHTLY!"
**Kanji:** しっ (sharp grip) + かり (borrow). "GRIPPING TIGHTLY!"

## 403. しっかり (しっかり / shikkari) — reliable, dependable
**Pronunciation:** "SHI-KKA-RI — SHE's CAR is RELIABLE!"
**Kanji:** Same word. "GRIPPING FIRMLY = RELIABLE!"

## 404. 髪 (かみ / kami) — hair, hairstyle
**Pronunciation:** "KA-MI — CAR MY HAIR! KA-MI = HAIR!"
**Kanji:** 髪 has 彡 (hair strokes) at bottom — those are HAIR strands!

## 405. 元 (もと / moto) — former, ex-
**Pronunciation:** "MO-TO — MOAT! The FORMER moat is now dry!"
**Kanji:** 元 = origin/beginning. "The ORIGINAL = FORMER!"

## 406. 得る (える / eru) — to obtain, to gain
**Pronunciation:** "E-RU — EEL ROO! I GAINED an eel-kangaroo!"
**Kanji:** 彳(step) + 見 (see). "Stepping and SEEING = OBTAINING!"

## 407. すぐ (すぐ / sugu) — at once, right away
**Pronunciation:** "SU-GU — SU-GO! GO RIGHT AWAY!"
**Kanji:** すぐ: RIGHT AWAY, IMMEDIATELY!

## 408. 来月 (らいげつ / raigetsu) — next month
**Pronunciation:** "RAI-GETSU — RYE GETS! NEXT MONTH I get rye!"
**Kanji:** 来 = come. 月 = month. "COMING MONTH = NEXT MONTH!"

## 409. 昔 (むかし / mukashi) — a long time ago
**Pronunciation:** "MU-KA-SHI — MOOSE CAR SHE! LONG AGO, a moose drove!"
**Kanji:** 昔 has 艹 (grass) + 日 (sun). "SUN under GRASS = ancient times!"

## 410. 近い (ちかい / chikai) — near
**Pronunciation:** "CHI-KA-I — CHEESE CAR EYE! The cheese is NEAR my eye!"
**Kanji:** 辶 (walk) + 斤 (axe). "Just a few STEPS (walk) away = NEAR!"

## 411. 動き (うごき / ugoki) — movement, motion
**Pronunciation:** "U-GO-KI — YOU GO KEY! Your movement has the KEY!"
**Kanji:** 動 = move (heavy + power). "HEAVY POWER = MOVEMENT!"

## 412. 見つめる (みつめる / mitsumeru) — to stare
**Pronunciation:** "MI-TSU-ME-RU — MY TSU-ME ROO! The kangaroo STARES!"
**Kanji:** 見 (see) + つ (point) + る. "SEEING with a FIXED POINT = STARING!"

## 413. 幸せ (しあわせ / shiawase) — happiness, good fortune
**Pronunciation:** "SHI-A-WA-SE — SHE AWAITS (shiawa) HAPPINESS!"
**Kanji:** 土 (earth) + ノ (diagonal) + 干 (dry). Think of earth, sky, and water — natural HAPPINESS!

## 414. 記憶 (きおく / kioku) — memory
**Pronunciation:** "KI-OKU — KEY OK! My KEY memory is OK!"
**Kanji:** 記 = record (言 speech). 憶 = recollect (忄heart). "SPEECH recorded in the HEART = MEMORY!"

## 415. 反応 (はんのう / hannou) — reaction, response
**Pronunciation:** "HAN-NOU — HAND NOU (now)! My hand REACTS NOW!"
**Kanji:** 反 = reverse/oppose. 応 = respond. "OPPOSING then RESPONDING = REACTION!"

## 416. 私たち (わたしたち / watashitachi) — we
**Pronunciation:** "WA-TA-SHI-TACHI — WAFFLE + SHE + TACHI (stand)! WE stand for waffles!"
**Kanji:** 私 = I. たち = plural. "I and others STANDING = WE!"

## 417. とにかく (とにかく / tonikaku) — in any case, anyway
**Pronunciation:** "TO-NI-KA-KU — TO NEAR CAR COOL! IN ANY CASE, near the car!"
**Kanji:** とにかく: IN ANY CASE, REGARDLESS!

## 418. 勝手 (かって / katte) — selfish, self-centered
**Pronunciation:** "KA-TTE — CAR TIGHT! A SELFISH car taking all the space!"
**Kanji:** 勝 = win. 手 = hand. "WINNING with your own HAND = SELFISH!"

## 419. 攻撃 (こうげき / kougeki) — attack, assault
**Pronunciation:** "KOU-GEKI — GOAT (kou) GECKO (geki)! The goat ATTACKS the gecko!"
**Kanji:** 攻 = attack (work + tap). 撃 = strike (車 car + 手 hand). "ATTACKING and STRIKING!"

## 420. 開ける (あける / akeru) — to open, to unlock
**Pronunciation:** "A-KE-RU — A KEY ROO! The kangaroo OPENS with a key!"
**Kanji:** 門 (gate) + 开 (open). "OPENING the GATE!"

## 421. 黙る (だまる / damaru) — to become silent
**Pronunciation:** "DA-MA-RU — DAMN ROO! The kangaroo goes SILENT!"
**Kanji:** 黒 (black) + 犬 (dog). "A BLACK DOG going SILENT!"

## 422. 最近 (さいきん / saikin) — recently
**Pronunciation:** "SAI-KIN — SIDE KIN! My SIDE KIN is RECENTLY here!"
**Kanji:** 最 = most. 近 = near. "MOST RECENT = RECENTLY!"

## 423. 全員 (ぜんいん / zen'in) — all members, entire staff
**Pronunciation:** "ZEN-IN — ZEN MONKS! ALL MEMBERS of the zen!"
**Kanji:** 全 = all. 員 = member. "ALL MEMBERS!"

## 424. せい (せい / sei) — cause, fault
**Pronunciation:** "SAY — It's your SAY! Your FAULT!"
**Kanji:** せい: THE FAULT/CAUSE of something! "It's your fault (sei)!"

## 425. 深い (ふかい / fukai) — deep, profound
**Pronunciation:** "FU-KA-I — FREAKY EYE! That DEEP freaky eye!"
**Kanji:** 氵(water) + 罙 (deep). "WATER going DEEP = PROFOUND!"

## 426. 別 (べつ / betsu) — another, different
**Pronunciation:** "BE-TSU — BET TSU! A DIFFERENT bet!"
**Kanji:** 另 (separate) + 刂(knife). "SEPARATED = DIFFERENT!"

## 427. 決まる (きまる / kimaru) — to be decided
**Pronunciation:** "KI-MA-RU — KEY MARBLE! The decision is SET!"
**Kanji:** 氵(water) + 夬 (decisive). "WATER making a DECISIVE cut = DECIDED!"

## 428. しばらく (しばらく / shibaraku) — for a while
**Pronunciation:** "SHI-BA-RA-KU — SHE BAR COOL FOR A WHILE!"
**Kanji:** しばらく: FOR A WHILE, FOR THE TIME BEING!

## 429. 行う (おこなう / okonau) — to perform, to do
**Pronunciation:** "O-KO-NA-U — OH CALL NAUGHTY! I PERFORM naughtiness!"
**Kanji:** 行 (go/do). "GOING and DOING = PERFORMING!"

## 430. 上がる (あがる / agaru) — to go up, to rise
**Pronunciation:** "A-GA-RU — A GO ROO! The kangaroo GOES UP!"
**Kanji:** 上 = up. "GOING UP = RISING!"

## 431. 隣 (となり / tonari) — next to, adjacent
**Pronunciation:** "TO-NA-RI — TOE NARROW! My toe is NEXT TO the narrow space!"
**Kanji:** 阝(village) + 辛 (bitter/spicy). "The VILLAGE NEXT DOOR!"

## 432. 扉 (とびら / tobira) — door
**Pronunciation:** "TO-BI-RA — TOE BE RA! My toe sees a DOOR!"
**Kanji:** 扉 has 戸 (door) + 非 (wrong). "A DOOR that was WRONG = the DOOR itself!"

## 433. 指 (ゆび / yubi) — finger, toe
**Pronunciation:** "YU-BI — YOU BE! YOU BE pointing with your FINGER!"
**Kanji:** 扌(hand) + 旨 (aim). "HAND with an AIM = FINGER!"

## 434. 結果 (けっか / kekka) — result, outcome
**Pronunciation:** "KEKKA — KICKER! The RESULT of the KICKER!"
**Kanji:** 結 = tie. 果 = fruit. "TIED together = the RESULT!"

## 435. 与える (あたえる / ataeru) — to give
**Pronunciation:** "A-TA-E-RU — A TAIL EEL ROO! GIVE the eel a tail!"
**Kanji:** 与 (give) + る. "GIVING something!"

## 436. 道 (みち / michi) — road
**Pronunciation:** "MI-CHI — MY CHEESE on the ROAD!"
**Kanji:** 道 has 辶 (walk) + 首 (head). "HEAD going for a WALK = ROAD!"

## 437. 乗る (のる / noru) — to ride, to take
**pronunciation:** "NO-RU — NO RULE! I RIDE with no rules!"
**Kanji:** 乗 has 木 (tree) + 匕 (spoon) + large strokes. "Standing on something = RIDING!"

## 438. 特に (とくに / tokuni) — specially, particularly
**Pronunciation:** "TO-KU-NI — TOE COOL KNEE! PARTICULARLY my cool knee!"
**Kanji:** 特 = special (cow + temple). "A SPECIAL cow at the temple = PARTICULARLY!"

## 439. 形 (かたち / katachi) — shape
**Pronunciation:** "KA-TA-CHI — CAR TIRE CHEESE! The SHAPE of a car tire cheese!"
**Kanji:** 形 has 开 (open) + 彡 (design). "OPEN design = SHAPE!"

## 440. さあ (さあ / saa) — Hmm, well...
**Pronunciation:** "SAA — SIGH... HMMMM... I'm not sure!"
**Kanji:** さあ: A hesitation sound. "WELL... HMM..."

## 441. 血 (ち / chi) — blood
**Pronunciation:** "CHEE — CHEESE! RED like BLOOD!"
**Kanji:** 血 has 氵 (water) + 皿 (dish). "WATER in a DISH = BLOOD in a vessel!"

## 442. 更に (さらに / sarani) — furthermore, even more
**Pronunciation:** "SA-RA-NI — SAW RA NEAR! Furthermore, I saw RA nearby!"
**Kanji:** 更 = renew/more. "RENEWING with EVEN MORE!"

## 443. 用意 (ようい / youi) — preparation, getting ready
**Pronunciation:** "YOU-I — YOU EYE! Get YOUR EYE READY!"
**Kanji:** 用 = use/matter. 意 = idea. "USE your IDEA = PREPARING!"

## 444. 負ける (まける / makeru) — to lose
**pronunciation:** "MA-KE-RU — MY KEY ROO LOSES!"
**Kanji:** 負 = lose (trade + shell). "Losing your MONEY = LOSING!"

## 445. 神 (かみ / kami) — god, spirit
**Pronunciation:** "KA-MI — CAR MY GOD! My car is divine!"
**Kanji:** 礻(altar) + 申 (say). "Speaking at an ALTAR = GOD!"

## 446. 実は (じつは / jitsuwa) — actually, the truth is
**Pronunciation:** "JI-TSU-WA — GEE TSU WA! Actually, gee, that's true!"
**Kanji:** 実 = truth/fruit. は = topic marker. "The TRUTH is..."

## 447. 服 (ふく / fuku) — clothes
**Pronunciation:** "FU-KU — FOSSIL COOL CLOTHES!"
**Kanji:** 月 (body) + 又 (again). "BODY covering AGAIN = CLOTHES!"

## 448. 不思議 (ふしぎ / fushigi) — strange, wonderful
**Pronunciation:** "FU-SHI-GI — FOOD SHE GEE! Food she gee = STRANGE!"
**Kanji:** 不 = not. 思 = think. 議 = discuss. "UNTHINKABLE = STRANGE, WONDERFUL!"

## 449. 意識 (いしき / ishiki) — consciousness, awareness
**Pronunciation:** "I-SHI-KEY — EYES (ishi) KEY! Eyes are the KEY to AWARENESS!"
**Kanji:** 意 = idea. 識 = recognize. "IDEAS you RECOGNIZE = CONSCIOUSNESS!"

## 450. 一体 (いったい / ittai) — what on earth
**Pronunciation:** "ITTAI — HITTAI! WHAT ON EARTH hit?!"
**Kanji:** 一 = one. 体 = body. "ONE BODY = WHAT ON EARTH?!"

## 451. とりあえず (とりあえず / toriaezu) — for now, first of all
**Pronunciation:** "TO-RI-A-E-ZU — TWO REA EZU! FOR NOW, two reasons!"
**Kanji:** とりあえず: FOR NOW, FOR THE TIME BEING!

## 452. 送る (おくる / okuru) — to send
**Pronunciation:** "O-KU-RU — OH COOL ROO! SEND the cool kangaroo!"
**Kanji:** 辶 (walk) + 関 (relation). "WALKING with a message = SENDING!"

## 453. では (では / de wa) — then
**Pronunciation:** "DE-WA — DAY WALK! THEN let's walk today!"
**Kanji:** で (at/by) + は (topic). "THEN, by that means!"

## 454. 安心 (あんしん / anshin) — peace of mind, relief
**Pronunciation:** "AN-SHIN — ANXIETY then SHIN! Now I have PEACE!"
**Kanji:** 安 = cheap/peaceful. 心 = heart. "PEACEFUL HEART = RELIEF!"

## 455. 失う (うしなう / ushinau) — to lose, to miss
**Pronunciation:** "U-SHI-NA-U — YOU SHE NOT YOU! You lost her, not you!"
**Kanji:** 失 = lose (person slipping). "A person LOSING grip = MISSING!"

## 456. 行動 (こうどう / koudou) — action, conduct
**Pronunciation:** "KOU-DOU — GO DO! Take ACTION — GO DO it!"
**Kanji:** 行 = go. 動 = move. "GOING and MOVING = ACTION!"

## 457. 触れる (ふれる / fureru) — to touch, to come in contact
**Pronunciation:** "FU-RE-RU — FOOD RAY ROO! TOUCH the food ray kangaroo!"
**Kanji:** 角 (horn) + 虫 (insect). "A HORN touching an INSECT!"

## 458. そもそも (そもそも / somosomo) — in the first place
**Pronunciation:** "SO-MO-SO-MO — SORE MO (that also) IN THE FIRST PLACE!"
**Kanji:** そもそも: FUNDAMENTALLY, FROM THE START!

## 459. 少ない (すくない / sukunai) — few, little
**Pronunciation:** "SU-KU-NA-I — SUE COOL NINE! Only FEW cool nines!"
**Kanji:** 少 = few (small + drops). "SMALL with FEW drops = LITTLE!"

## 460. 結局 (けっきょく / kekkyoku) — after all, finally
**Pronunciation:** "KEK-KYOU-KU — KICK YOU COOL! AFTER ALL, kick you cool!"
**Kanji:** 結 = tie. 局 = office/situation. "TIED situation FINALLY resolved!"

## 461. 空 (そら / sora) — sky, weather
**Pronunciation:** "SO-RA — SOAR (sora)! Birds SOAR in the SKY!"
**Kanji:** 穴 (cave/hole) + 工 (work). "WORKING through a HOLE in the clouds = SKY!"

## 462. 済む (すむ / sumu) — to be settled, to complete
**Pronunciation:** "SU-MU — SUE MOO! Sue's problem is SETTLED!"
**Kanji:** 氵(water) + 青 (blue). "Water turning BLUE = SETTLED, COMPLETED!"

## 463. 任せる (まかせる / makaseru) — to entrust to
**Pronunciation:** "MA-KA-SE-RU — MY CAR SAYS ROO! I ENTRUST my car!"
**Kanji:** 任 = responsibility (亻person + ノ + 工). "A PERSON taking RESPONSIBILITY = ENTRUSTING!"

## 464. 赤い (あかい / akai) — red
**Pronunciation:** "A-KA-I — A CAR EYE! A RED car eye!"
**Kanji:** 赤 has 土 (earth) + ノ + ノ. Think of RED clay earth!

## 465. 大事 (だいじ / daiji) — important, precious
**Pronunciation:** "DAI-JI — DIE GEE! It's IMPORTANT not to die!"
**Kanji:** 大 = big. 事 = thing. "BIG THING = IMPORTANT!"

## 466. 残す (のこす / nokosu) — to leave, to reserve
**Pronunciation:** "NO-KO-SU — NO CALL SUE! I'll LEAVE Sue!"
**Kanji:** 歹 (death) + 戋. "After death, what you LEAVE behind!"

## 467. 止まる (とまる / tomaru) — to stop, to come to a stop
**Pronunciation:** "TO-MA-RU — TOM MARBLE! Tom's marble STOPS!"
**Kanji:** 止 = stop (foot planted). "PLANTING your foot = STOPPING!"

## 468. 朝 (あさ / asa) — morning
**Pronunciation:** "A-SA — A SAW in the MORNING!"
**Kanji:** 朝 has 十 + 日 (sun) + 十 + 月 (moon). "SUN (日) and MOON (月) together = DAWN/MORNING!"

## 469. 命 (いのち / inochi) — life
**Pronunciation:** "I-NO-CHI — INO (dog) CHEESE! A dog's LIFE is cheesy!"
**Kanji:** 令 (order) + 口 (mouth). "ORDERS from the mouth = LIFE force!"

## 470. 不安 (ふあん / fuan) — uneasiness, anxiety
**Pronunciation:** "FU-AN — FOOD ANXIETY! I'm ANXIOUS about food!"
**Kanji:** 不 = not. 安 = peaceful. "NOT PEACEFUL = ANXIOUS!"

## 471. 結構 (けっこう / kekkou) — all right, no thank you
**Pronunciation:** "KEK-KOU — KICKER! NO THANK YOU, kicker!"
**Kanji:** 結 = tie. 構 = structure. "STRUCTURED ENOUGH = ALL RIGHT!"

## 472. 結構 (けっこう / kekkou) — quite
**Pronunciation:** "KEK-KOU — KICKER! QUITE a good kicker!"
**Kanji:** Same kanji. "STRUCTURED = QUITE good!"

## 473. 新しい (あたらしい / atarashii) — new
**Pronunciation:** "A-TA-RA-SHII — A TARA (fish) SHE! A NEW fish for her!"
**Kanji:** 親 (parent) + 斤 (axe). "PARENT cutting with a NEW axe = NEW!"

## 474. 程度 (ていど / teido) — degree, extent
**Pronunciation:** "TEI-DO — TED DO! Ted does it to a DEGREE!"
**Kanji:** 程 = extent (禾 grain + 呈 present). 度 = degree. "The EXTENT of the DEGREE!"

## 475. 壁 (かべ / kabe) — wall, partition
**pronunciation:** "KA-BE — CAR BAY! The car hits the WALL!"
**Kanji:** 壁 has 土 (earth) on top — walls built from EARTH!

## 476. 用 (よう / you) — things to do, task
**Pronunciation:** "YOU — YOU have things to do! TASKS!"
**Kanji:** 用 has 田 (field) with a stroke — "things to do in the FIELD!"

## 477. 背中 (せなか / senaka) — back
**Pronunciation:** "SE-NA-KA — SAY NAH CAR! My BACK says nah to the car!"
**Kanji:** 背 = back (north + flesh). 中 = middle. "The MIDDLE of your BACK!"

## 478. 気付く (きづく / kizuku) — to realize, to notice
**Pronunciation:** "KI-ZU-KU — KEY ZOO COOL! I NOTICE the key at the zoo!"
**Kanji:** 気 = spirit. 付 = attach. "SPIRIT ATTACHING = REALIZING!"

## 479. いきなり (いきなり / ikinari) — all of a sudden
**Pronunciation:** "I-KI-NA-RI — I KEY NAH READ! ALL OF A SUDDEN!"
**Kanji:** いきなり: WITHOUT WARNING, SUDDENLY!

## 480. 渡す (わたす / watasu) — to carry across, to hand over
**Pronunciation:** "WA-TA-SU — WATER SUE! SUE HANDS OVER water!"
**Kanji:** 氵(water) + 度 (degree). "WATER going ACROSS = CARRYING ACROSS!"

## 481. 喜ぶ (よろこぶ / yorokobu) — to be happy
**Pronunciation:** "YO-RO-KO-BU — YOUR ROPE COOL BUDDY! HAPPY rope buddy!"
**Kanji:** 吉 (fortune) + 口 (mouth) + 豆 (beans). "FORTUNE (吉) in your MOUTH = HAPPY!"

## 482. 可愛い (かわいい / kawaii) — cute
**Pronunciation:** "KA-WA-II — CAR WALK EYE! The car walks with CUTE eyes!"
**Kanji:** 可 = possible. 愛 = love. "POSSIBLE LOVE = CUTE!"

## 483. 始まる (はじまる / hajimaru) — to begin
**Pronunciation:** "HA-JI-MA-RU — HAZE MY ROO! The BEGINNING of the haze!"
**Kanji:** 始 = start (woman + pedestal). "A WOMAN BEGINNING the show!"

## 484. 色 (いろ / iro) — color
**Pronunciation:** "I-RO — EYE ROW of COLORS!"
**Kanji:** 色 has ⺈ (knife) on top and 巴 (coil) below. "A KNIFE cutting through COILED colors!"

## 485. 認める (みとめる / mitomeru) — to recognize, to approve
**Pronunciation:** "MI-TO-ME-RU — MY TOE ME ROO! I RECOGNIZE my toe-kangaroo!"
**Kanji:** 言 (speech) + 認 (know). "SPEECH that KNOWS = RECOGNIZING!"

## 486. 急 (きゅう / kyu) — suddenly
**Pronunciation:** "KYU — CUTE! Suddenly CUTE!"
**Kanji:** 急 has 刍 (straw) + 心 (heart). "STRAW on your HEART = SUDDEN shock!"

## 487. 向こう (むこう / mukou) — the other side, opponent
**Pronunciation:** "MU-KOU — MOOSE GO! The moose goes to THE OTHER SIDE!"
**Kanji:** 向 (face/direction). "FACING the OTHER SIDE!"

## 488. 求める (もとめる / motomeru) — to seek, to wish for
**Pronunciation:** "MO-TO-ME-RU — MOTEL ROO! SEEK the kangaroo motel!"
**Kanji:** 求 looks like a cross with dots = SEEKING in all directions!

## 489. 肩 (かた / kata) — shoulder
**Pronunciation:** "KA-TA — CAR TIRE on my SHOULDER!"
**Kanji:** 肩 has 戸 (door) + 月 (flesh). "A DOOR made of FLESH = SHOULDER!"

## 490. 仲間 (なかま / nakama) — comrade, peer
**Pronunciation:** "NA-KA-MA — NAH CAR MA! Nah, my COMRADE's car!"
**Kanji:** 仲 = middle (亻person + 中 middle). 間 = between. "People BETWEEN each other = COMRADES!"

## 491. ようやく (ようやく / youyaku) — at last
**Pronunciation:** "YOU-YA-KU — YOUR YAKUZA! AT LAST, your yakuza arrives!"
**Kanji:** ようやく: FINALLY, AFTER ALL THIS TIME!

## 492. 思わず (おもわず / omowazu) — unintentionally
**Pronunciation:** "O-MO-WA-ZU — OH MY WAFFLE! UNINTENTIONALLY!"
**Kanji:** 思 = think. わず = without thinking. "WITHOUT THINKING = UNINTENTIONALLY!"

## 493. ほとんど (ほとんど / hotondo) — almost, hardly
**Pronunciation:** "HO-TON-DO — HOT DOG! ALMOST a hot dog!"
**Kanji:** ほとんど: NEARLY, ALMOST! Hardly any left!

## 494. 突然 (とつぜん / totsuzen) — abruptly, suddenly
**Pronunciation:** "TO-TSU-ZEN — TOE TSU ZEN! My toe suddenly went ZEN!"
**Kanji:** 突 = stab suddenly (穴 hole + 大 big). 然 = so. "SUDDENLY like that!"

## 495. 自身 (じしん / jishin) — self, oneself
**Pronunciation:** "JI-SHIN — GEE SHEEN! Gee, what a SHEEN on MYSELF!"
**Kanji:** 自 = self (nose = pointing at SELF). 身 = body. "MY OWN BODY = MYSELF!"

## 496. 再び (ふたたび / futatabi) — again, a second time
**Pronunciation:** "FU-TA-TA-BI — FOOD TIE TIE AGAIN! A SECOND TIME tying food!"
**Kanji:** 再 = again. び = time. "AGAIN = A SECOND TIME!"

## 497. 静か (しずか / shizuka) — quiet
**Pronunciation:** "SHI-ZU-KA — SHE ZOO CAR! The zoo car is QUIET!"
**Kanji:** 青 (blue) + 爪 (claw). "BLUE CLAWS = QUIET and calm!"

## 498. 飛ぶ (とぶ / tobu) — to fly
**pronunciation:** "TO-BU — TOE BUZZ! My toe BUZZES and FLIES!"
**Kanji:** 飛 has wings on both sides — a bird FLYING! 飛ぶ = to fly!

## 499. 白い (しろい / shiroi) — white, blank
**pronunciation:** "SHI-RO-I — SHE RODE EYES! She rode with WHITE eyes!"
**Kanji:** 白 = white (a DROP of white inside a box = pure WHITE!)

## 500. 届く (とどく / todoku) — to reach, to be received
**Pronunciation:** "TO-DO-KU — TOE DOOR COOL! The message REACHES the cool door!"
**Kanji:** 届 has 戸 (door) + 由 (reason). "A REASON reaching your DOOR = RECEIVED!"

# Kaishi 1.5k Mnemonics — Words 501–600

---

## 501. 生む (うむ / umu) — to have children
**Pronunciation:** "U-MU — YOU MOO! You MOO and HAVE CHILDREN!"
**Kanji:** 生 = birth/life. "GIVING BIRTH = having children!"

## 502. 倒す (たおす / taosu) — to bring down, to overthrow
**Pronunciation:** "TA-O-SU — TIE (ta) OH SUE! TIE oh sue DOWN!"
**Kanji:** 亻(person) + 到 (arrive). "A person ARRIVING to OVERTHROW!"

## 503. 似る (にる / niru) — to look similar
**Pronunciation:** "NI-RU — KNEE ROO! My knee LOOKS LIKE the kangaroo's!"
**Kanji:** 亻(person) + 以 (by means of). "A person who is LIKE another!"

## 504. 空気 (くうき / kuuki) — atmosphere, air
**Pronunciation:** "KUU-KI — COOL KEY! The AIR has a cool key!"
**Kanji:** 空 = sky/empty. 気 = spirit/air. "SKY'S AIR = ATMOSPHERE!"

## 505. 腰 (こし / koshi) — hip, lower back
**Pronunciation:** "KO-SHI — CALL SHE! Call her HIP!"
**Kanji:** 月 (flesh) + 要 (important). "IMPORTANT FLESH = your HIP!"

## 506. 名 (な / na) — name, reputation
**Pronunciation:** "NA — NAH! That's my NAME!"
**Kanji:** 夕 (evening) + 口 (mouth). "MOUTH in the EVENING = saying your NAME!"

## 507. 危険 (きけん / kiken) — danger, dangerous
**Pronunciation:** "KI-KEN — KEY KEN! Key Ken is in DANGER!"
**Kanji:** 危 = danger (cliff + person). 隹 (bird) + 阝(hill). "DANGER on a cliff!"

## 508. 付く (つく / tsuku) — to attach itself to
**Pronunciation:** "TSU-KU — TSU COOL! It ATTACHES itself to cool!"
**Kanji:** 付 = attach (亻person + 寸 inch). "PERSON ATTACHING!"

## 509. つく (つく / tsuku) — to be switched on
**Pronunciation:** "TSU-KU — TSU COOL! The light SWITCHES ON!"
**Kanji:** 付く related. "ATTACHING to power = SWITCHED ON!"

## 510. 熱い (あつい / atsui) — hot (things, touch)
**Pronunciation:** "A-TSU-I — A TSU EYE! My eye is HOT!"
**Kanji:** 熱 has 埶 (force) + 灬 (fire). "FORCE with FIRE = HOT!"

## 511. ただいま (ただいま / tadaima) — I'm back
**Pronunciation:** "TA-DA-I-MA — TIE DIE EYE MA! I'M BACK from dying my eye!"
**Kanji:** ただいま: Said when arriving home. "I'M HOME!"

## 512. 頂きます (いただきます / itadakimasu) — let's eat
**Pronunciation:** "I-TA-DA-KI-MA-SU — I TIE DA KI MA SUE! Let's eat!"
**Kanji:** 頂 = receive (head + arrive). "HUMBLY RECEIVING food = let's eat!"

## 513. 慌てる (あわてる / awateru) — to be flustered
**Pronunciation:** "A-WA-TE-RU — A WATER ROO! The kangaroo is FLUSTERED in water!"
**Kanji:** 忄(heart) + 亡 (lost). "HEART LOST = FLUSTERED!"

## 514. 周囲 (しゅうい / shuu'i) — circumference, surroundings
**Pronunciation:** "SHUU-I — SHOE EYE! My SHOE eye sees the SURROUNDINGS!"
**Kanji:** 周 = circumference. 囲 = surround (囗 enclosure + 韋 leather). "SURROUNDING area!"

## 515. 気分 (きぶん / kibun) — mood, feeling
**Pronunciation:** "KI-BUN — KEY BUN! My KEY BUN gives me a good MOOD!"
**Kanji:** 気 = spirit. 分 = divide. "SPIRIT DIVIDED = MOOD, FEELING!"

## 516. 事実 (じじつ / jijitsu) — fact, truth
**Pronunciation:** "JI-JI-TSU — GEE GEE TSU! GEE GEE is the TRUTH!"
**Kanji:** 事 = thing. 実 = truth/fruit. "The THING is TRUE = FACT!"

## 517. まず (まず / mazu) — first (of all)
**Pronunciation:** "MA-ZU — MY ZOO! FIRST, let's go to MY ZOO!"
**Kanji:** まず: FIRST, TO BEGIN WITH!

## 518. 本気 (ほんき / honki) — seriousness
**Pronunciation:** "HON-KEY — HONKEY! I'm SERIOUS about the honkey!"
**Kanji:** 本 = true/origin. 気 = spirit. "TRUE SPIRIT = SERIOUSNESS!"

## 519. 頷く (うなずく / unazuku) — to nod
**Pronunciation:** "U-NA-ZU-KU — YOU NA ZOO COOL! You NOD at the cool zoo!"
**Kanji:** 頁 (head) + 合 (fit). "HEAD fitting down = NODDING!"

## 520. 通り (とおり / toori) — street
**Pronunciation:** "TO-O-RI — TWO OLD READ! Two old people READ on the STREET!"
**Kanji:** 通 = pass through. "Something that you PASS THROUGH = STREET!"

## 521. 想像 (そうぞう / souzou) — imagination
**Pronunciation:** "SOU-ZOU — SOUL ZOO! My SOUL has IMAGINATION like a zoo!"
**Kanji:** 想 = think (tree + heart). 像 = image. "THOUGHT IMAGE = IMAGINATION!"

## 522. 店 (みせ / mise) — shop, store
**Pronunciation:** "MI-SE — MISS! MISS runs the SHOP!"
**Kanji:** 店 has 广 (building) + 占 (fortune teller). "A BUILDING with a fortune teller = SHOP!"

## 523. 何だか (なんだか / nandaka) — somehow, for some reason
**Pronunciation:** "NAN-DA-KA — NAN DA CAR! SOMEHOW nan da car!"
**Kanji:** なんだか: SOMEHOW, FOR SOME REASON!

## 524. 目的 (もくてき / mokuteki) — purpose, aim
**Pronunciation:** "MO-KU-TE-KEY — MOAT KEY! The PURPOSE is the moat key!"
**Kanji:** 目 = eye. 的 = target. "EYE on the TARGET = PURPOSE!"

## 525. 眠る (ねむる / nemuru) — to sleep
**Pronunciation:** "NE-MU-RU — NAY MOOSE ROO! The moose SLEEPS!"
**Kanji:** 目 (eye) + 民 (people). "PEOPLE's EYES closing = SLEEPING!"

## 526. 確か (たしか / tashika) — certainly, surely
**Pronunciation:** "TA-SHI-KA — TIE SHE CAR! I CERTAINLY tied her car!"
**Kanji:** 確 = certain (stone + bird). "CERTAIN as stone!"

## 527. 十分 (じゅうぶん / juubun) — plenty
**Pronunciation:** "JUU-BUN — JUICE BUN! PLENTY of juice buns!"
**Kanji:** 十 = ten. 分 = divide. "TEN divided = PLENTY!"

## 528. 料理 (りょうり / ryouri) — cooking, cuisine
**Pronunciation:** "RYOU-RI — RURAL READ! RURAL COOKING!"
**Kanji:** 料 = measure. 理 = reason. "Measuring REASONS = COOKING!"

## 529. 頃 (ころ / koro) — approximate time
**Pronunciation:** "KO-RO — CALL ROW! APPROXIMATE TIME to call row!"
**Kanji:** 頃 has 頁 (head) + 匕 (spoon). "HEAD with a spoon = around THAT TIME!"

## 530. なかなか (なかなか / nakanaka) — rather, fairly
**Pronunciation:** "NA-KA-NA-KA — NAH CAR NAH CAR! FAIRLY nah car!"
**Kanji:** なかなか: RATHER, FAIRLY, NOT EASILY!

## 531. 正直 (しょうじき / shoujiki) — honest, upright
**Pronunciation:** "SHOU-JI-KEY — SHOW GEE KEY! Show the HONEST key!"
**Kanji:** 正 = correct. 直 = straight. "CORRECT and STRAIGHT = HONEST!"

## 532. 身 (み / mi) — body, one's own person
**Pronunciation:** "MI — MY BODY! MI = MY!"
**Kanji:** 身 looks like a BODY with arms inside!

## 533. いっぱい (いっぱい / ippai) — full, a lot
**Pronunciation:** "IP-PAI — EYE PIE! My eye is FULL of pie!"
**Kanji:** いっぱい: FULL, A LOT, BRIMMING!

## 534. 美しい (うつくしい / utsukushii) — beautiful
**Pronunciation:** "U-TSU-KU-SHII — YOU TSU COOL SHE! YOU are BEAUTIFUL!"
**Kanji:** 羊 (sheep) + 大 (big) + 口 (mouth). "A BIG SHEEP with a pretty MOUTH = BEAUTIFUL!"

## 535. 甘い (あまい / amai) — sweet
**Pronunciation:** "A-MA-I — A MY EYE! A sweet treat for MY eye!"
**Kanji:** 甘 has 日 (sun) + 二 (two). "Two drops of SUNLIGHT = SWEET!"

## 536. むしろ (むしろ / mushiro) — rather
**Pronunciation:** "MU-SHI-RO — MOOSE SHE RO! RATHER the moose she roars!"
**Kanji:** むしろ: RATHER, ON THE CONTRARY!

## 537. 首 (くび / kubi) — neck
**Pronunciation:** "KU-BI — COOL BEE! A bee on my NECK!"
**Kanji:** 首 looks like a HEAD with a neck and collar!

## 538. 感情 (かんじょう / kanjou) — feeling, emotion
**Pronunciation:** "KAN-JOU — CAN JOE! CAN Joe express FEELINGS?"
**Kanji:** 感 = feel. 情 = emotion. "FEELING + EMOTION = FEELINGS!"

## 539. 周り (まわり / mawari) — around, surroundings
**Pronunciation:** "MA-WA-RI — MY WALK READ! I READ around!"
**Kanji:** 周 = circumference. "Going AROUND = SURROUNDINGS!"

## 540. 方法 (ほうほう / houhou) — method, way
**Pronunciation:** "HOU-HOU — WHO HO! WHO knows the METHOD?"
**Kanji:** 方 = direction. 法 = law. "DIRECTION + LAW = METHOD!"

## 541. もう一度 (もういちど / mou ichido) — once more
**Pronunciation:** "MOU ICHI-DO — MORE ITCHY DOG! ONCE MORE itchy dog!"
**Kanji:** もう (already) + 一度 (one time). "ONE MORE TIME!"

## 542. 遅い (おそい / osoi) — slow, late
**Pronunciation:** "O-SO-I — OH SLOW EYE! My eye is SLOW!"
**Kanji:** 辶 (walk) + 尺 (measure). "WALKING at measured pace = SLOW!"

## 543. 会話 (かいわ / kaiwa) — conversation
**Pronunciation:** "KAI-WA — COW WALK! A COW walks into CONVERSATION!"
**Kanji:** 会 = meet. 話 = talk. "MEETING and TALKING = CONVERSATION!"

## 544. 合う (あう / au) — to fit
**Pronunciation:** "A-U — A YOU! A FIT for YOU!"
**Kanji:** 人 (person) + 一 (one). "PERSON + ONE = FITTING together!"

## 545. やって来る (やってくる / yatte kuru) — to come along
**Pronunciation:** "YA-TTE-KU-RU — YACHT COOL ROO! The yacht COMES ALONG!"
**Kanji:** やって (doing) + 来る (come). "DOING and COMING = COMING ALONG!"

## 546. 森 (もり / mori) — forest
**Pronunciation:** "MO-RI — MORE TREES! A FOREST has MORE trees!"
**Kanji:** 森 has THREE trees 木木木 = a FOREST of trees!

## 547. 動かす (うごかす / ugokasu) — to move (something)
**Pronunciation:** "U-GO-KA-SU — YOU GO CAR SUE! MAKE the car move, Sue!"
**Kanji:** 動 = move. "CAUSING to MOVE!"

## 548. 能力 (のうりょく / nouryoku) — ability, capacity
**Pronunciation:** "NOU-RYOU-KU — NOW RULE COOL! NOW rule with ABILITY!"
**Kanji:** 能 = ability (rice + moon). 力 = power. "ABILITY + POWER = CAPACITY!"

## 549. 震える (ふるえる / furueru) — to tremble, to shake
**Pronunciation:** "FU-RU-E-RU — FOOD RULE EEL ROO! The eel TREMBLES!"
**Kanji:** 雨 (rain) + 辰 (dragon). "DRAGON in the RAIN = TREMBLING!"

## 550. 期待 (きたい / kitai) — expectation, anticipation
**Pronunciation:** "KI-TAI — KEY TIE! I EXPECT the key tie!"
**Kanji:** 期 = period. 待 = wait. "WAITING for a PERIOD = EXPECTATION!"

## 551. 無事 (ぶじ / buji) — safely, peacefully
**Pronunciation:** "BU-JI — BOO GEE! BOO! But I'm SAFE!"
**Kanji:** 無 = nothing. 事 = thing. "NOTHING happened = SAFELY!"

## 552. 起こす (おこす / okosu) — to bring about, to cause
**Pronunciation:** "O-KO-SU — OH CALL SUE! CAUSE Sue to wake up!"
**Kanji:** 起 = rise. "CAUSING to RISE!"

## 553. 起こす (おこす / okosu) — to wake up
**Pronunciation:** "O-KO-SU — OH CALL SUE! WAKE UP, Sue!"
**Kanji:** 起 = rise (run + self). "SELF RISING = WAKING UP!"

## 554. 耳 (みみ / mimi) — ear, hearing
**Pronunciation:** "MI-MI — MY MY! My MY EARS!"
**Kanji:** 耳 looks like an EAR with folds inside!

## 555. 生まれる (うまれる / umareru) — to be born
**Pronunciation:** "U-MA-RE-RU — YOU MAry ROO! You're BORN to marry a kangaroo!"
**Kanji:** 生 = birth. "BIRTH = BEING BORN!"

## 556. 移動 (いどう / idou) — movement, shift
**Pronunciation:** "I-DO-U — I DO YOU! I SHIFT you!"
**Kanji:** 移 = shift (禾 grain + 多 many). 動 = move. "SHIFTING and MOVING!"

## 557. 邪魔 (じゃま / jama) — obstacle, hindrance
**Pronunciation:** "JA-MA — JAM IT! JAM an OBSTACLE!"
**Kanji:** 邪 = evil. 魔 = demon. "EVIL DEMON = OBSTACLE!"

## 558. 遊ぶ (あそぶ / asobu) — to play
**Pronunciation:** "A-SO-BU — A (a) SOBO (sobu)! A sobo who PLAYS!"
**Kanji:** 辶 (walk) + 方 (direction). "WALKING in any DIRECTION = PLAYING!"

## 559. 要る (いる / iru) — to need, to require
**Pronunciation:** "I-RU — EEL ROO! I NEED the eel kangaroo!"
**Kanji:** 要 = important (西 + 女). "IMPORTANT = NEEDED!"

## 560. 無くなる (なくなる / naku naru) — to be lost, used up
**Pronunciation:** "NA-KU-NA-RU — NAUGHTY CAR ROO! The naughty kangaroo is GONE!"
**Kanji:** 無 = nothing. "BECOMING NOTHING = USED UP!"

## 561. 唇 (くちびる / kuchibiru) — lip
**Pronunciation:** "KU-CHI-BI-RU — COOL CHEESE BEER on my LIP!"
**Kanji:** 辰 (dragon) + 肉 (flesh). "DRAGON FLESH = LIPS!"

## 562. 着る (きる / kiru) — to wear
**Pronunciation:** "KI-RU — KEY ROO! I WEAR the key kangaroo outfit!"
**Kanji:** 衣 (clothing) + 穴 (hole). "CLOTHING through a HOLE = WEARING!"

## 563. 色々 (いろいろ / iroiro) — various
**Pronunciation:** "I-RO-I-RO — EYE ROW EYE ROW! VARIOUS rows of eyes!"
**Kanji:** 色 = color. "MANY COLORS = VARIOUS!"

## 564. どうぞ (どうぞ / douzo) — go ahead
**Pronunciation:** "DOU-ZO — DO ZOO! GO AHEAD, do the zoo!"
**Kanji:** どうぞ: Please, GO AHEAD!

## 565. 立ち上がる (たちあがる / tachiagaru) — to stand up
**Pronunciation:** "TA-CHI-A-GA-RU — TIE CHEESE A GO ROO! STAND UP for cheese!"
**Kanji:** 立 = stand. 上がる = rise. "STANDING and RISING = STANDING UP!"

## 566. 思い (おもい / omoi) — thought
**Pronunciation:** "O-MOI — OH MY! What a THOUGHT!"
**Kanji:** 思 = think (field + heart). "THINKING from the HEART = THOUGHT!"

## 567. 以前 (いぜん / izen) — before, ago
**Pronunciation:** "I-ZEN — EYE ZEN! BEFORE zen, my eye saw!"
**Kanji:** 以 = by means of. 前 = before. "BEFORE this = AGO!"

## 568. もう少し (もうすこし / mou sukoshi) — a little more
**Pronunciation:** "MOU SU-KO-SHI — MORE SUE CALL SHE! A LITTLE MORE!"
**Kanji:** もう (more) + 少し (a little). "MORE + A LITTLE = A LITTLE MORE!"

## 569. 襲う (おそう / osou) — to assault, to attack
**Pronunciation:** "O-SOU — OH SEW! I'll SEW you = ATTACK!"
**Kanji:** 龍 (dragon) + 衣 (clothing). "DRAGON attacking with clothing = ASSAULT!"

## 570. 見つかる (みつかる / mitsukaru) — to be found
**Pronunciation:** "MI-TSU-KA-RU — MY TSU CAR ROO! My tsu-car is FOUND!"
**Kanji:** 見 = see. 付 = attach. "SEEN and ATTACHED = FOUND!"

## 571. 急ぐ (いそぐ / isogu) — to hurry
**Pronunciation:** "I-SO-GU — EYE SO GOOD! Hurry, my eye sees GOOD!"
**Kanji:** 心 (heart) + 又 (again). "HEART going AGAIN = HURRYING!"

## 572. せっかく (せっかく / sekkaku) — with much trouble, specially
**Pronunciation:** "SEK-KA-KU — SECURE COOL! SECURE it with MUCH TROUBLE!"
**Kanji:** せっかく: WITH GREAT EFFORT, SPECIALLY!

## 573. 降りる (おりる / oriru) — to get off, to go down
**Pronunciation:** "O-RI-RU — OH READ ROO! GET OFF and read, kangaroo!"
**Kanji:** 阝(hill) + 丰 (abundant). "Coming DOWN the hill!"

## 574. 下りる (おりる / oriru) — to go down, to come down
**Pronunciation:** "O-RI-RU — OH READ ROO! GO DOWN, kangaroo!"
**Kanji:** Same reading as 降りる. GOING DOWN, DESCENDING!

## 575. 切る (きる / kiru) — to cut
**Pronunciation:** "KI-RU — KEY ROO! CUT the key kangaroo!"
**Kanji:** 七 (seven) + 刂(knife). "SEVEN cuts with a KNIFE!"

## 576. 事件 (じけん / jiken) — affair, case
**Pronunciation:** "JI-KEN — GEE KEN! GEE, that's a CASE!"
**Kanji:** 事 = thing. 件 = matter. "THING + MATTER = an AFFAIR, CASE!"

## 577. 返事 (へんじ / henji) — reply
**Pronunciation:** "HEN-JI — HEN GEE! The HEN replies GEE!"
**Kanji:** 反 = reverse. 事 = thing. "REVERSING a THING = REPLY!"

## 578. 酷い (ひどい / hidoi) — terrible, cruel
**Pronunciation:** "HI-DO-I — HE DO EYE! He does a CRUEL eye!"
**Kanji:** 酉 (jar) + 告 (tell). "A TELLING from a JAR = CRUEL!"

## 579. 重い (おもい / omoi) — heavy
**Pronunciation:** "O-MOI — OH MY, it's HEAVY!"
**Kanji:** 重 has 千里 (thousand miles) stacked. "THOUSAND MILES = HEAVY!"

## 580. 起こる (おこる / okoru) — to occur, to happen
**Pronunciation:** "O-KO-RU — OH CALL ROO! Something OCCURS!"
**Kanji:** 起 = rise. "Something RISING = OCCURRING!"

## 581. 逆 (ぎゃく / gyaku) — reverse, inverse
**Pronunciation:** "GYA-KU — GUY COOL! A GUY in REVERSE!"
**Kanji:** 逆 has 辶 (walk) + 屰 (opposite). "WALKING the OPPOSITE way = REVERSE!"

## 582. 納得 (なっとく / nattoku) — consent, acceptance
**Pronunciation:** "NAT-TO-KU — NATTY COOL! I CONSENT to NATTY COOL!"
**Kanji:** 納 = accept (糸 thread + 入 enter). 得 = gain. "THREAD entering = ACCEPTING!"

## 583. 楽しむ (たのしむ / tanoshimu) — to enjoy
**Pronunciation:** "TA-NO-SHI-MU — TANGO SHIMMY! I ENJOY the tango shimmy!"
**Kanji:** 楽 = pleasure. "EXPERIENCING PLEASURE = ENJOYING!"

## 584. 叫ぶ (さけぶ / sakebu) — to shout, to cry out
**Pronunciation:** "SA-KE-BU — SAKE BUDDY! SAKE! I SHOUT!"
**Kanji:** 口 (mouth) + 斤 (axe). "MOUTH with an AXE = SHOUTING!"

## 585. 集まる (あつまる / atsumaru) — to gather
**Pronunciation:** "A-TSU-MA-RU — A TSU MY ROO! A tsu my kangaroo GATHERS!"
**Kanji:** 隹 (bird) + 木 (tree). "BIRDS on a TREE = GATHERING!"

## 586. 構う (かまう / kamau) — to mind, to care for
**Pronunciation:** "KA-MA-U — CAR MA YOU! Do you CARE about the car?"
**Kanji:** 木 (tree) + 亢 (high). "TREE growing HIGH = CARING for it!"

## 587. 王 (おう / ou) — king
**Pronunciation:** "OH — OH, the KING!"
**Kanji:** 王 has THREE horizontal strokes connected by a vertical = three levels of ROYALTY = KING!

## 588. 途中 (とちゅう / tochuu) — on the way, half way
**Pronunciation:** "TO-CHUU — TOE CHEW! ON THE WAY, I chew!"
**Kanji:** 途 = way (辶 walk). 中 = middle. "In the MIDDLE of the WAY!"

## 589. 慣れる (なれる / nareru) — to grow accustomed to
**Pronunciation:** "NA-RE-RU — NARU ROO! The kangaroo gets USED TO it!"
**Kanji:** 慣 has 忄(heart) + 貫 (pierce). "HEART being PIERCED = GETTING ACCUSTOMED!"

## 590. 終わり (おわり / owari) — end
**Pronunciation:** "O-WA-RI — OH WAR READ! The END of oh war read!"
**Kanji:** 終 = end (thread + winter). "THREAD reaching WINTER = THE END!"

## 591. 一応 (いちおう / ichiou) — for the time being
**Pronunciation:** "ICHI-OU — ITCHY OH! FOR NOW, itchy oh!"
**Kanji:** 一 = one. 応 = respond. "ONE RESPONSE = FOR THE TIME BEING!"

## 592. 引く (ひく / hiku) — to draw, to pull
**Pronunciation:** "HI-KU — HE COOL! He COOL-ly PULLS!"
**Kanji:** 弓 (bow) + │ (straight line). "PULLING a BOW = DRAWING!"

## 593. 海 (うみ / umi) — sea, ocean
**Pronunciation:** "U-MI — YOU ME at the SEA!"
**Kanji:** 海 has 氵(water) + 每 (every). "EVERY drop of WATER = the SEA!"

## 594. 諦める (あきらめる / akirameru) — to give up
**Pronunciation:** "A-KI-RA-ME-RU — A KEY RA ME ROO! I GIVE UP the key!"
**Kanji:** 諦 = truth (言 speech + 帝 emperor). "Speaking the emperor's truth = ACCEPTING, GIVING UP!"

## 595. 必ず (かならず / kanarazu) — always, certainly
**Pronunciation:** "KA-NA-RA-ZU — CANARY ZOO! The CANARY ALWAYS goes to the zoo!"
**Kanji:** 必 = certain (heart with slash). "CERTAINLY, WITHOUT EXCEPTION!"

## 596. 狙う (ねらう / nerau) — to aim, to be after
**Pronunciation:** "NE-RA-U — NAIL (ne) RA YOU! I AIM a nail at you!"
**Kanji:** 犭(animal) + 且 (moreover). "ANIMAL aiming = HUNTING, AIMING!"

## 597. いくら (いくら / ikura) — how much
**Pronunciation:** "I-KU-RA — EYE COOL RA! HOW MUCH is the eye?"
**Kanji:** いくら: HOW MUCH? WHAT PRICE?

## 598. 落とす (おとす / otosu) — to drop
**Pronunciation:** "O-TO-SU — OH TOE SUE! DROP the toe, Sue!"
**Kanji:** 艹 (grass) + 水 (water) + 口 (mouth). "Water FALLING from grass = DROPPING!"

## 599. 部分 (ぶぶん / bubun) — part, section
**Pronunciation:** "BU-BUN — BOO BUN! BOO! Just a PART of the bun!"
**Kanji:** 部 = part. 分 = divide. "DIVIDED PART = SECTION!"

## 600. 必死 (ひっし / hisshi) — frantic, desperate
**Pronunciation:** "HI-SSI — HE SIT! He sits DESPERATELY!"
**Kanji:** 必 = certain/death. 死 = die. "CERTAIN DEATH = DESPERATE!"

# Kaishi 1.5k Mnemonics — Words 601–700

---

## 601. 珍しい (めずらしい / mezurashii) — rare, uncommon
**Pronunciation:** "ME-ZU-RA-SHII — ME ZOO RARE SHE! My zoo has a RARE she!"
**Kanji:** 珍 has 玉 (jade) + 人 (person). "A JADE person = RARE!"

## 602. 意外 (いがい / igai) — unexpected, unforeseen
**Pronunciation:** "I-GAI — EYE GUY! The eye guy is UNEXPECTED!"
**Kanji:** 意 = idea. 外 = outside. "Ideas OUTSIDE expectations = UNEXPECTED!"

## 603. 報告 (ほうこく / houkoku) — report
**Pronunciation:** "HOU-KOKU — WHO CALLED? A REPORT!"
**Kanji:** 報 = report. 告 = tell. "REPORTING and TELLING = REPORT!"

## 604. 握る (にぎる / nigiru) — to grasp, to grip
**Pronunciation:** "NI-GI-RU — KNEE GEAR ROO! GRIP the knee gear!"
**Kanji:** 扌(hand) + 屋 (room). "HAND in the room = GRASPING!"

## 605. 結婚 (けっこん / kekkon) — marriage
**Pronunciation:** "KEK-KON — KICKER CON! KICKER's MARRIAGE convention!"
**Kanji:** 結 = tie. 婚 = marry (女 woman + 昏 dusk). "TYING the knot at DUSK = MARRIAGE!"

## 606. 戦い (たたかい / tatakai) — war, battle
**Pronunciation:** "TA-TA-KA-I — TIE TIE CAR EYE! BATTLE of tie-tie car eye!"
**Kanji:** 戦 = fight (weapon + single). "FIGHTING = BATTLE!"

## 607. 感謝 (かんしゃ / kansha) — appreciation, thanks
**Pronunciation:** "KAN-SHA — CAN SHE! CAN she give THANKS?"
**Kanji:** 感 = feel. 謝 = apologize/thank. "FEELING THANKFUL = APPRECIATION!"

## 608. 激しい (はげしい / hageshii) — intense, violent
**Pronunciation:** "HA-GE-SHII — HAZY SHE! HAZY and INTENSE!"
**Kanji:** 氵(water) + 敵 (enemy). "WATER like an ENEMY = VIOLENT!"

## 609. 倒れる (たおれる / taoreru) — to collapse
**Pronunciation:** "TA-O-RE-RU — TIE OH RAY ROO! COLLAPSE tie ray kangaroo!"
**Kanji:** 亻(person) + 到 (arrive). "Person ARRIVING at the ground = COLLAPSING!"

## 610. 既に (すでに / sude ni) — already, too late
**Pronunciation:** "SU-DE-NI — SUE DEAR KNEE! SUE is ALREADY dear!"
**Kanji:** 既 = already (艮 + ヨ). "ALREADY DONE, TOO LATE!"

## 611. 疲れる (つかれる / tsukareru) — to get tired
**Pronunciation:** "TSU-KA-RE-RU — TSU CAR RAY ROO! The tsu-car kangaroo is TIRED!"
**Kanji:** 疒(sickness) + 皮 (skin). "SICKNESS in your SKIN = TIRED!"

## 612. 生活 (せいかつ / seikatsu) — life, lifestyle
**Pronunciation:** "SEI-KA-TSU — SAY CAR TSU! SAY car tsu = LIFESTYLE!"
**Kanji:** 生 = life. 活 = live/active. "LIFE + ACTIVE = LIFESTYLE!"

## 613. 場 (ば / ba) — opportunity, occasion
**Pronunciation:** "BA — BUY! This is my OPPORTUNITY to BUY!"
**Kanji:** 土 (earth) + 昜 (bright). "A bright PLACE = OPPORTUNITY!"

## 614. 愛する (あいする / aisuru) — to love
**Pronunciation:** "AI-SU-RU — ICE RULE! I LOVE ice rules!"
**Kanji:** 愛 = love (claw + cover + heart + walking). "LOVE from the HEART!"

## 615. 冷たい (つめたい / tsumetai) — cold to the touch
**Pronunciation:** "TSU-ME-TA-I — TSU ME TIE EYE! COLD tsu me tie eye!"
**Kanji:** 冫(ice) + 正 (correct). "ICE that is CORRECTLY COLD!"

## 616. 捨てる (すてる / suteru) — to throw away
**Pronunciation:** "SU-TE-RU — SUE TEA ROO! THROW AWAY Sue's tea!"
**Kanji:** 扌(hand) + 舎 (house). "HAND throwing from the HOUSE = DISCARDING!"

## 617. 過ごす (すごす / sugosu) — to spend time
**Pronunciation:** "SU-GO-SU — SUE GO SUE! Sue SPENDS time going!"
**Kanji:** 辶 (walk) + 咼 (wrong). "WALKING through TIME = SPENDING!"

## 618. 食事 (しょくじ / shokuji) — meal, dinner
**pronunciation:** "SHO-KU-JI — SHOW COOL GEE! SHOW a COOL MEAL!"
**Kanji:** 食 = eat. 事 = thing. "EATING THING = MEAL!"

## 619. 働く (はたらく / hataraku) — to work
**Pronunciation:** "HA-TA-RA-KU — HOT (ha) TIRE (ta) RA COOL! WORK in hot tire!"
**Kanji:** 亻(person) + 動 (move). "A PERSON MOVING = WORKING!"

## 620. 答え (こたえ / kotae) — answer, response
**Pronunciation:** "KO-TA-E — CALL TAIL! The ANSWER is a call tail!"
**Kanji:** 竹 (bamboo) + 合 (fit). "FITTING under BAMBOO = ANSWER!"

## 621. 風 (かぜ / kaze) — wind
**pronunciation:** "KA-ZE — CAR ZEBRA! The WIND blows the car zebra!"
**Kanji:** 風 has 几 (table) + 虫 (insect). "INSECTS blown by WIND!"

## 622. 黒い (くろい / kuroi) — black, dark
**pronunciation:** "KU-RO-I — COOL ROW EYE! A COOL BLACK eye!"
**Kanji:** 黒 has 黒 (black) = a pot over a fire turning BLACK!

## 623. 助かる (たすかる / tasukaru) — to be saved
**Pronunciation:** "TA-SU-KA-RU — TIE SU CAR ROO! The tie-sue car is SAVED!"
**Kanji:** 且 + 力 (power). "With POWER = being SAVED!"

## 624. 受け取る (うけとる / uketoru) — to receive words
**Pronunciation:** "U-KE-TO-RU — YOU KEY TOE ROO! YOU receive the key toe!"
**Kanji:** 受 = receive. 取 = take. "RECEIVING and TAKING = ACCEPTING words!"

## 625. 足りる (たりる / tariru) — to be enough
**Pronunciation:** "TA-RI-RU — TIE READ ROO! That's ENOUGH reading!"
**Kanji:** 足 = foot/enough. "FOOT = ENOUGH!"

## 626. 判断 (はんだん / handan) — judgment, decision
**Pronunciation:** "HAN-DAN — HAND DAN! DAN makes the JUDGMENT!"
**Kanji:** 判 = judge (half + knife). 断 = sever. "JUDGING and SEVERING = DECISION!"

## 627. 未来 (みらい / mirai) — future
**Pronunciation:** "MI-RA-I — MY RYE EYE! MY FUTURE rye eye!"
**Kanji:** 未 = not yet. 来 = come. "NOT YET COMING = FUTURE!"

## 628. 食う (くう / kuu) — to eat (casual)
**Pronunciation:** "KUU — COO! I EAT and COO!"
**Kanji:** 食 (eat) simplified. "EATING = 食う!"

## 629. 分 (ぶん / bun) — amount, share
**Pronunciation:** "BU-NO — BOO! That's my SHARE!"
