**Kanji:** 分 = divide (八 + 刀). "DIVIDED = a SHARE, an AMOUNT!"

## 630. そっち (そっち / sotchi) — there
**Pronunciation:** "SOT-CHI — SOCK CHEESE over THERE!"
**Kanji:** それ (that) + っち (casual direction). "Over THERE!"

## 631. 上手い (うまい / umai) — skilled, good (at doing)
**Pronunciation:** "U-MAI — YOU MY! You're GOOD at that!"
**Kanji:** 上 = up. 手 = hand. "HAND going UP = SKILLED!"

## 632. 合わせる (あわせる / awaseru) — to adjust, to match
**Pronunciation:** "A-WA-SE-RU — A WASH ROO! ADJUST the wash kangaroo!"
**Kanji:** 合 = fit. "FITTING together = MATCHING!"

## 633. 大切 (たいせつ / taisetsu) — important, valuable
**Pronunciation:** "TAI-SETS — TIE SETS! TIE sets are IMPORTANT!"
**Kanji:** 大 = big. 切 = cut. "BIG CUT = IMPORTANT!"

## 634. 花 (はな / hana) — flower
**Pronunciation:** "HA-NA — HA NA! HA, a FLOWER!"
**Kanji:** 艹 (grass) + 化 (change). "GRASS CHANGING into a FLOWER!"

## 635. 謝る (あやまる / ayamaru) — to apologize
**Pronunciation:** "A-YA-MA-RU — AH YAMA ROO! AH, the mountain kangaroo APOLOGIZES!"
**Kanji:** 言 (speech) + 射 (shoot). "SPEECH shooting out = APOLOGIZING!"

## 636. 奪う (うばう / ubau) — to snatch, to take by force
**Pronunciation:** "U-BA-U — YOU BOW YOU! SNATCH your bow!"
**Kanji:** 大 (big) + 冖(cover) + 又 (hand). "BIG HAND SNATCHING!"

## 637. 勢い (いきおい / ikioi) — vigor, momentum
**Pronunciation:** "I-KI-O-I — EYE KEY OH EYE! The EYE has VIGOR!"
**Kanji:** 勢 = force/vigor. "FORCE and ENERGY = MOMENTUM!"

## 638. 涙 (なみだ / namida) — tear
**Pronunciation:** "NA-MI-DA — NAH MY DA! NAH, my TEARS!"
**Kanji:** 氵(water) + 目 (eye) + 大 (big). "BIG WATER from EYES = TEARS!"

## 639. 雰囲気 (ふんいき / fun'iki) — atmosphere, mood
**Pronunciation:** "FUN-I-KI — FUN KEY! The ATMOSPHERE has a fun key!"
**Kanji:** 雰 = atmosphere. 囲気 = surrounding spirit. "SURROUNDING SPIRIT = MOOD!"

## 640. 嘘 (うそ / uso) — lie
**Pronunciation:** "U-SO — YOU SO! YOU SO LIED!"
**Kanji:** 虍 (tiger) + 业 (business). "A TIGER's BUSINESS = a LIE!"

## 641. 望む (のぞむ / nozomu) — to desire, to hope
**Pronunciation:** "NO-ZO-MU — NO ZOO MOO! I HOPE there's no zoo moo!"
**Kanji:** 亡 (lost) + 月 (moon) + 王 (king). "LOST MOON and KING = DESIRING!"

## 642. 緊張 (きんちょう / kinchou) — tension, nervousness
**Pronunciation:** "KIN-CHOU — KIN CHOW! My KIN is nervous at CHOW!"
**Kanji:** 緊 = tight (糸 thread + 緊 tight). 張 = stretch. "TIGHT and STRETCHED = NERVOUS!"

## 643. 一緒 (いっしょ / issho) — together
**Pronunciation:** "I-SSHO — ISHO! Let's GO TOGETHER!"
**Kanji:** 一 = one. 隻 = bird (counter). "ONE BIRD = TOGETHER!"

## 644. 救う (すくう / sukuu) — to save
**Pronunciation:** "SU-KUU — SUE COO! SUE COOS and SAVES!"
**Kanji:** 求 (seek) + 攵 (hand). "SEEKING with a HAND = SAVING!"

## 645. 自ら (みずから / mizukara) — oneself
**Pronunciation:** "MI-ZU-KA-RA — MY ZOO CAR! MYSELF and my zoo car!"
**Kanji:** 自 = self. "By ONESELF, on one's OWN!"

## 646. 地面 (じめん / jimen) — surface, ground
**Pronunciation:** "JI-MEN — GEE MEN! GEE, the GROUND men!"
**Kanji:** 地 = ground. 面 = surface. "GROUND SURFACE!"

## 647. 失敗 (しっぱい / shippai) — failure, blunder
**Pronunciation:** "SHIP-PAI — SHIP PIE! The SHIP PIE is a FAILURE!"
**Kanji:** 失 = lose. 敗 = defeat. "LOSING and DEFEATING = FAILURE!"

## 648. 避ける (さける / sakeru) — to avoid
**Pronunciation:** "SA-KE-RU — SAKE ROO! AVOID the sake kangaroo!"
**Kanji:** 辶 (walk) + 辟 (avoid). "WALKING to AVOID!"

## 649. 窓 (まど / mado) — window
**Pronunciation:** "MA-DO — MY DOOR! My DOOR window!"
**Kanji:** 穴 (hole) + 心 (heart) + 囗 (enclosure). "A HOLE in the wall with a HEART = WINDOW!"

## 650. 素直 (すなお / sunao) — honest, straightforward
**Pronunciation:** "SU-NA-O — SUE NA OH! SUE is HONEST — NAH OH!"
**Kanji:** 素 = plain. 直 = straight. "PLAIN and STRAIGHT = HONEST!"

## 651. 肉 (にく / niku) — flesh, meat
**Pronunciation:** "NI-KU — KNEE COOL! Cool MEAT on my knee!"
**Kanji:** 肉 looks like a body with flesh inside!

## 652. 冗談 (じょうだん / joudan) — joke
**Pronunciation:** "JOU-DAN — JOE DAN! JOE DAN tells a JOKE!"
**Kanji:** 冗 = redundant. 談 = discuss. "REDUNDANT discussion = JOKE!"

## 653. 余裕 (よゆう / yoyuu) — room, leeway
**Pronunciation:** "YO-YUU — YO YOO! YO, you have LEEWAY!"
**Kanji:** 余 = surplus. 裕 = rich. "SURPLUS RICHES = LEEWAY!"

## 654. 連絡 (れんらく / renraku) — connection, communication
**Pronunciation:** "REN-RAKU — WRANGLER! WRANGLING a CONNECTION!"
**Kanji:** 連 = link. 絡 = tie. "LINKING and TYING = COMMUNICATION!"

## 655. 人生 (じんせい / jinsei) — (human) life
**Pronunciation:** "JIN-SEI — JEAN SAY! JEAN says about LIFE!"
**Kanji:** 人 = person. 生 = life. "PERSON's LIFE!"

## 656. 放つ (はなつ / hanatsu) — to emit, to set free
**Pronunciation:** "HA-NA-TSU — HA NAUGHTY! SET FREE the naughty!"
**Kanji:** 方 (direction) + 攵 (hand). "HAND in a DIRECTION = RELEASING!"

## 657. 流れる (ながれる / nagareru) — to flow, to pass
**Pronunciation:** "NA-GA-RE-RU — NAH GO RAY ROO! The water FLOWS!"
**Kanji:** 氵(water) + 㐬 (flow). "WATER FLOWING!"

## 658. 特別 (とくべツ / tokubetsu) — special, extraordinary
**Pronunciation:** "TO-KU-BETSU — TOE COOL BETS! A SPECIAL toe bet!"
**Kanji:** 特 = special. 別 = separate. "SPECIALLY SEPARATE = EXTRAORDINARY!"

## 659. 連中 (れんちゅう / renchuu) — party, this bunch
**Pronunciation:** "REN-CHUU — WRANGLER CHEW! This BUNCH of wranglers!"
**Kanji:** 連 = link. 中 = middle. "LINKED in the MIDDLE = this BUNCH!"

## 660. 両手 (りょうて / ryoute) — both hands
**Pronunciation:** "RYOU-TE — RULE TEA! RULE with BOTH HANDS!"
**Kanji:** 両 = both. 手 = hand. "BOTH HANDS!"

## 661. 増える (ふえる / fueru) — to increase
**pronunciation:** "FU-E-RU — FOOD EEL ROO! FOOD increases for the eel!"
**Kanji:** 増 has 土 (earth) + 曾 (once). "EARTH growing ONCE MORE = INCREASING!"

## 662. 出会う (であう / deau) — to meet by chance
**Pronunciation:** "DE-A-U — DAY AH YOU! I MET you by CHANCE today!"
**Kanji:** 出 = go out. 会 = meet. "GOING OUT and MEETING = ENCOUNTERING!"

## 663. 少年 (しょうねん / shounen) — boy
**Pronunciation:** "SHOU-NEN — SHOW NEN! The SHOW boy!"
**Kanji:** 少 = few/small. 年 = year. "SMALL YEARS = YOUNG BOY!"

## 664. 繰り返す (くりかえす / kurikaesu) — to repeat
**Pronunciation:** "KU-RI-KA-E-SU — COOL RECAR SUE! Sue REPEATS the cool recar!"
**Kanji:** 繰 = spin. 返 = return. "SPINNING BACK = REPEATING!"

## 665. 我慢 (がまん / gaman) — patience, bearing with
**Pronunciation:** "GA-MAN — GUY MAN! The GUY MAN has PATIENCE!"
**Kanji:** 我 = self. 慢 = slow. "SELF being SLOW = PATIENCE!"

## 666. 我々 (われわれ / wareware) — we (formal)
**Pronunciation:** "WA-RE-WA-RE — WARE WARE! We are the WARE WARE!"
**Kanji:** 我 = I. "I and I = WE (formally)!"

## 667. 感覚 (かんかく / kankaku) — sensation
**Pronunciation:** "KAN-KAKU — CAN COOL! CAN feel the SENSATION!"
**Kanji:** 感 = feel. 覚 = perceive. "FEELING and PERCEIVING = SENSATION!"

## 668. 響く (ひびく / hibiku) — to reverberate
**Pronunciation:** "HI-BI-KU — HE BEE COOL! His bee rings COOL — REVERBERATES!"
**Kanji:** 音 (sound) + 響 (echo). "SOUND ECHOING = REVERBERATING!"

## 669. 何とか (なんとか / nantoka) — somehow, please
**Pronunciation:** "NAN-TO-KA — NAN TOE CAR! SOMEHOW nan toe car!"
**Kanji:** 何とか: SOMEHOW, ONE WAY OR ANOTHER!

## 670. 山 (やま / yama) — mountain
**Pronunciation:** "YA-MA — YA MOUNTAIN!"
**Kanji:** 山 looks like THREE PEAKS of a MOUNTAIN!

## 671. 当たり前 (あたりまえ / atarimae) — of course, obvious
**Pronunciation:** "A-TA-RI-MA-E — ATARI MA EYE! ATARI is OF COURSE obvious!"
**Kanji:** 当 = hit. 前 = before. "HITTING the FRONT = OBVIOUS!"

## 672. 予定 (よてい / yotei) — schedule, plan
**Pronunciation:** "YO-TEI — YOUR TED! Your TED's SCHEDULE!"
**Kanji:** 予 = beforehand. 定 = decide. "DECIDED BEFOREHAND = SCHEDULE!"

## 673. 残念 (ざんねん / zannen) — regrettable
**Pronunciation:** "ZAN-NEN — ZANY NEN! How ZANY — how REGRETTABLE!"
**Kanji:** 残 = remain. 念 = thought. "REMAINING THOUGHTS = REGRET!"

## 674. 追う (おう / ou) — to chase
**pronunciation:** "OH — OH! CHASE HIM!"
**Kanji:** 辶 (walk) + 自 (self). "WALKING SELF = CHASING!"

## 675. 綺麗 (きれい / kirei) — beautiful, clean
**Pronunciation:** "KI-REI — KEY RAY! A BEAUTIFUL key ray!"
**Kanji:** 綺 = beautiful silk. 靡 = beautiful. "BEAUTIFUL silk = CLEAN!"

## 676. いらっしゃる (いらっしゃる / irassharu) — honorific (be/come/go)
**Pronunciation:** "I-RA-SSHA-RU — I RULE SHARE ROO! I HUMBLY share!"
**Kanji:** Most polite form of いる/行く/来る. "HUMBLY BEING, COMING, GOING!"

## 677. 若い (わかい / wakai) — young
**pronunciation:** "WA-KA-I — WALK EYE! YOUNG people WALK and see!"
**Kanji:** 若 has 艹 (grass) + 右 (right). "GRASS on the RIGHT = YOUNG growth!"

## 678. どうしても (どうしても / doushitemo) — by any means, really
**Pronunciation:** "DOUSHITEMO — DO SHE TEA MORE! BY ANY MEANS!"
**Kanji:** どうして (why) + も (also). "WHY + ALSO = BY ANY MEANS!"

## 679. 考え (かんがえ / kangae) — thought, view
**Pronunciation:** "KAN-GA-E — CAN GUY! CAN a guy have THOUGHTS?"
**Kanji:** 考 = think (old + bent). "THINKING deeply = THOUGHT!"

## 680. 過去 (かこ / kako) — the past
**Pronunciation:** "KA-KO — CAR CALL! CAR called in THE PAST!"
**Kanji:** 過 = pass. 去 = go. "PASSED and GONE = THE PAST!"

## 681. 気配 (けはい / kehai) — indication, sign
**Pronunciation:** "KE-HAI — KEY HI! KEY says HI — a SIGN!"
**Kanji:** 気 = spirit. 配 = arrange. "SPIRIT ARRANGED = a SIGN!"

## 682. 本人 (ほんにん / honnin) — the person in question
**pronunciation:** "HON-NIN — HON NINJA! The HON ninja is THE PERSON!"
**Kanji:** 本 = true/origin. 人 = person. "The TRUE PERSON = THE ONE IN QUESTION!"

## 683. 現実 (げんじつ / genjitsu) — reality, actuality
**Pronunciation:** "GEN-JI-TSU — GEN GEE TSU! GEN's REALITY!"
**Kanji:** 現 = appear. 実 = truth. "APPEARING TRUTH = REALITY!"

## 684. 決して (けっして / kesshite) — never, by no means
**Pronunciation:** "KE-SSHU-TE — KISS SHE TEA! I'll NEVER kiss her tea!"
**Kanji:** 決 = decide. "ABSOLUTELY DECIDED = NEVER!"

## 685. いつか (いつか / itsuka) — some time, some day
**Pronunciation:** "I-TSU-KA — EYE TSU CAR! SOME DAY the eye tsu car!"
**Kanji:** いつ (when) + か (question). "WHEN + SOME = SOME DAY!"

## 686. 床 (ゆか / yuka) — floor
**pronunciation:** "YU-KA — YOU CAR! Your car is on the FLOOR!"
**Kanji:** 床 has 广 (building) + 木 (tree). "BUILDING with TREES = FLOOR boards!"

## 687. 横 (よこ / yoko) — side, width
**pronunciation:** "YO-KO — YO COOL! Stand to the SIDE!"
**Kanji:** 木 (tree) + 黄 (yellow). "TREE to the YELLOW SIDE!"

**pronunciation:** "YA-SU-MU — YAH SUE MOO! YAH, Sue RESTS and MOOS!"
**Kanji:** 亻(person) + 木 (tree). "PERSON leaning on a TREE = RESTING!"

**Pronunciation:** "FU-RI-KA-E-RU — FOOD RECAR ROO! LOOK BACK at the food recar!"
**Kanji:** 振 = swing. 返る = return. "SWINGING BACK = LOOKING BACK!"

**pronunciation:** "WA-ZA-WA-ZA — WALK WALK WAZA! Going out of your WAY!"
**Kanji:** わざわざ: GOING OUT OF YOUR WAY, DELIBERATELY!"

**pronunciation:** "SHI-TSU-MON — SHE TSU MON! She asks a QUESTION!"
**Kanji:** 質 = substance. 問 = ask. "SUBSTANCE to ASK = QUESTION!"

## 692. 興味 (きょうみ / kyoumi) — interest
**pronunciation:** "KYOU-MI — CUTE ME! I have INTEREST in cute me!"
**Kanji:** 興 = excitement. 味 = taste. "EXCITING TASTE = INTEREST!"

**Kanji:** 足 (foot) + 巨 (huge). "HUGE FOOTSTEP = DISTANCE!"

**Kanji:** 内 = inside. 容 = contain. "INSIDE CONTENT = CONTENTS!"

**pronunciation:** "TSUI — CHEWY! I INADVERTENTLY ate chewy!"
**Kanji:** つい: INADVERTENTLY, CARELESSLY!"

**Kanji:** 囗 (enclosure) + 大 (big). "BIG thing going AROUND!"

**Kanji:** 二 (two) + ノ (diagonal). "TWO in a ROW = LINING UP!"

**Kanji:** 糸 (thread) + 冬 (winter). "THREAD reaching WINTER = ENDING!"

**Kanji:** 態 = attitude. 度 = degree. "Degree of MANNER = ATTITUDE!"

**Pronunciation:** "KA-NE — CAR KNEE! CAR KNEE = MONEY!"
**Kanji:** 金 has 人 (person) on top and dots below = METALS = MONEY!

**pronunciation:** "TA-CHI-BA — TIE CHEESE BUY! TIE cheese BUYER's standpoint!"
**Kanji:** 立 = stand. 場 = place. "STANDING PLACE = STANDPOINT!"

## 793. 石 (いし / ishi) — stone, small rock
**pronunciation:** "I-SHI — EYE SHE! EYE sees a STONE!"
**Kanji:** 石 looks like a STONE with a chisel mark!

## 794. 挨拶 (あいさつ / aisatsu) — greeting
**pronunciation:** "AI-SA-TSU — EYE SAW TSU! EYE SAW TSU = GREETING!"
**Kanji:** 扌(hand) + 矢 (arrow). "HAND + ARROW = GREETING gesture!"

## 795. 相談 (そうだん / soudan) — consultation, advice
**pronunciation:** "SOU-DAN — SOUL DAN! SOUL DAN gives ADVICE!"
**Kanji:** 相 = mutual. 談 = discuss. "MUTUAL DISCUSSION = CONSULTATION!"

## 796. 受け入れる (うけいれる / ukeireru) — to accept
**pronunciation:** "U-KE-I-RE-RU — YOU KEY EAT ROO! YOU ACCEPT the key food!"
**Kanji:** 受 = receive. 入 = enter. "RECEIVING and ENTERING = ACCEPTING!"

## 797. 練習 (れんしゅう / renshuu) — practice, training
**pronunciation:** "REN-SHUU — WRANGLER SHOE! PRACTICE the wrangler shoe!"
**Kanji:** 練 = refine (糸 thread + 束 bundle). 習 = learn. "REFINING and LEARNING = PRACTICE!"

## 798. 無駄 (むだ / muda) — wasteful, useless
**pronunciation:** "MU-DA — MOODA! MOODA is WASTEFUL!"
**Kanji:** 無 = nothing. 駄 = bad horse. "NOTHING + BAD = WASTEFUL!"

## 799. 抱える (かかえる / kakaeru) — to hold in one's arms
**pronunciation:** "KA-KA-E-RU — CAR CAR EEL ROO! HOLD the car eel!"
**Kanji:** 扌(hand) + 卨 (wrap). "HAND wrapping = HOLDING!"

## 800. 示す (しめす / shimesu) — to show, to indicate
**pronunciation:** "SHI-ME-SU — SHE MAY SUE! SHE MAY SHOW SUE!"
**Kanji:** 二 (two) + 小 (small) + 木 (tree). "TWO small TREES = SHOWING a sign!"

## 801. 成功 (せいこう / seikou) — success
**pronunciation:** "SEI-KOU — SAY COAL! SAY SUCCESS!"
**Kanji:** 成 = accomplish. 功 = merit. "ACCOMPLISHING + MERIT = SUCCESS!"

**Kanji:** 扌(hand) + 包 (wrap). "HAND wrapping = HUGGING!"

**Kanji:** 口 (mouth) + 未 (not yet). "MOUTH that has NOT YET tasted = TASTE!"

## 804. 男性 (だんせい / dansei) — male, man

**Kanji:** 男 = male (field + power). 性 = gender. "MALE GENDER!"

## 805. 否定 (ひてい / hitei) — denial, negation

**Kanji:** 否 = no. 定 = decide. "DECIDED NO = DENIAL!"

**Kanji:** 至 (reach) + 攵 (hand). "REACHING with HAND = DOING humbly!"

**pronunciation:** "GEN-ZAI — GEN ZAI! GEN lives in the PRESENT!"
**Kanji:** 現 = appear. 在 = exist. "APPEARING + EXISTING = NOW!"

## 808. 輝く (かがやく / kagayaku) — to glitter, shine
**pronunciation:** "KA-GA-YA-KU — CAR GUY YAKU! The car guy SHINES!"
**Kanji:** 車 (car) + 光 (light). "CAR with LIGHT = SHINING!"

## 809. 熱 (ねつ / netsu) — fever

**Kanji:** 埶 + 灬 (fire). "Fire + energy = FEVER!"

## 810. 始め (はじめ / hajime) — beginning, origin

**Kanji:** 女 (woman) + 台 (stand). "WOMAN STANDING at the START!"

**Kanji:** 厂 (cliff) + 敢 (dare). "DARING on a cliff = STRICT!"

## 812. 匂い (におい / nioi) — smell, odor

**Kanji:** 勹 (wrap) + 大 (big). "BIG WRAPPED SMELL = ODOR!"

**Kanji:** 巾 (cloth) + 廿 (20) + 系. "CLOTH on a seat!"

**Kanji:** 士 (samurai) + 冖 (cover). "SAMURAI covering = SELLING!"

**Kanji:** 艹 (grass) + 日 (day) + 耂 (old). "Grass + day + old = LIVING life!"

**Kanji:** 利 = profit. 用 = use. "PROFITABLE USE = UTILIZATION!"

**Kanji:** ふと: SUDDENLY, UNINTENTIONALLY!"

**Kanji:** 経 = pass through. 験 = test. "PASSING through TESTING = EXPERIENCE!"

**Kanji:** 日 (sun) + 月 (moon). "SUN + MOON = BRIGHT!"

**pronunciation:** "TA-TE-MO-NO — TIE TEAM NO! TIE TEAM BUILDING!"
**Kanji:** 建 = build. 物 = thing. "BUILT THING = BUILDING!"

**pronunciation:** "HON-RAI — HON RIDE! ORIGINALLY HON RIDE!"
**Kanji:** 本 = root. 来 = come. "ROOT + COMING = ORIGINALLY!"

**pronunciation:** "A-TSU-KA-U — A TSU CAR YOU! HANDLE the tsu car!"
**Kanji:** 扌(hand) + 刈 (cut). "HAND + CUT = HANDLING!"

## 1005. 真面目 (まじめ / majime) — serious, earnest
**pronunciation:** "MA-JI-ME — MY GEE MAY! MY GEE is SERIOUS!"
**Kanji:** 真 = truth. 目 = eye. 面 = face. "TRUTH + EYE + FACE = EARNEST!"

**Kanji:** 影 = shadow. 響 = echo. "SHADOW + ECHO = INFLUENCE!"

**Kanji:** 真 = true. 赤 = red. "TRUE RED = DEEP RED!"

**Kanji:** 支 = support (branch + hand). "HAND holding BRANCH = SUPPORTING!"

**Kanji:** 姉 = older sister + さん (honorific). "HONORABLE SISTER!"

**Kanji:** おば (aunt) + さん (honorific). "HONORABLE AUNT!"

**Kanji:** 車 looks like a CAR from above!

**Kanji:** 事 = thing. 態 = condition. "THING + CONDITION = SITUATION!"

**Kanji:** 味 = taste. 方 = direction. "TASTE + DIRECTION = ALLY!"

**Kanji:** 展 = spread. 開 = open. "SPREADING + OPENING = UNFOLDING!"

**Kanji:** 土 (earth) + 刂 (knife). "EARTH + KNIFE = MODEL!"

**Kanji:** 日 = day + 日 = day. "DAY + DAY = DAILY!"

**Kanji:** 行 = go. 為 = do. "GOING + DOING = ACTING!"

**Kanji:** ともかく: AT LEAST, ANYWAY!"

**Kanji:** 氵(water) + 亡 (lost). "WATER LOST = DECREASING!"

**Kanji:** 知 = know. 合 = fit. "KNOWING + FITTING = ACQUAINTANCE!"

**Kanji:** 天 looks like HEAVEN above the sky!

**Kanji:** いつの間にか: UNNOTICED, BEFORE ONE KNOWS!"

**Kanji:** 直 = straight. "STRAIGHTENING = REPAIRING!"

**Kanji:** 常 = regular. "REGULARLY = ALWAYS!"

**Kanji:** 犯 = commit crime. 人 = person. "CRIME + PERSON = CRIMINAL!"

**Kanji:** 許 = permit. 可 = possible. "PERMIT + POSSIBLE = PERMISSION!"

**Kanji:** 門 (gate) + 才 (talent). "GATE + TALENT = CLOSING!"

## 1075. 細い (ほそい / hosoi) — thin, narrow

**Kanji:** 糸 (thread) + 田 (rice field). "THREAD + RICE = THIN!"

**pronunciation:** "HI-SA-SHI-BU-RI — HE SA SHOE BUDDY READ! IT'S BEEN A WHILE!"
**Kanji:** 久 = long time. "LONG TIME = AFTER A WHILE!"

## 1077. 目立つ (めだつ / medatsu) — to stand out
**pronunciation:** "ME-DA-TSU — MAY DICE TSU! MAY DICE = STAND OUT!"
**Kanji:** 目 = eye. 立 = stand. "EYE STANDING = CONSPICUOUS!"

**Kanji:** 到 = arrive. 着 = wear/arrive. "ARRIVING = ARRIVAL!"

**Kanji:** 扌(hand) + 反 (reverse). "HAND REVERSING = HOLDING DOWN!"

**Kanji:** 殳 (weapon) + 几 (table). "WEAPON + TABLE = STRIKING!"

## 1082. 広げる (ひろげる / hirogeru) — to spread

**Kanji:** 田 (rice field) + 力 (power). "RICE + POWER = TURN!"

## 1088. 戦争 (せんそう / sensou) — war

**Kanji:** 戦 = fight. 争 = contend. "FIGHTING + CONTENDING = WAR!"

## 1089. 寒い (さむい / samui) — cold

**Kanji:** 宀 (roof) + 二 (two) + 丶 (dot). "ROOF + TWO + DOT = COLD!"

## 1090. 見事 (みごと / migoto) — splendid, admirable
**pronunciation:** "MI-GO-TO — MY GO TOE! MY GO TOE = SPLENDID!"
**Kanji:** 見 = see. 事 = thing. "SEEING THING = SPLENDID!"

## 1091. 酒 (さけ / sake) — alcoholic drink

**Kanji:** 氵(water) + 酉 (bird/wine). "WATER + WINE = SAKE!"

## 1092. 恋人 (こいびと / koibito) — lover, sweetheart
**pronunciation:** "KO-I-BI-TO — CALL EAT BEE TOE! LOVER calls eat bee toe!"
**Kanji:** 恋 = love. 人 = person. "LOVE PERSON = LOVER!"

## 1093. 一切 (いっさい / issai) — not at all, nothing
**pronunciation:** "IT-SAI — EAT SAI! EAT SAI = NOTHING!"

## 1094. 穴 (あな / ana) — hole

**Kanji:** 穴 has 穴 (cave) + 八 (eight). "CAVE + EIGHT = HOLE!"

## 1095. 依頼 (いらい / irai) — request, commission
**pronunciation:** "I-RAI — EAT RYE! EAT RYE = REQUEST!"
**Kanji:** 依 = depend. 頼 = rely. "DEPENDING + RELYING = REQUESTING!"

## 1096. 中身 (なかみ / nakami) — content, interior
**pronunciation:** "NA-KA-MI — NAH CAR MY! NAH CAR MY = CONTENT!"

## 1097. 多少 (たしょう / tashou) — a little, somewhat
**pronunciation:** "TA-SHOU — TIE SHOW! TIE SHOW = A LITTLE!"
**Kanji:** 多 = many. 少 = few. "MANY + FEW = SOMEWHAT!"

## 1098. 真実 (しんじつ / shinjitsu) — truth, reality
**pronunciation:** "SHIN-JI-TSU — SHEEN GEE TSU! SHEEN GEE = TRUTH!"
**Kanji:** 真 = true. 実 = truth. "TRUE + TRUTH = REALITY!"

## 1099. 見た目 (みため / mitame) — outward appearance

**Kanji:** 見 = see. 目 = eye. "SEEING + EYE = APPEARANCE!"

## 1100. 間違い (まちがい / machigai) — mistake, error
**pronunciation:** "MA-CHI-GA-I — MY CHEESE GUY! MY CHEESE GUY = MISTAKE!"
**Kanji:** 間 = interval. 違 = differ. "DIFFERING INTERVAL = MISTAKE!"

# Kaishi 1.5k Mnemonics — Words 1101–1200

---

## 1101. 青い (あおい / aoi) — blue
**pronunciation:** "A-O-I — A OH EYE! A BLUE eye!"
**Kanji:** 青 = blue (green + earth). "BLUE = GREEN EARTH!"

## 1102. 辛い (からい / karai) — spicy
**pronunciation:** "KA-RA-I — CAR RYE EYE! CAR RYE is SPICY!"
**Kanji:** 辛 = bitter/spicy. "BITTER = SPICY!"

## 1103. 辛い (つらい / tsurai) — difficult, harsh
**pronunciation:** "TSU-RA-I — TSU RYE EYE! TSU RYE is HARSH!"
**Kanji:** Same kanji as 1102. "BITTER = DIFFICULT, HARSH!"

## 1104. 対応 (たいおう / taiou) — response, correspondence
**pronunciation:** "TAI-OU — TIE OH! TIE OH = RESPONSE!"
**Kanji:** 対 = opposite. 応 = respond. "OPPOSITE + RESPOND = CORRESPONDENCE!"

## 1301. 部下 (ぶか / buka) — subordinate

**Kanji:** 部 = part. 下 = below. "PART + BELOW = SUBORDINATE!"

# Kaishi 1.5k Mnemonics — Words 1401–1500

---


## 1401. 祈る (いのる / inoru) — to pray
**Pronunciation:** "I-NO-RU — EAT NO ROO! EAT NO ROO = PRAY!"
**Kanji:** 礻(spirit) + 斤 (axe). "SPIRIT + AXE = PRAYING!"

## 1402. 午後 (ごご / gogo) — afternoon
**Pronunciation:** "GO-GO — GO GO! GO GO = AFTERNOON!"
**Kanji:** 午 = noon. 後 = after. "NOON + AFTER = AFTERNOON!"

## 1403. 同様 (どうよう / douyou) — similar
**Pronunciation:** "DOU-YOU — DO YOUR! DO YOUR = SIMILAR!"
**Kanji:** 同 = same. 様 = manner. "SAME + MANNER = SIMILAR!"

## 1404. 外れる (はずれる / hazureru) — to come undone
**Pronunciation:** "HA-ZU-RE-RU — HAZEL RAY ROO! HAZEL RAY ROO = COME UNDONE!"
**Kanji:** 外 = outside. "GOING OUTSIDE = BEING REMOVED!"

## 1405. 歌う (うたう / utau) — to sing
**Pronunciation:** "U-TA-U — YOU TIE YOU! YOU TIE YOU = SING!"
**Kanji:** 哥 (sing). "SINGING!"

## 1406. 回 (かい / kai) — times, counter
**Pronunciation:** "KAI — KEY! KEY = TIMES!"
**Kanji:** 囗 (enclosure) + 大 (big). "TURNING = TIMES!"

## 1407. 固い (かたい / katai) — stiff, tight
**Pronunciation:** "KA-TA-I — CAR TIE EYE! CAR TIE EYE = STIFF!"
**Kanji:** 囗 (enclosure) + 古 (old). "ENCLOSURE + OLD = STIFF!"

**Pronunciation:** "SAI-KYOU — SAI CUTE! SAI CUTE = STRONGEST!"
**Kanji:** 最 = most. 強 = strong. "MOST + STRONG = STRONGEST!"

**Pronunciation:** "HI-KU — HE COOL! HE COOL = STRUM!"
**Kanji:** 弓 (bow) + 貫 (pierce). "BOW + PIERCE = FLICKING!"

## 1410. 正解 (せいかい / seikai) — right answer
**Pronunciation:** "SEI-KAI — SAY KEY! SAY KEY = CORRECT!"
**Kanji:** 正 = correct. 解 = untie. "CORRECT + UNTYING = RIGHT ANSWER!"

## 1411. きつい (きつい / kitsui) — tight, tough
**Pronunciation:** "KI-TSU-I — KEY TSU EYE! KEY TSU EYE = TIGHT!"
**Kanji:** きつい. "TIGHT, TOUGH!"

## 1412. お陰 (おかげ / okage) — help, thanks to
**Pronunciation:** "O-KA-GE — OH CAR GATE! OH CAR GATE = THANKS!"
